On eight SAM and SAM3 candidate pools spanning camouflaged, salient, and dichotomous segmentation and camouflage under low light, SphereTrust exceeds the strongest evaluated external baseline on six pools by 1.7 to 9.3 percentage points in mean selected Dice. These comparisons include published selection rules and explicitly labeled adaptations of DSS and UCOD-MKD. On the two prompted camouflage pools, its mean selected Dice is within 0.1 percentage points of the candidate-derived DSS adaptation, with a lower catastrophic-error rate. Which cue carries the signal depends on the candidate pool. The same sphere also supports training. The leading candidates enter as a candidate set with their scores as priors, prototypes reorder them, and a cross-fitted second round completes the labels, raising weighted F by 4.5, 2.3, and 5.5 points over fixed-label training on the three MLLM anchor pools, with students competitive with published unsupervised methods on nineteen test sets.

</details>


### [102] [Accelerating Video Diffusion via Training-Free Trajectory Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30096)
*Mustafa Munir,Huy Vu,Shreyas Misra,Rohit Jena,Sajad Norouzi,Ali Taghibakhshi,Anis Ahmad,Anjul Patney,Pavlo Molchanov,Nima Tajbakhsh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACK通过校准阶段比较大小模型在同一潜变量、时间步、条件与引导输入下的预测分歧，得到跨扩散步的分歧分数图，并据此制定切换策略：质量敏感步保留大模型，低分歧步路由到小模型，从而在免训练、不改架构与调度器的前提下实现异构去噪加速。在Wan 2.1、Cosmos 3、TurboDiffusion、FastVideo上分别取得1.95倍、2.04-2.73倍、2.69倍、2.17倍加速，聚合质量相当且多样性保持良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型推理代价高昂：大模型需跨多个去噪步反复执行，即便采用步蒸馏，每个蒸馏步仍需一次昂贵的大模型评估。作者希望在不重训练、不改架构与调度器的前提下降低每步去噪的平均计算成本，同时保住生成质量。

Method: 提出TRACK（TRajectory-Aware Capacity routing via top-K selection），一种异构去噪策略：先用大模型rollout一条参考轨迹；在每个步同时采集小模型预测并与大模型预测对比，得到相对分歧分数（两模型接收相同的latent、timestep、conditioning、guidance输入）；在校准集上聚合该信号，形成跨扩散步的分歧分数图，据此确定切换策略。推理时每步只执行被选中的模型，无需重训练、无需架构或调度器改动，也无需在线双模型评估。

Result: 在Wan 2.1、Cosmos 3、TurboDiffusion、FastVideo上分别获得1.95×、2.04×–2.73×、2.69×、2.17×的加速，聚合质量可比，多样性保持较高。

Conclusion: TRACK确立了"自动化、免训练的模型切换"作为视频扩散一种实用的加速范式。

Abstract: Video diffusion is computationally expensive, as it requires executing a large model across many denoising steps. Even with step-distillation, inference remains expensive because every distilled step still requires a costly model evaluation. We present TRACK: TRajectory-Aware Capacity routing via top-K selection, a heterogeneous denoising strategy that switches between compatible large and small models at selected steps, reducing the average cost per denoising evaluation. The switching steps are determined using a calibration process. TRACK first rolls out a reference trajectory with the large model. Then at each step, the small model's prediction is also collected and compared against the large model's prediction to obtain a relative disagreement score. Both models receive the same latent, timestep, conditioning, and guidance inputs. Aggregating this signal over a calibration set produces a disagreement score map across diffusion steps, which determines a switching policy for an efficient inference process: quality-sensitive steps keep using the large model, while steps with low disagreement scores are routed to the small model. Inference executes only the selected model at each step, requiring no retraining, architecture or scheduler changes, or online dual-model evaluation. Across Wan 2.1, Cosmos 3, TurboDiffusion, and FastVideo, TRACK yields $1.95\times$, $2.04\times$-$2.73\times$, $2.69\times$, and $2.17\times$ speedups, respectively, with comparable aggregate quality and high diversity retention. TRACK thereby establishes automated, training-free model switching as a practical acceleration paradigm for video diffusion.

</details>


### [103] [Smartphone-Based Method for Automated Speed Enforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30107)
*Keya Li,Jahnavi Malagavalli,Lamha Goel,Tong Wang,Kara M. Kockelman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出并测试基于智能手机的自动测速与车辆识别流水线（车牌、品牌/型号、颜色），用于辅助交通执法，并在巴西UFPR-ALPR数据集及奥斯汀实拍视频上评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共机构在交通执法中缺乏低成本、易部署的技术手段，智能手机与CV具备潜力，但实际可用性、精度与法律/实践可行性尚不明确，需验证其能否可靠识别超速车辆。

Method: 构建智能手机端CV流水线：车牌文本识别、车辆品牌/厂商与型号识别、颜色分类及速度估计；在UFPR-ALPR巴西开源数据集（1800张图像）与奥斯汀手持手机视频（n=73）及路侧摄像头（n=42）白天录像上测试，并讨论执法相关的法律、技术与实践问题。

Result: UFPR-ALPR上车牌文本识别准确率约46%；实拍数据颜色检测60.8%、厂商识别48.6%、品牌与型号识别16.89%；20%误差内速度估计准确率16.3%，品牌Top-3为16.9%，车牌Top-10为29.7%。总体精度偏低，难以直接用于执法。

Conclusion: 智能手机CV在交通执法场景中仍处于早期阶段，精度不足以支撑可靠执法；需在技术、法律与实操层面进一步改进，才能转化为实用工具。

Abstract: Smartphone cameras and computer vision (CV) hold significant promise in assisting public agencies with enforcing traffic laws and enhancing road safety. This work designs and tests a smartphone-based method for automated speed estimation and vehicle identification (license plate, make/model, and color recognition) via an automated pipeline to assist enforcement agencies in reliably identifying speeders. The CV code accurately recognizes nearly half (46%) of the license plates' text on 1,800 images from a Brazil open-source dataset, called UFPR-ALPR. Code tests on daytime recordings from hand-held smartphone videos (n = 73) and roadside cameras (n = 42) in Austin, Texas yield 60.8% accuracy for color detection (among all possible RGB color categories), 48.6% on vehicle make/manufacturer identification, and 16.89% on vehicle make and model identification. Prediction accuracy for speed estimation (within a 20% range), vehicle make (within the top 3 predictions), and license plate recognition (within the top 10 predictions) are 16.3%, 16.9%, and 29.7%, respectively. This paper also illuminates the legal, technological, and practical aspects of using smartphones for enforcement, including the potential use of recordings for enforcement purposes, emphasizing the need to transform the potential of smartphone-based CV technologies into practical tools for vital information on traffic violations.

</details>


### [104] [Multimodal Thinking with Renderable Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30130)
*Sunli Chen,Ding Zhong,Ziqiao Ma,Jiaxin Liu,Zeyuan Yang,Hao Zhang,Lie Lu,Joyce Chai,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SVGLM框架，利用可缩放矢量图形(SVG)图元作为文本与图像之间的可解释媒介，让通用VLM在推理链中生成图像，并给出SVG图像编辑数据集与开源VLM微调范式，在数学推理基准上验证了SVG生成与"用图思考"能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM擅长视觉理解与文本推理，但其结构阻碍了将图像纳入推理链；Omnimodal模型虽统一了文本与图像生成，却聚焦开放域视觉任务，且图像以栅格或潜表示存在，缺乏可追踪性与可解释性。

Method: 以SVG图元作为统一表征，利用SVG兼具"图像描述"与"文本指令"的双重性，构建紧凑、可解释的图像生成接口；构建大规模人工整理的SVG图像编辑数据集，并提出面向开源VLM的微调范式，使模型在推理过程中生成图像。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，SVGLM展现出较强的SVG生成能力与"think-with-image"推理智能，表明文本思考与像素图像之间的鸿沟可被SVG弥合。

Conclusion: SVG是一种适合构建更鲁棒数字域智能体的媒介，可作为多模态推理中图像-文本统一的可解释中间表示，为通用VLM赋予推理内生图能力。

Abstract: Current vision-language models (VLMs) excel at visual content understanding and text-based reasoning, yet their structure limits the advancement of incorporating images into the reasoning chain. Though Omnimodal models have made efforts in unifying text and image generation, they focus on visual tasks in the open-domain, lacking tractability due to rasterized or latent representations of images. We introduce SVGLM, a framework that uses scalable vector graphics (SVG) primitives to connect text and image in reasoning tasks. We exploit the duality of SVG as both image description and text instructions, yielding a more compact, interpretable solution to equip general VLMs with the capability of generating images within the reasoning process. We provide a large curated dataset of SVG-based image editing dataset, as well as the paradigm to tune open-source VLMs. Experiments on a mathematical reasoning benchmark demonstrate that SVGLM achieves strong SVG generation power as well as think-with-image intelligence. Our results highlight SVG as a suitable medium for building more robust digital domain agents, bridging the gap between text-based thinking and pixel-based images.

</details>


### [105] [Ego-Exo4D Human Meshes Dataset: 4D Human Motion Reconstruction for Ego-Exo Captures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30187)
*Abhiram Maddukuri,Georgios Pavlakos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Ego-Exo4D-HM：为 Ego-Exo4D 构建的大规模 4D 人体运动（SMPL 网格）重建数据集，并开源配套重建流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ego-Exo4D 提供同步的第一人称与多视角第三人称视频，是技能学习、程序性活动理解与具身 AI 的重要资源；但其原始数据仅含稀疏 3D 人体姿态标注，从多视角视频恢复稠密人体运动困难，限制了数据可用性。

Method: 基于 Ego-Exo4D 的多视角捕获构建重建流水线，输出时序稠密的 4D 人体运动（逐帧、时序一致的全身网格/参数化人体表示），并规模化处理整个数据集，形成 Ego-Exo4D-HM 数据集，同时开源代码与文档。摘要未披露具体优化细节。

Result: 产出覆盖 Ego-Exo4D 全部捕获的大规模稠密 4D 人体运动重建数据集，并公开重建流水线、数据集与文档。摘要中未给出量化精度或效率指标。

Conclusion: 将稀疏人体标注的 ego-exo 视频资源升级为稠密的 4D 人体运动数据，为技能评估、交互理解与具身 AI 研究提供基础设施，代码与数据开放。

Abstract: Ego-Exo4D is a large-scale dataset providing synchronized egocentric and multi-view exocentric video, a rich resource for skill learning and assessment, procedural activity understanding, and embodied AI. However, the dataset ships with only sparse 3D human pose annotations, and reconstructing dense human motion from its multi-view captures is nontrivial. To this end, we present Ego-Exo4D-HM, a large-scale dataset of 4D human motion reconstructions for Ego-Exo4D's captures, and release the accompanying reconstruction pipeline. The code, dataset, and documentation can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [106] [The Alignment Illusion in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30210)
*Hong-Han Wang,Yuntao Wang,Hu Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文质疑MLLM中层间视觉-文本相似度分数反映跨模态内容对齐的传统解读，通过向视觉流注入高斯噪声等受控干预，在13个MLLM（5个家族，0.5B–72B）上发现CKA、SVCCA、MIR、主角度余弦等标量指标无法稳定区分损坏流与原始流，称之为"对齐幻觉"，并将其归因于各向异性MLP下投影造成的权重诱导对齐；为此提出主角度间隙（PA gap，前两个主角度余弦之差），在分级视觉损坏下比标量指标更一致地跟踪任务准确率，并揭示内部几何与任务表现分离的情形。结论：MLLM内部视觉-文本对齐应视为语言模型内视觉流的几何诊断，而非内容级跨模态交互的直接代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域普遍将MLLM逐层视觉-文本相似度（标量对齐分数）解读为语言模型逐步将视觉内容整合进共享表征空间的证据。但这一解读隐含假设：标量分数反映内容级跨模态交互。作者要检验该假设是否成立——即这些分数是否真能区分"有内容的视觉流"与"无内容的破坏视觉流"。

Method: 对视觉流施加受控干预：将projector输出的视觉token替换为高斯噪声，并采用分级视觉损坏与结构化但无关图像。在13个MLLM（5个家族，0.5B–72B参数）上评测任务准确率与四种标准标量对齐指标（CKA、SVCCA、MIR、主角度余弦）。通过分析语言模型共享通路（各向异性MLP下投影将视觉与文本token拉向共同输出方向）解释失败原因，提出主角度间隙（PA gap = 前两个主角度余弦之差）以分离权重诱导相似性与多方向视觉结构。

Result: 用高斯噪声替换projector输出视觉token后任务准确率显著下降，但四种标量指标均无法一致地区分损坏流与原始流，作者称之为"对齐幻觉"；其根源是共享语言模型通路中MLP下投影的各向异性导致的权重诱导对齐（本质近一维）。PA gap在分级视觉损坏下比所考察的标量分数更一致地跟踪任务准确率；在结构化但无关图像下进一步暴露内部几何与任务准确率分离的情形。

Conclusion: MLLM内部视觉-文本对齐最好被理解为语言模型内视觉流的几何诊断工具，而非内容级跨模态交互的直接代理；只有结合受控任务证据进行校准，该诊断才最具信息量。

Abstract: Layer-wise visual-text similarity in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) is widely interpreted as evidence that the language model progressively integrates visual content into a shared representation space. This reading rests on the assumption that scalar alignment scores reflect content-level cross-modal interaction. To test this assumption, we apply controlled interventions to the visual stream. Across 13 MLLMs from five families spanning 0.5B to 72B parameters, replacing projector-output visual tokens with Gaussian noise sharply reduces task accuracy, yet four standard scalar measures (CKA, SVCCA, MIR, and the leading principal-angle cosine) fail to consistently separate the corrupted stream from the original. We call this failure the alignment illusion and trace it to the shared language-model pathway: anisotropic MLP down-projections pull visual and text tokens toward common output directions, producing weight-induced alignment. Because this component is essentially one-dimensional, we introduce the principal-angle gap (PA gap), defined as the difference between the top two principal-angle cosines, which separates weight-induced similarity from multi-directional visual structure. Under graded visual corruption, the PA gap tracks task accuracy more consistently than the scalar scores we consider; under a structured but irrelevant image, it further exposes regimes in which internal geometry and task accuracy come apart. Internal visual-text alignment in MLLMs is therefore best read as a geometric diagnostic of the visual stream inside the language model rather than a direct proxy for content-level cross-modal interaction, and is most informative when calibrated by controlled task evidence.

</details>


### [107] [WanPE: Towards Cinematic Prompt Enhancement for Modern Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30221)
*Yubo Zhu,Yawen Shao,Ziyun Dai,Zixun Fang,Kai Zhu,Siyang Sun,Haolan Xue,Chuxin Wang,Tingyu Weng,Jingming Luo,Chen Shi,Lianghua Huang,Yufeng Ai,Yuzheng Wang,Wenyuan Zhang,Yu Shang,Yuxiang Bao,Zoubin Bi,Jie Xiao,Jinbo Xing,Jiaxing Zhao,Chongyang Zhong,Hengjian Chen,Chenwei Xie,Akide Liu,Zhehan Kan,Yu Liu,Wei Zhai,Sheng Zhong,Wei Tong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出397B参数的电影级提示增强模型WanPE，用视频反向构建shot级分镜计划并以SC-GRPO保持语义一致，配WanPEval基准；接入Wan3.0后人类偏好显著提升，30秒档提升50.86分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成已扩展到30秒并需遵循复杂条件，文本提示实际承担了导演级规划职责（动作、运镜、光照、声音在多镜头序列中的展开）。但原始用户提示缺乏专业分镜规划能力，导致长时长、多镜头条件下语义漂移与偏好下降，因此需要专门的长视频细化提示增强能力。

Method: WanPE为397B参数提示增强模型，基于105万真实视频训练，通过视频驱动的反向构建（reverse construction）从成片反推shot级电影化计划，而非前向改写；并采用语义一致性GRPO（SC-GRPO）在跨镜头、跨时间上保持用户原始要求不变。同时构建WanPEval人工标注测试床，覆盖5–30秒与不同意图粒度，约11K次盲评配对评估。

Result: 驱动Wan3.0生成器时，WanPE-397B相对原始用户提示在5–15秒区间人类偏好提升10.66–18.84分，30秒档提升50.86分。消融显示反向构建显著优于前向改写，SC-GRPO在不同模型规模下稳健保持语义忠实度。在5–15秒区间领先所有评测的商业方案，30秒档与Seedance 2.5具竞争力。

Conclusion: 提示增强可被建模为导演级分镜规划问题，从视频反向构建计划比前向改写更有效，配以语义一致性强化学习可在长时长多镜头下稳定保持用户意图；该方法为长视频生成提供了可扩展的提示规划范式。

Abstract: Video generation begins in text space by authoring a cinematic screenplay, then materializes into pixels. As contemporary video generators scale to 30 seconds and faithfully follow complex conditions, the textual prompt largely directs the production, planning how actions, camera trajectories, lighting, and sound unfold across multi-shot sequences. In this paper, we present WanPE, a 397B-parameter prompt enhancement model trained on 1.05M real-world videos to master director-level cinematic planning. WanPE formulates shot-level cinematic plans via video-grounded reverse construction and employs Semantic-Consistency GRPO (SC-GRPO) to faithfully preserve user requirements across shots and over time. To benchmark this capability, we curate WanPEval, a human-annotated testbed covering durations from 5 to 30 seconds across varying intent granularities, supported by approximately 11K blind pairwise assessments. When powering Wan3.0's video generator, WanPE-397B boosts human preference over raw user prompts by 10.66-18.84 points at 5-15 seconds and by a dramatic 50.86 points in the 30-second arena. Ablation studies show that reverse construction demonstrates clear superiority over forward rewriting, while SC-GRPO robustly preserves semantic fidelity across model scales. Ultimately, WanPE leads all evaluated commercial offerings at 5-15 seconds and remains competitive with Seedance 2.5 at 30 seconds.

</details>


### [108] [TrackEverything: Long Horizon Dense Tracking via De-Duplicating 3D Scene Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30222)
*Ayush Jain,Sreeharsha Paruchuri,Ishita Gupta,Fan Zhang,Tanner Schmidt,Jakob Engel,Katerina Fragkiadaki,Adam W. Harley*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrackEverything 将视频表示为世界坐标系下的持久3D场景轨迹，通过体素去重、端点精修+轻量轨迹精修、以及3D WAFT特征采样，实现在40GB显存内对超1000帧视频的所有可见点进行密集3D跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有点跟踪模型存在根本性权衡：要么仅跟踪稀疏查询点但可覆盖长时序，要么跟踪所有点但仅限短视频片段。作者希望打破该权衡，让跟踪的复杂度与视频时长解耦，转而随物理场景几何的规模增长。

Method: 核心思想是将视频视为底层3D世界的2D投影，从而以持久3D场景轨迹建模。三项创新：(1) 基于体素的去重机制，在滑动窗口边界合并位置重合的轨迹，避免同一表面被重复观测而冗余累积；(2) 将跟踪分解为端点精修器（预测每点终点与静/动分类）与轻量轨迹精修器（仅对动态点解码密集轨迹）；(3) 提出3D WAFT，用场景点云中的高效特征采样替代内存开销巨大的4D相关体。

Result: 在TAPVid-3D上，短片段中相较所有开源全帧密集3D跟踪器APD提升超过20%；在长序列上与最先进稀疏跟踪器保持竞争力，且跟踪点数远多于后者。它是首个能在40GB GPU显存内跟踪超过1000帧视频中所有可见点的3D跟踪器。

Conclusion: 通过世界坐标系下的持久3D场景轨迹表示与静/动分解，TrackEverything突破了稀疏长时序与密集短片段之间的权衡，为长视频全点密集3D跟踪提供了可行且高效的新范式，对世界模型与具身感知中的场景动态建模具有参考价值。

Abstract: Existing point tracking models face a fundamental tradeoff: they can either track a sparse set of query points over long horizons, or track all points across only short clips. We introduce TrackEverything, a 3D point tracker that breaks this trade-off by representing videos as persistent 3D scene tracks in world coordinates. Grounded in the insight that videos are 2D projections of an underlying 3D world, TrackEverything decouples model complexity from video duration, allowing it to scale with unique physical scene geometry instead. Our approach introduces three key innovations. First, we employ a voxelization-based de-duplication mechanism at sliding-window boundaries to merge co-located tracks, preventing repeated observations of the same surface from redundantly accumulating. Second, we decompose tracking into an endpoint refiner that predicts each point's destination and static-versus-dynamic classification, followed by a lightweight trajectory refiner that decodes dense trajectories exclusively for dynamic points. Third, we propose 3D WAFT, replacing memory-prohibitive 4D correlation volumes with efficient feature sampling in the scene cloud. To the best of our knowledge, TrackEverything is the first 3D tracker capable of tracking all visible points across videos exceeding 1000 frames within 40 GB of GPU memory. On TAPVid-3D, TrackEverything outperforms all open-source all-frame dense 3D trackers by more than 20% APD on short clips, while remaining competitive with state-of-the-art sparse trackers on long sequences, despite tracking far more points.

</details>


### [109] [BiCC: Bidirectional Connected-Component Loss for Instance-Aware Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30223)
*Luc Bouteille,Frederic Jonske,Jens Kleesiek,Alexander Jaus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双向连通域损失BiCC，同时使用标注与预测得到的实例划分，对假阳性连通域给予独立惩罚，从而在病灶级F1上优于CC-DiceCE与blob loss。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体素级分割损失（如DiceCE）按体积加权，小病灶权重过低；blob loss与CC-DiceCE虽引入实例级项，但实例仅来自标注，假阳性成分得不到实例级惩罚。在计算机辅助阅片中，每个假阳性成分都需单独复核，因此除召回外，精度与假阳性负担同样重要。

Method: BiCC（bidirectional connected-component loss）构建两个方向的连通域划分：一路来自标注，一路来自预测。预测导出的分支按预测成分自身的尺度打分，从而不依赖体积地对假阳性连通域直接施加惩罚；并引入平衡参数α控制病灶级精度-召回权衡。

Result: 在5个数据集上以nnU-Net进行五折交叉验证：BiCC在4个数据集上病灶级F1优于CC-DiceCE，在全部5个数据集上优于blob loss；在3个数据集上显著优于DiceCE，另2个持平。相比之下，CC-DiceCE偏向召回，精度最多下降0.363。

Conclusion: 通过双向（标注+预测）连通域实例化，弥补了仅由标注定义实例的损失对假阳性缺乏约束的缺陷，使病灶级精度与召回可调可控，适用于对假阳性负担敏感的辅助阅片场景。

Abstract: Common segmentation losses aggregate errors voxel-wise, so lesions influence the objective in proportion to their volume, giving small but clinically critical lesions disproportionately little weight. Instance-aware losses aim to address this mismatch by assigning each lesion its own term. However, blob loss and CC-DiceCE derive their regions solely from annotations, so false-positive components receive no instance-level term. This matters in computer-assisted review, where each false-positive component may require separate inspection, making precision and false-positive burden important alongside recall. We introduce the bidirectional connected-component loss (BiCC), which pairs annotation- and prediction-derived partitions to score predicted components on their own scale. By deriving instances from the predictions, this branch directly penalizes false-positive components regardless of their size. The balance parameter $\alpha$ allows control over the lesion-wise precision-recall trade-off. Across five datasets with five-fold cross-validation using nnU-Net, BiCC outperforms CC-DiceCE in lesion-wise F1 on four datasets and blob loss on all five. It significantly improves over DiceCE on three datasets and matches it on two; CC-DiceCE instead loses up to 0.363 precision by favoring recall. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [110] [OmniFabric: Coherent UV Space Texture Synthesis for 3D Garment Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30234)
*Ding-Jiun Huang,Yuanhao Wang,Cheng Zhang,Hugo Bertiche,Alexandru-Eugen Ichim,Thabo Beeler,Fernando De la Torre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniFabric 提出在2D缝纫纸样（sewing pattern）空间中合成全局一致的纹理贴图，先由3D网格与VLM先验建立粗初始化，再用经合成数据训练的扩散Transformer在规范UV域精修，去除烘烤光照与畸变，生成可用于仿真与重光照的照片级3D服装资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图生成可生产的3D服装资产是数字内容创作的核心难题。现有生成模型虽在几何重建上进步显著，但纹理合成仍是瓶颈：方法常把环境光照与阴影烘烤进贴图，或缺乏全局结构一致性，导致资产无法用于物理仿真与重光照。

Method: 以单张参考图为输入，估计3D网格，利用VLM生成先验在展开的缝纫纸样上建立完整但粗糙的纹理初始化；随后使用经自动化合成数据引擎训练、以3D位置特征为条件的专用扩散Transformer，在规范UV域直接精修该初始化，从而去除畸变与烘烤伪影，得到保持原始服装设计、干净且归一化的纹理贴图。

Result: 大量实验表明，OmniFabric 显著优于当前最先进基线，能生成具有高质量纹理的照片级真实3D服装。

Conclusion: 将纹理合成置于缝纫纸样/UV规范域并结合VLM先验与扩散Transformer精修，是获得无光照烘烤、几何一致且可仿真、可重光照的3D服装纹理的有效范式。

Abstract: Automated generation of production-ready 3D garment assets from a single image is a central challenge in digital content creation. While recent generative models have significantly advanced 3D geometry reconstruction, synthesizing high-quality textures remains a bottleneck. Existing methods often bake environmental illumination and shadows directly into the texture map, or they fail to maintain global structural coherence, making the resulting assets unusable for physical simulation and relighting. In this work, we introduce OmniFabric, a novel approach that synthesizes globally coherent texture maps directly within the 2D sewing pattern space. Given a single reference image, our pipeline utilizes an estimated 3D mesh and generative priors of powerful Vision-Language Models (VLM) to establish a complete but coarse texture initialization across the unwrapped sewing patterns. We then leverage a specialized diffusion transformer, trained via an automated synthetic data engine and conditioned on 3D positional features, to refine this initialization directly in the canonical UV domain. This effectively removes distortion and baked-in artifacts to extract a clean and normalized texture map that preserves the original garment design. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OmniFabric significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, yielding photorealistic 3D garments with high-quality textures.

</details>


### [111] [Towards Practical Compression of 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30245)
*Pengpeng Yu,Yueru Chen,Fei Song,Tai Qin,Qi Zhang,Jing Wang,Yulan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: COSA-GS利用锚点因果分解构建无空间聚合的上下文模型，实现3DGS的率失真优化压缩与跨平台位精确熵解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS存储开销大；现有压缩方法依赖不规则3D表示上的空间上下文建模，训练与编码复杂度高；浮点上下文推断在不同平台存在数值不一致，易导致熵解码失败。

Method: 以每个锚点坐标导出的几何上下文构建紧凑可学习锚点隐变量，再与几何上下文融合形成锚点上下文用于属性编码，即锚点级因果分解，避免空间聚合；上下文模型仅由线性变换与激活构成；采用率失真优化加自适应高斯剪枝训练，并引入量化感知训练与整数推断，实现跨平台位精确符号一致性。

Result: 实验表明达到SOTA压缩性能，同时保持快速且跨平台一致的解码。

Conclusion: COSA-GS提供了一个简单有效的3DGS压缩框架，兼顾压缩率、解码速度与跨平台一致性，具备实用价值。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-quality novel-view synthesis but requires substantial storage. Existing compression methods often rely on spatial context modeling over irregular 3D representations, increasing the complexity of training and coding. Meanwhile, floating-point context inference can introduce numerical inconsistencies across platforms, causing entropy-decoding failures. To address these practical challenges, we propose COSA-GS, which constructs context without spatial aggregation through anchor-wise causal factorization. Specifically, we use geometry context derived from each anchor's coordinates to model a compact learnable anchor latent. The anchor latent is then fused with the geometry context to form an anchor context for attribute coding. The resulting context model features a simple architecture composed solely of linear transformations and activations. We train COSA-GS using rate--distortion optimization with adaptive Gaussian pruning. Further, we develop quantization-aware training and integer inference for the context model to achieve bit-exact consistency of entropy-decoded symbols across platforms. Experiments demonstrate that COSA-GS achieves state-of-the-art compression performance while retaining fast and consistent cross-platform decoding, providing a simple yet effective framework for practical 3DGS compression. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Know Your Body: A Harness for Direct and Self-Improving Robot Control with VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28530)
*Zeyu Lou,Yanhong Zeng,Yong Wang,Chenyang Si*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KnowBody 通过冻结模型权重、构建可查询可修订的"身体关系"部分模型，让通用视觉语言模型显式理解机器人运动学与功能部件，从而提升真实机器人任务完成率并减少规划轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉语言模型虽能理解任务目标，但不知道特定机器人的运动方式和功能部件如何产生预期效果，即缺乏对"动作相关身体关系"的显式认识。作者希望在不微调模型的前提下弥补这一缺口。

Method: 提出 KnowBody 框架：从一条非任务轨迹初始化部分身体模型，用于指导动作选择与对过往交互的解释；新证据持续修订该模型，并在复用前重新校验依赖已修订身体估计的知识；全程保持模型权重冻结。

Result: 在四个真实机器人任务、32 次固定预算试验中，初始化 KnowBody 完成率 75%，原生框架为 25%；在两者都完成的任务上，成功试验所需规划轮次更少。开启持续更新后，从第一次到第五次成功记录，规划轮次减少 29%–53%。

Conclusion: 显式、可查询、可修订的身体关系建模能在不训练模型的情况下显著提升通用视觉语言模型在真实机器人上的任务表现与规划效率。

Abstract: A general-purpose vision-language model can understand a task goal without knowing how a particular robot's motion and functional parts produce the intended effect. We introduce KnowBody, a harness that makes these action-relevant body relations explicit, queryable, and revisable while keeping the model weights frozen. Initialized from one off-task trajectory, a partial body model guides action selection and the interpretation of past interactions. New evidence refines the model, and knowledge dependent on revised body estimates is rechecked before reuse. Across 32 fixed-budget trials on four real-robot tasks, initialized KnowBody achieves 75% completion versus 25% for the native harness and requires fewer planner rounds on successful trials in tasks completed by both. With persistent updates enabled, planner rounds decrease by 29-53% from the first to the fifth recorded success.

</details>


### [113] [Morphometric Imitation: From Morphology and Contact Aware Hand Retargeting to Sim-to-Real Visuomotor Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28660)
*Tara Sadjadpour,Siming He,C.K. Wolfe,Haozhi Qi,Lea Wilken,S. Shankar Sastry,Claire Tomlin,Jitendra Malik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Morphometric Imitation三阶段框架，将重建的人-物交互(HOI)转化为可零样本sim-to-real部署的视觉运动策略：形态优化(MMO)运动学重定向、残差强化学习精修生成动力学可行示范、示范蒸馏为视觉运动策略，在3只机器人手、10类HOI上验证，真实世界300次试验零样本成功率89.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人手-物体交互示范是灵巧操作的丰富数据来源，但直接学习面临三大障碍：人手与机器人手之间的形态差异(morphology gap)、重定向运动的动力学不可行性、以及仿真到真实的迁移。已有工作难以同时解决这三个问题。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) Morphometric Optimization (MMO) 在保持演示接触的前提下，将人手运动跨形态做运动学重定向；(2) 残差强化学习以物体位姿与接触信息为条件精修运动学参考，生成动力学可行的机器人示范，并做了残差RL的消融分析验证物体位姿与接触信息的互补作用；(3) 将示范蒸馏为视觉运动策略，实现零样本sim-to-real部署。

Result: 在3只机器人手、10类HOI上，MMO相较5个基线中最强者，每只手的接触F1至少提升8个点，并使下游动力学重定向成功率最高提升35个点；视觉运动策略在30个物体、300次真实世界试验中达到89.3%零样本成功率。

Conclusion: 形态优化重定向 + 残差RL精修 + 策略蒸馏的组合可有效跨越形态与动力学鸿沟，实现手-物交互示范到真实机器人灵巧操作的零样本迁移，验证了以接触保持为核心的HOI重定向价值。

Abstract: Human hand-object interactions (HOIs) provide a rich source of demonstrations for dexterous manipulation, but learning directly from them presents challenges in bridging morphology gaps, ensuring dynamical feasibility, and sim-to-real deployment. We present Morphometric Imitation, a three-stage framework that transforms reconstructed HOIs into zero-shot sim-to-real visuomotor policies. First, morphometric optimization (MMO) kinematically retargets human motion across hand morphologies while preserving demonstrated contacts. Second, residual reinforcement learning (RL) refines the kinematic reference using object pose and contact information from the human motion to produce dynamically feasible robot demonstrations. Third, these demonstrations are distilled into visuomotor policies. Across three robot hands and ten HOIs, MMO improves contact F1 over the strongest of five baselines by at least 8 points for every hand, while also improving the success rate of downstream dynamic retargeting by as much as 35 points. Ablations on the residual RL show complementary benefits from using object pose and contact information. Finally, the visuomotor policies achieve 89.3% zero-shot success in 300 real-world trials on 30 objects. Project page: $\href{ this https URL }{\text{this https URL}}$

</details>


### [114] [OA-MPPI: Occlusion-Aware Model Predictive Path Integral Control for UAV Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28709)
*Vittorio Palladino,Teaya Yang,Ruiqi Zhang,Mark W. Mueller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OA-MPPI：在MPPI控制中引入遮挡边界约束，使四旋翼在未知/遮挡区域附近主动保持安全裕度，以规避可能突然出现的移动体，并在仿真与实机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主无人机在杂乱且部分未知环境中飞行时，仅考虑已观测障碍不足，遮挡区域后可能隐藏移动体并突然进入飞行路径，需要显式对遮挡区域进行推理。

Method: 在MPPI框架上扩展：每个规划步从在线占据地图中提取3D遮挡边界；据此建模预测时域内隐藏体可达的膨胀区域；在非线性四旋翼动力学与各旋子推力限制下的rollout中，对进入该区域的轨迹施加惩罚。

Result: 仿真与实机飞行实验均表明，相比基线MPPI，所提方法对遮挡边界的保持距离更大；仿真中还成功规避了自遮挡区出现的移动体，完整流程可在机载实时运行。

Conclusion: 将遮挡感知作为MPPI代价项的一体化建模，可在不依赖显式意图预测的情况下提升遮挡场景下的飞行安全性，具备机载实时性。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV flight through cluttered and partially unknown environments requires reasoning not only about observed obstacles but also about occluded regions that the sensor cannot observe. We present OA-MPPI, an obstacle- and occlusion-aware extension of Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control for quadrotor flight that accounts for potential moving agents emerging from these regions into the vehicle's path. At every planning step, we extract a 3D occlusion boundary from the online occupancy map and use it to model the regions that hidden agents could reach over the prediction horizon. We penalize trajectories that enter these expanding regions within MPPI rollouts generated using nonlinear quadrotor dynamics and accounting for individual rotor thrust limits. We validate the proposed approach in simulation and hardware flight experiments, with the complete pipeline running onboard the vehicle in real time. Results show increased clearance from occlusion boundaries compared to baseline MPPI in both settings, as well as avoidance of an agent emerging from occlusion in simulation.

</details>


### [115] [Temporal Learning for End-Effector Position Estimation under Aerodynamic Disturbances in Aerial Continuum Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28716)
*Niloufar Amiri,Houman Masnavi,Farrokh Janabi-Sharifi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用连续时间(CfC)神经网络对受气动扰动的空中连续体机械臂末端位置进行残差估计，RMSE较MLP和GRU分别降低39.52%与20.62%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中连续体机械臂(ACM)在无人机气动扰动下末端位置难以精确估计；传统应变参数化运动学模型无法建模气动残余，而静态或离散时间学习器对该动态残差的拟合能力有限。

Method: 采集静止(rotor-off)与自由悬停两种条件下的实验数据集，覆盖不同连续体构型与无人机高度；先以递进丰富应变基的应变参数化运动学模型建立标称基线，再以闭式连续时间(CfC)网络进行3D位置残差估计，并与MLP、GRU对比（5个随机种子）。

Result: 未见测试集上CfC RMSE为22.00±1.70 mm，MLP为36.38±3.58 mm，GRU为27.72±2.92 mm，相对二者分别降低39.52%与20.62%。

Conclusion: 相对静态学习与离散时间学习方法，连续时间学习在空中连续体机械臂受气动扰动下的末端位置估计中更有效，标称运动学+残差学习的框架具备实用价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates temporal neural networks for \mbox{end-effector} position \mbox{estimation} of an aerial continuum manipulator (ACM) operating under aerodynamic effects induced by the unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). An experimental dataset is collected under stationary (\mbox{rotor-off}) and \mbox{free-hovering} conditions across continuum robot (CR) configurations and UAV altitudes, providing \mbox{end-effector} position measurements with and without aerodynamic residuals. To establish a nominal framework, \mbox{strain-parameterized} kinematic models with progressively richer strain bases are evaluated to balance model complexity and prediction accuracy. The selected nominal model then serves as the baseline for 3D position residual estimation using a \mbox{closed-form} \mbox{continuous-time} (CfC) neural network, with a multilayer perceptron (MLP) and a gated recurrent unit (GRU) used for comparison. On unseen test experiments, the CfC achieves an RMSE of \(22.00\pm1.70~\mathrm{mm}\) over five random seeds, compared with \(36.38\pm3.58~\mathrm{mm}\) for the MLP and \(27.72\pm2.92~\mathrm{mm}\) for the GRU, corresponding to reductions of \(39.52\%\) and \(20.62\%\), respectively. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of \mbox{continuous-time} learning for \mbox{end-effector} position estimation under aerodynamic disturbances relative to static and \mbox{discrete-time} learning methods.

</details>


### [116] [TAPESIM: Efficient Simulation of Adhesive Tape Dispensing for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28766)
*Zhaofeng Luo,Xinyu Lu,jaehoon Choi,Zhehuan Chen,Trinity Chung,Xiaowen Qiu,Hugh Nicholas Perkins,Gianna Calderon,Alexis Duburcq,Sanghyun Son,Tsun-Hsuan Wang,Yi-Ling Qiao,Minchen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TapeSim：通过刚体簇+可变形套环将变形集中于放卷区，加速胶带仿真并提升贴附/剥离复现精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人贴胶带/封箱需协调柔性胶带、运动卷筒与可反复贴附-脱离的表面，仿真可提升可复现性；但逐层求解粘附代价高、在实用求解容差下会抑制卷筒转动，而永久刚体卷筒又无法释放材料。

Method: 将变形集中在放卷区域与已释放胶带段：用刚体簇表示大部分缠绕材料，配合前进式可变形套环(collar)实现放卷并保持释放段柔性可再贴附；可选可释放键(releasable bonds)简化粘附界面。

Result: 固定牛顿容差下32圈时，聚类带来3.2-3.4x平均物理步加速，相当卷筒运动下4.5-8.4x；五个真实运动Stick回放中图像平面核心地标误差较全壳内聚基线降低23-29%；100对Peel案例平衡准确率由50%提升至72.9-76.3%；遥操作封箱序列演示贴附、放卷、切割、封合连续流程。

Conclusion: TapeSim为胶带类接触-放卷-剥离仿真提供了高效且更保真的建模方案，并计划开源代码。

Abstract: Applying adhesive tape to secure wire harnesses or seal packages requires robots to coordinate a flexible strip, a moving roll, and surfaces that attach and detach. Simulation could make these interactions repeatable for robot development and evaluation, but resolving every adhesive layer is expensive and can suppress roll motion at practical solver tolerances, while a permanently rigid roll cannot release material. We present TapeSim, a tape simulator that concentrates deformation near the unwinding region and along the released strip. We will release the source code. A rigid cluster represents most wound material, while an advancing deformable collar enables payout and leaves released tape flexible and reattachable. Optional releasable bonds simplify adhesive interfaces and reduce mean step times for smaller rolls. Controlled swing tests show improved roll rotation. At 32 turns, clustering gives 3.2-3.4x mean physics-step speedups at a fixed Newton tolerance and 4.5-8.4x for comparable roll motion. Across five real-motion Stick replays, the clustered variants reduce mean image-plane core-landmark error by 23-29% relative to the full-shell cohesive baseline. On 100 paired Peel cases, they improve balanced accuracy from 50% to 72.9-76.3%, with interface rankings varying across tasks. A teleoperated box-sealing sequence demonstrates attachment, dispensing, cutting, and sealing in a continuous workflow.

</details>


### [117] [Human-in-the-Loop Geospatial Annotation for Rapid Dataset Construction in Field-Deployed UAV Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28767)
*Morgan Masters,Nikolaas Bender,T. Luca Altaffer,Colleen Josephson,Steve McGuire*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BirdsEye 将专家标注从图像迁移到现场：操作员用 RTK 记录目标的世界坐标，再通过标定的投影几何把观测传播到所有可见帧；论文给出相机位姿不确定性到像素不确定性的线性映射，并据此推导传感器设计工具，实测投影精度达亚分米级，在三个农业现场由两名标注员 12 小时产出 12,524 帧、55,600 个标注，效率提升 25.5 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实感知系统需要适应不断变化的环境，但人工图像标注无法扩展到野外数据规模；需要一种可规模化、且能保证物理标注与图像观测对齐的现场标注范式。

Method: 以 RTK 定位在世界坐标系中记录目标位置，用标定投影几何把单次观测传播到所有可见帧；推导相机位姿方差到像素方差的一阶线性映射（经蒙特卡洛验证），并将其反解为传感器设计工具：标注容差到凸的位姿噪声预算集、部署传感器组的最大容许缩放闭式解、等预算分摊下的唯一逐轴位姿规格；同时分析平面近似误差随地形坡度的变化。

Result: 平面近似在坡度 10 度以内成立；除持续偏航外，AGL 10-20m 下投影精度为亚分米（亚 30 像素）。三个农业站点案例研究中，两名现场工作者约 12 小时产出 12,524 帧、55,600 个标签，单人效率提升 25.5 倍。用该流程采集数据训练的检测器在地理上不同的农场恢复 56-89% 的视野内勘测目标（预注册工作点），对领先配置的人工复核估计检测精度 83-87%（覆盖三种簇判定约定）。

Conclusion: 现场物理标注加几何传播是可行的可扩展标注范式，配套的位姿-像素不确定性映射既是精度校验工具也是传感器选型/部署设计工具，并在农业检测任务中显示出跨站点泛化能力。

Abstract: Real-world perception systems must adapt to changing environments, but manual image annotation cannot scale to field data volumes. We present BirdsEye, which shifts expert annotation from images to the field: an operator records target locations in world coordinates using RTK positioning and calibrated projective geometry propagates each observation to all frames where the target is visible. To quantify how well physical annotations align with image observations, we derive a first-order mapping from camera-pose uncertainty to pixel uncertainty and validate it against Monte Carlo simulation. This mapping is linear in the six per-axis pose variances, so it inverts into a sensor design tool: we give a sufficient condition converting an annotation tolerance into a convex set of admissible pose-noise budgets, a closed-form largest admissible scaling of a deployed sensor suite, and a unique per-axis pose specification under an equal-budget-share allocation. We also analyze the planar-surface approximation underlying the projection, which holds up to 10 degrees of terrain slope. By direct measurement, we show that system projection accuracy is sub-decimeter (sub-30 pixel) at AGL altitudes of 10-20m under conditions excluding sustained yawing. During an in-field case study across three agricultural sites, two field workers produced 12,524 annotated frames carrying 55,600 labels in roughly 12 hours (25.5x per-worker rate increase over manual labeling). Detectors trained on imagery collected by this workflow recovered 56-89% of in-view surveyed targets at a geographically distinct farm, at pre-registered operating points; human review of the leading configuration estimates detection precision at 83-87%, spanning three tie-break conventions for clusters carrying contradictory human verdicts.

</details>


### [118] [OCC4M: Object-Centric 4D Memory for Spatiotemporal Reasoning in Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28798)
*Jack B. Jedlicki,Tanguy Dieudonné,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OCC4M 提出以物体为中心的4D记忆（持久轨迹+时序/运动/包含关系），供VLM查询后驱动无历史低层执行器，在长时程操作中显著优于全历史VLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作常需推理当前视野中缺失的状态（物体消失后的位置、时序身份、被翻动容器内容物），而直接把完整观测历史喂给VLM成本高且对视角变化脆弱。

Method: 构建以物体为中心的4D记忆OCC4M：在共享世界坐标系中维护持久物体轨迹，显式表示时序、运动与包含(containment)关系；VLM查询该结构化记忆以选取可操作目标，交给无历史信息的低层执行器执行。

Result: 7个仿真条件350回合：记忆成功率96.6%、端到端88.9%，对比FrameSamp（Gemini 3.7 Flash+完整历史+同一执行器）为54.6%/57.7%；视角迁移测试中OCC4M保持100%记忆/98%端到端，FrameSamp近乎归零；20个固定相机Franka回合中联合记忆准确率85%（FrameSamp≤30%），两阶段任务完成率45%。

Conclusion: 结果表明显式的以物体为中心记忆是实现长时程操作中持久时空推理的有效途径。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation often requires reasoning about state absent from the current view, such as a vanished object's location, temporal identity, or the contents of a shuffled container. We present OCC4M ("Occam"), an object-centric 4D memory that maintains persistent tracks in a shared world frame and explicitly represents temporal, motion, and containment relations. A vision-language model (VLM) queries this structured memory to select actionable targets for history-free low-level execution. Across seven simulation conditions and 350 episodes, OCC4M achieves 96.6% memory success and 88.9% end-to-end success, versus 54.6% and 57.7% for FrameSamp, a raw-history VLM baseline using Gemini 3.7 Flash with the complete observation history and the same executor. In a controlled viewpoint-transfer test, OCC4M maintains 100% memory and 98% end-to-end success after a viewpoint change, while full-history FrameSamp falls to near-zero success. On 20 fixed-camera Franka episodes, OCC4M reaches 85% joint memory accuracy, versus at most 30% for FrameSamp across context sizes from $K=16$ to the complete history, and completes 45% of full two-stage tasks. These results support explicit object-centric memory for persistent spatiotemporal reasoning in long-horizon manipulation. Qualitative videos are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [119] [An Analysis of Streaming Deep Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Continual Learning in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28807)
*Teeratham Vitchutripop,Alyssa Quarles,Wenhe Zhang,Richard Xue,Daniel Rakita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统分析"流式深度强化学习"（streaming deep RL）作为机器人持续自适应学习框架的可行性：在预训练后，仅用最新经验在线更新策略，可使机器人快速适应自身、环境或目标的未知变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人部署后常遇到训练分布外的新场景导致性能衰减。主流做法是扩大离线数据集以提升策略鲁棒性，但这是批式、离线的；而生物学习是逐时刻的流式经验学习。已有工作虽证明流式深度 RL 可行，但尚未证明它能作为机器人策略适应未知变化的可行持续学习框架，因此需要首次系统分析。

Method: 在初始预训练阶段之后，采用基于流（stream）的深度 RL 更新范式：每次仅使用最新经验进行在线更新，不依赖经验回放式的批量数据。重点考察特定优化器与"可塑性损失（plasticity loss）缓解技术"的组合，利用预训练获得的领域与任务知识，实现在线快速适应。实验以四足运动（quadruped locomotion）为主，随后扩展到机器人操作任务以检验结论的可迁移性。

Result: 在四足运动任务中，配备合适优化器与可塑性缓解技术的深度神经网络策略，能利用预训练知识通过流式学习快速在线适应多种变化，性能优于批式 on-policy 方法，任务成功率相对预训练策略最高提升 90%。在操作任务上，该优势可部分复现，但存在稳定性与性能方面的局限。

Conclusion: 流式深度 RL 是有前景的机器人持续自适应学习框架：预训练 + 流式在线更新可在无需扩充离线数据集的情况下应对未知变化，尤其在四足运动上效果显著，但在操作任务上仍有稳定性与性能限制。作者讨论了工作局限，并展望其对未来持续机器人学习研究的意义。

Abstract: Over the course of a lifetime, robots may encounter novel scenarios unaccounted for in its original training that result in performance degradation. One common approach to mitigating this issue is to further grow the offline training dataset in hopes of producing a policy robust to these changes. In contrast, biological learning occurs moment-to-moment via a stream of experience, unlike the predominantly batch-based and offline nature of deep learning. Although recent works show the feasibility of stream-based deep reinforcement learning, where updates use only the latest experience, none have shown it to be a viable continual learning framework for adapting robotic policies to unseen changes. In this paper, we present the first analysis of streaming deep reinforcement learning for adaptive continual learning in robotics. In particular, we show that, following an initial pretraining phase, streaming deep RL can enable a robot to successfully adapt to unforeseen changes to itself, its environment, or goals. Our primary experiments within quadruped locomotion demonstrate that a deep neural network robotic policy with certain optimizers and plasticity loss mitigation techniques can successfully leverage domain task knowledge from its pretraining to quickly adapt online to diverse changes via stream learning, outperforming batch-based on-policy methods and improving task success rates by up to 90% over the pretrained policy. Furthermore, we perform additional evaluations on robotic manipulation tasks to determine if our previous observations extend to different robotic morphologies and scenarios. Our results show that the successes observed in quadruped locomotion can be partially realized in manipulation with stability and performance limitations. We conclude with a discussion on the limitations of our work and its implications for the future of continual robot learning.

</details>


### [120] [FlyCNS: Connectome-Grounded Information Organization for Communication-Constrained Embodied Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28816)
*Jinchang Zhang,Jiakai Lin,Guoyu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FlyCNS，一个受果蝇脑-神经索连接组启发的具身信息组织框架：保留各肢体的局部传感运动计算，通过上行/下行分离的路由通路进行选择性长程通信，用连接组方向结构复杂度作为通信分配的弱先验，消息内容与时序由RL学习；在Unitree Go1仿真中收紧通信预算时性能退化更graceful。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人本体天然是分布式感知与驱动，但基于学习的控制仍普遍依赖集中式信息处理。核心问题是：在通信受限的具身控制中，哪些计算应保持局部、哪些信息值得传输以完成全身协调。

Method: 受果蝇脑-神经索连接组启发，FlyCNS在每个肢体内部保留局部传感运动计算，并通过独立的上行与下行路由通路实现选择性长程通信。从真实连接组中提取两类通路的方向性结构复杂度，作为通信资源分配的弱先验；而消息内容、传输时机与运动策略保持任务自适应，通过强化学习习得。

Result: 在Unitree Go1仿真中，通信预算收紧时FlyCNS性能退化更平缓。最严格设置下仅使用全通信参考约21–22%的通信量，在两种指令协议下仍保持约0.882的跟踪分数，与全通信参考差距不超过6.1%。

Conclusion: 真实神经连接组不仅能指导控制网络的结构设计，还可为跨本体的信息组织提供可迁移的归纳偏置，引导机器人在有限通信资源下平衡局部计算与长程协调。

Abstract: Robotic bodies are inherently distributed in sensing and actuation, yet learning-based control still commonly relies on centralized information processing. This work studies the problem of information organization in communication-constrained embodied control: which computations should remain local, and which information is worth transmitting for whole-body coordination. We propose FlyCNS, an embodied information-organization framework inspired by the Drosophila brain--nerve-cord connectome. FlyCNS preserves local sensorimotor computation within each limb and enables selective long-range communication through separate ascending and descending routing pathways. From a real connectome, FlyCNS extracts the directional structural complexity of these two pathway types and uses it as a weak prior over communication allocation, while message content, transmission timing, and locomotion policies remain task-adaptive and are learned through reinforcement learning. In Unitree Go1 simulation, FlyCNS exhibits more graceful performance degradation as the communication budget is tightened. Under the most restrictive setting, it uses only about 21--22\% of the communication of the full-communication reference, while still maintaining a tracking score of approximately 0.882 under both command protocols, with a gap of no more than 6.1\% from the full-communication reference. These results indicate that real neural connectomes can inform not only the structural design of control networks, but also provide transferable inductive biases for information organization across embodiments, guiding robots in balancing local computation and long-range coordination under limited communication resources.

</details>


### [121] [KeyGen: Unsupervised Keypoint based Object-Centric Representations for Category-Level Policy Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28818)
*Shuxin Cao,Liquan Wang,Masoud Moghani,Benjamin Joffe,Animesh Garg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KeyGen 从点云学习规范化语义 3D 关键点，作为结构化物体中心表示，配合视动扩散策略生成操作轨迹，在类别级泛化上显著优于基线，并在仿真与真机中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统行为克隆易过拟合到具体实例的几何与外观，导致对未见物体的迁移能力差。机器人操作泛化需要在形状、尺寸、位姿各异的未见实例上完成任务，因此需要一种具备跨实例几何对应关系的物体中心表示。

Method: 提出 KeyGen 框架：从点云中学习规范化（canonicalized）语义 3D 关键点，作为结构化的物体中心表示；视动扩散策略以这些关键点结合物体中心几何为条件，预测完整操作轨迹。同时构建了含三类操作任务的写实仿真基准，以及规划驱动的数据生成流水线，可跨多样物体实例产出专家轨迹。

Result: 在已见与未见物体、位姿变化条件下均显著优于先前方法；随每物体示范数量增加可有效扩展；对物体尺度变化保持稳健；在仿真与真实世界操作中均取得良好性能。

Conclusion: 规范化语义 3D 关键点作为物体中心结构化表示，可有效缓解实例级过拟合、提升类别级泛化，是操作策略学习的一种有效归纳偏置。

Abstract: Generalization in robotic manipulation requires policies to perform tasks across diverse unseen object instances that vary in shape, size, and pose. However, conventional behavior cloning (BC) methods often overfit to instance-specific geometry and appearance, limiting transfer to novel objects. We introduce KeyGen, a framework that learns canonicalized semantic 3D keypoints from point clouds and uses them as structured object-centric representations for policy learning. A visuomotor diffusion policy conditions on these keypoints together with object-centric geometry to predict full manipulation trajectories, enabling consistent geometric correspondence across object instances. To evaluate category-level generalization, we construct a photorealistic simulation benchmark with three manipulation tasks and a planning-driven data generation pipeline that produces expert trajectories across diverse object instances. Experiments show that KeyGen significantly outperforms prior methods on both seen and unseen objects under pose variation, scales effectively with additional demonstrations per object, maintains robustness to object rescaling, and achieves strong performance in both simulation and real-world manipulation.

</details>


### [122] [Uncertainty-Gated Exploration Noise Suppresses Task Collapse in Online RL Fine-Tuning of a Flow-Matching Vision-Language-Action Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28838)
*Mehmet Turan Yardımcı,Yunus Emre Çoğurcu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在LIBERO-10上以450M SmolVLA+PPO(SDE)做小算力对比实验，提出"任务坍缩"失效模式，并用不确定性门控的探索噪声控制器显著缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习微调预训练flow-matching VLA策略时，持续更新常破坏单任务能力，而聚合指标仍显正常，这种"任务坍缩"失效模式此前缺乏受控研究。

Method: 在匹配小算力预算下，于LIBERO-10对450M SmolVLA用PPO配合随机(SDE)采样微调，仅改变一个变量——探索噪声策略：固定噪声尺度、ReinFlow式学习噪声网络、以及基于任务无关新颖度与能力信号、无任务标签与回合边界的不确定性门控控制器（在任务流间重分配探索）。

Result: 池化定义下，固定噪声在3个种子中2个发生坍缩，学习噪声在每个种子（测至迭代200）均坍缩，门控控制器3个种子均无坍缩；参数位移显示控制器动作专家持续变化而平均施加噪声接近固定尺度；降低固定尺度仅减缓而未阻止退化；该预算下无任何方案优于行为克隆基线。此外记录bfloat16无fp32主副本时96.02%动作专家元素跨三个连续迭代位级不变，fp32主副本在参考学习率下单种子观测两臂皆坍缩。

Conclusion: 受控对比支持门控控制器对任务保持的有效性，但其跨状态与跨时间自适应的独立贡献未被解耦；同时揭示了低精度训练下参数几乎不更新的现象，并开源四定义下逐任务坍缩度量工具。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning fine-tuning of pretrained flow-matching vision-language-action (VLA) policies promises robots that keep learning after deployment, but continued updates often destroy competence on individual tasks while the aggregate still looks healthy. We study this failure mode, which we call task collapse, under a matched small-compute budget on LIBERO-10 with a 450M-parameter SmolVLA policy trained by PPO with stochastic (SDE) sampling. Three exploration-noise policies differ in one live variable: a fixed noise scale, a ReinFlow-style learned noise network, and an uncertainty-gated controller that redistributes exploration across task streams from task-agnostic novelty and competence signals, without task labels or episode boundaries. Under the pooled definition, fixed noise collapses tasks in two of three seeds and learned noise in every seed measured to iteration 200, while the controller collapses none in any of its three seeds. Measured parameter displacement shows the controller's action expert keeps changing, while its mean applied noise is close to the fixed scale in the available logs. The matched comparison supports the controller's effect on task preservation; the separate contributions of its adaptation across states and over time are not disentangled. A lower fixed scale slows the decline but does not stop it. No arm improves on the behavior-cloning baseline in this budget. Two properties of that regime are measured beside this result, not offered as its cause: following the reference recipe, training runs in bfloat16 with no fp32 master copy, under which 96.02% of the action expert's elements stay bit-identical across three consecutive iterations, and an fp32 master copy at the reference learning rate collapses both arms in a single-seed observation. We release tools measuring per-task collapse under four definitions, rescoring noise and instrument tares.

</details>


### [123] [Online Sim-to-Real Adaptation via Closed-Loop System Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28878)
*Yuhao Huang,Samuel A. Moore,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OSRAM提出在部署后只调整控制器的参考指令（在线参考自适应），将机器人+策略视为统一闭环动力学系统，通过闭环系统建模提升sim-to-real跟踪精度，无需微调策略或辨识完整物理动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sim-to-real迁移虽已有进展，但残余动力学失配会导致硬件上控制器稳定但跟踪精度下降。传统修正方法（辨识系统动力学、自适应控制策略、回仿真微调）都需要大量数据和计算。

Method: OSRAM把已部署机器人和其策略建模为统一闭环动力学系统，直接学习任务级"指令—响应"行为。闭环动力学模型在仿真中跨随机动力学进行元训练，部署后用有限真实交互快速微调；再用自适应模型优化未来参考指令，底层控制策略保持不变。

Result: 在仿真和硬件上评估双足速度跟踪与loco-manipulation任务：闭环建模提升了对未见动力学的预测与跟踪精度；在线参考自适应在不同控制目标和硬件配置下降低了残余sim-to-real跟踪误差。

Conclusion: 调整机器人—策略闭环行为的参考指令，是替代策略微调或完整物理动力学辨识进行sim-to-real迁移的实用方案。

Abstract: Sim-to-real transfer has made substantial progress, but can still produce controllers that remain stable and functional on hardware while suffering from degraded tracking accuracy due to residual dynamics mismatch. Correcting these errors typically requires identifying the underlying system dynamics, adapting the control policy, or returning to simulation for additional training and finetuning, all of which can require substantial data and computation. We propose OSRAM (Online Sim-to-Real Adaptation via Closed-Loop System Modeling), a framework that instead adapts the reference commands provided to an existing controller. OSRAM treats the deployed robot and its policy as a unified closed-loop dynamical system and learns its task-level command-response behavior directly from tracking observations. A closed-loop dynamics model is meta-trained across randomized dynamics in simulation and rapidly finetuned after deployment using limited real-world interaction. The adapted model is then used to optimize future reference commands while leaving the underlying control policy unchanged. We evaluate OSRAM on bipedal velocity tracking and loco-manipulation in simulation and on hardware. Results show that closed-loop modeling improves prediction and tracking accuracy under unseen dynamics, while online reference adaptation reduces residual sim-to-real tracking errors across different control objectives and hardware configurations. These results demonstrate that adapting the behavior of the robot-policy closed loop provides a practical alternative to finetuning the policy or identifying the full physical dynamics for sim-to-real transfer. More information can be found at this http URL .

</details>


### [124] [Fly, Drive, Reconfigure: A Modular Reconfigurable Aerial-Ground Platform for Field Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28887)
*Li-Yu Lo,Yanbaihui Liu,Chengchuan Shu,Tyler Harris,Jonathan Ryan,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HARP是一个异构空中机器人模块平台：侦察机测绘环境并做能耗感知规划，漫游车与载荷模块独立飞行后自主组装为地面车辆，实现跨地形高效载荷运输与任务执行，并完成格陵兰冰盖实地部署测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构机器人团队虽能通过专用智能体分散互补能力，但物理角色与容量在任务全程通常固定不变，难以适应远程、难通行区域的环境采样等任务。作者希望让机器人的物理能力本身可在任务中动态组合。

Method: HARP由三类模块组成：搭载传感器的侦察机（scouts）、飞行-驱动一体化的rover模块、以及任务专用载荷模块。侦察机测绘环境并向能耗感知规划器提供信息，规划器联合选择路径与空地移动模式；rover与载荷模块独立飞越地面通行受限的地形，随后自主组装为协同地面车辆完成节能载荷运输。

Result: 通过覆盖感知、规划、重构、空地移动、载荷运输与任务执行的全链条实地实验验证系统，并在格陵兰冰盖开展模块级部署测试，面向未来自主任务。

Conclusion: HARP表明异构机器人团队不仅可调整自身行为，还可调整其物理能力在任务中的组合方式。

Abstract: Heterogeneous robot teams distribute complementary capabilities across specialized agents, but their physical roles and capacities typically remain fixed throughout a mission. We present HARP, a Heterogeneous Aerial Robotic modules Platform in which independently deployable aerial robots physically reconfigure to compose their capabilities for field operations. HARP comprises sensor-equipped scouts, flydrive rover modules, and task-specific payload modules. Scouts map the environment and inform an energy-aware planner that jointly selects routes and air-ground mobility modes. Rover and payload modules fly independently across terrain that constrains ground travel, then autonomously assemble into a cooperative ground vehicle for energy-efficient payload transport. Motivated by environmental sampling in remote and difficult-to-traverse regions, we evaluate HARP through field experiments spanning sensing, planning, reconfiguration, airground mobility, payload transport, and task execution. We further conduct module-level deployment tests on the Greenland Ice Sheet toward future autonomous missions. HARP demonstrates how heterogeneous robot teams can adapt not only their actions, but also how their physical capabilities are composed during a mission.

</details>


### [125] [Robots That Take Initiative: A Framework for Building and Evaluating Proactive Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28910)
*Maithili Patel,Sonia Chernova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出主动式机器人辅助的统一形式化（分三级），指出现有离线评估会高估性能，并给出闭环评估（含自适应人类模型）及方法 GAP，从被动观察中学习预测用户目标并行动；闭环下先前 SOTA 方法崩溃，GAP 稳健且大幅胜出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人若仅执行狭窄角色与重复任务，无法真正有效辅助人类；主动辅助（自行判断该做什么而非等待指令）研究零散、缺乏统一形式化，且普遍采用静态人类模型的离线评估，无法体现机器人行为对环境与用户行为的反馈影响。

Method: 1) 建立主动式机器人辅助的统一形式化，划分为三个层级；2) 针对最高层"未受提示的主动辅助"提出解决框架；3) 指出离线评估在此设定下高估性能，提出含"会随机器人适应"的人类模型的闭环评估；4) 提出 GAP 方法实例化该框架，从被动观察中学习以预判用户目标并行动。

Result: 在闭环评估下，先前 SOTA 主动辅助方法性能崩溃，某些情况下其引入的额外负担超过节省的工作量；GAP 保持稳健并显著优于这些方法。

Conclusion: 主动机器人辅助需要统一形式化与闭环、自适应人类模型的评估范式；以被动观察学习预判目标的 GAP 在该范式下展现出显著优势，表明离线评测的结论不可靠。

Abstract: Effective robot assistance beyond narrow roles and repetitive tasks requires robots to be proactive - to decide what needs to be done rather than waiting to be told. While proactivity is increasingly explored, it lacks a unified formulation, and work in the domain is typically evaluated offline against static human models that cannot capture the effect of a robot's actions on the environment and the user's own behavior. We introduce a unified formalism for proactive robot assistance, organize it into three levels, and provide a framework to address the highest level of unprompted proactive assistance. We then show that offline evaluation overstates performance in this setting, and contribute a closed-loop evaluation with a human model that adapts to the robot. Finally, we present a method, GAP, that instantiates our framework, learning from passive observation to anticipate user goals and act. Under closed-loop evaluation, prior state-of-the-art methods collapse, in some cases adding more work than they save, while GAP remains robust and substantially outperforms them.

</details>


### [126] [Koopman-Accelerated Model-Based Diffusion for Real-Time Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28920)
*Bohyeong Pak,Kangmin Lee,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双线性Koopman模型扩散（BK-MBD）：每控制步仅一次状态提升至高维空间，候选轨迹在提升空间内以固定次数矩阵-向量乘完成rollout，实现在线实时轨迹优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模型的扩散（MBD）虽通过噪声退火实现有效轨迹优化，但需反复rollout系统动力学，计算开销大，通常只能离线使用。作者希望在保持扩散规划质量的同时把单次规划更新压缩到控制周期内，实现在线部署。

Method: 将机器人状态在每个控制步仅提升一次到高维空间，此后所有候选在提升空间中传播，使每次rollout退化为固定次数的矩阵-向量乘法。提升后的动力学为双线性形式，使预测输入增益可随机器人构型变化（线性提升模型无法表达）。结合退火噪声调度与学得的代理rollout。

Result: 仿真中每次规划更新≤14.7 ms（控制周期50 ms），所有试验均到达目标，而线性提升几乎从不成功；退火调度在学得rollout下比固定噪声调度闭环精度更高；精确rollout下退火与固定窄噪声调度均能到达目标，说明退火降低了对代理模型误差的敏感度；BK-MBD通过了无单一凸区域覆盖的狭窄通道，而凸化双线性控制器很少成功。实体机械臂上跟踪初始未知的运动目标，是唯一同时满足跟踪与控制时限的方法。

Conclusion: 双线性Koopman提升使基于模型的扩散规划在控制周期内实时可行，保持构型相关输入增益的表达能力，并在仿真与实体机械臂上验证了实时性与任务成功率优势。

Abstract: Conventional model-based diffusion (MBD) achieves effective trajectory optimization by leveraging noise annealing. However, its high computational cost, primarily arising from repeated rollouts of the plant dynamics, has largely confined its use to offline settings. To address this limitation, this paper proposes bilinear Koopman model-based diffusion (BK-MBD). The proposed method lifts the robot's state into a high-dimensional space only once per control step and propagates all candidates in the lifted space thereafter, so each rollout reduces to a fixed number of matrix-vector multiplications. The lifted dynamics are bilinear, allowing the predicted input gain to vary with the robot's configuration, which a linear lifted model cannot represent. In simulation, BK-MBD completed each planning update in at most 14.7 ms within a 50 ms control period and reached the goal on every trial, whereas a linear lift almost never did. The annealed schedule improves closed-loop accuracy over fixed-noise schedules under the learned rollout. Under the exact rollout, both the annealed and fixed-narrow schedules reach every goal, indicating that annealing reduces sensitivity to surrogate-model error. BK-MBD also threaded a passage that no single convex region covers, whereas a convexified bilinear controller rarely succeeded. On a physical manipulator, BK-MBD tracked an initially unknown moving target within the control period and was the only method that met both the tracking task and the deadline. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [127] [Streaming-WAM: Action-Conditioned World-Action Model for Asynchronous Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28927)
*Xuyao Huang,Yixuan Wang,Zengyao Ye,Boyuan Zhao,Chenyang Yu,Haoran Wen,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Streaming-WAM，将动作条件世界模型与异步机器人控制耦合，在流式更新中把已承诺动作作为固定前缀进行未来视觉预测，以指导剩余动作生成，显著降低推理等待时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM 推理时需预测未来视觉，生成开销大；异步执行可重叠推理与运动以减少等待，但后续动作生成所需的视觉预测必须考虑推理期间已调度执行动作的影响，现有方法未处理这一"已承诺动作"问题。

Method: 每个流式更新时，以最新观测与已承诺动作（构成下一个动作块的固定前缀）为条件预测未来视觉；由此得到的动作条件视觉特征在同一联合更新内指导剩余动作的生成，使动作延续部分能够感知固定前缀执行期间预期的场景变化。

Result: LIBERO 上平均成功率 98.35%，平均回合时间相对 Fast-WAM 缩短 2.93 倍；真实 Stamp Paper 任务平均回合时间从同步 Joint-WAM 的 90 s 降至 38 s，同时保持高成功率。

Conclusion: Streaming-WAM 通过将已承诺动作纳入未来视觉预测，实现了高效异步控制并保持高任务成功率，验证了动作条件世界模型与异步执行耦合的有效性。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) that use future visual prediction at inference time incur substantial generation costs. Asynchronous execution reduces waiting by overlapping inference with robot motion, but visual predictions used for subsequent action generation must anticipate the effects of actions already scheduled for execution during inference. We introduce Streaming-WAM, which couples action-conditioned world modeling with asynchronous robot control to account for committed actions in future visual prediction. At each streaming update, the model conditions future visual prediction on the latest observation and the committed actions, which form the fixed prefix of the next action chunk. The resulting action-conditioned visual features guide generation of the remaining actions within the same joint update, so the continuation is informed by the scene changes expected during execution of the fixed prefix. On LIBERO, Streaming-WAM achieves an average success rate of 98.35\% and reduces mean episode time by a factor of 2.93 relative to Fast-WAM. On the real-world Stamp Paper task, mean episode time falls from 90 s with synchronous Joint-WAM to 38 s with Streaming-WAM. These results show that Streaming-WAM supports efficient asynchronous control while maintaining high task success rates.

</details>


### [128] [A Field-Deployable GNSS-based Navigation Stack for Outdoor Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28933)
*Yiyuan Lin,Cole Regnier,Yu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一套ROS 2户外农业机器人导航栈，支持单GNSS-IMU与双天线GNSS两种定位前端，统一输出局部ENU状态，并对比纯追踪、横偏差PID、NMPC与分段混合调度器在800次葡萄园实车试验中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外机器人导航不仅需要精确接收机与路径跟踪律，还要求从地理航点到执行器指令保持几何一致性，并显式暴露量测有效性与时序、应对失效或过期状态信息。现有工作缺乏对定位前端与控制器组合的系统化、可复现的实地评估。

Method: 构建ROS 2导航栈，定位前端可互换（单GNSS+IMU / 双天线GNSS），统一提供局部ENU状态接口；控制器包含纯追踪、虚拟点横偏差PID、有限时域非线性MPC及分段依赖的混合调度器。架构层面明确坐标约定、基准初始化、异步状态构建、航点几何、控制器方程、质量门限、指令仲裁与看门狗机制。在2025–2026年葡萄园部署中采集独立实车数据，约199.6 m路线，8种控制器-定位组合各100次，共800次平衡试验。

Result: 行间混合模式取得最低的运行平均采集后横向误差MAE：单GNSS+IMU为0.00952 m，双天线GNSS为0.00846 m。结果刻画了在评估条件下记录位置相对参考路径的偏差。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了系统化、可复现的户外导航架构规范与大规模实车对比证据，开源软件与部署说明可获取，可作为农业/户外移动机器人导航工程与算法选型的参考基线。

Abstract: Outdoor robots require more than an accurate receiver and a path-tracking law: the navigation system must preserve geometric consistency from geographic waypoints to actuator commands, expose measurement validity and timing, and respond to invalid or stale state information. This work presents a ROS~2 navigation stack with interchangeable single-GNSS--IMU and dual-antenna-GNSS localization front ends. Both provide a common local East--North--Up state interface for pure pursuit, virtual-point cross-track PID, finite-horizon nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC), and a segment-dependent hybrid dispatcher. The architecture specifies coordinate conventions, datum initialization, asynchronous state construction, waypoint geometry, controller equations, quality gates, command arbitration, and watchdog behavior. Independent physical field runs collected during 2025 and 2026 grape-vineyard deployments support a balanced evaluation of 800 runs, with 100 runs for each of eight controller--localization combinations on an approximately 199.6-m route. The row-hybrid mode yields the lowest run-averaged post-acquisition mean absolute cross-track error (MAE) in the evaluated dataset: 0.00952~m with single GNSS+IMU and 0.00846~m with dual GNSS. These findings characterize deviations of the recorded positions from the reference route under the evaluated conditions. The open-source navigation software and deployment instructions are available in the this https URL .

</details>


### [129] [RoboRecover: Benchmarking Robot Policy Recovery under Execution Deviations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28952)
*Yang Li,Chen Zhao,Zhuoran Wang,Jiankang Wang,Chao Shao,Yihan Lin,Haitao Shen,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboRecover：一个评估机器人在执行偏差下从中间状态恢复能力的基准，含 RoboTwin 与 LIBERO 共 2000 个场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略基准多从预设初始状态评估完整轨迹，关注初始场景与最终结果，忽视闭环执行中动作与接触造成的中间状态偏离及恢复需求。

Method: 从轨迹中选取偏差状态，通过回放动作前缀重建这些中间状态，并在原任务上评估策略，从而构建恢复基准；每平台 1000 个场景，固定 800/200 训练/测试划分。

Result: 初始状态性能不能决定恢复性能；不同策略在各场景中恢复能力差异明显；训练划分可支持恢复干预研究。

Conclusion: 将执行诱导中间状态下的恢复确立为机器人策略评估的独立维度。

Abstract: Robot-policy benchmarks increasingly cover diverse tasks and preset out-of-distribution conditions, but typically evaluate complete trajectories from predefined initial states. These evaluations often focus on the initialized scene and the final outcome, while paying less attention to the dynamic interaction process. During closed-loop execution, actions and contacts can alter object relations and task progress, producing off-nominal intermediate states that need recovery. Recovery requires a policy to infer how task progress has changed, correct the relevant relations, and continue the original goal. We introduce RoboRecover, a benchmark for robot policy recovery under execution deviations. RoboRecover selects deviation states from trajectories, reconstructs them by replaying action prefixes, and evaluates policies on the original task. RoboRecover contains 2,000 scenarios across RoboTwin and LIBERO, with 1,000 scenarios and a fixed 800/200 train/test split on each platform. Results show that initial-state performance does not determine recovery performance and policies exhibit different recovery strengths across scenarios. Using its training split, RoboRecover further supports study on recovery interventions. RoboRecover establishes recovery from execution-induced intermediate states as a distinct dimension of robot policy evaluation.

</details>


### [130] [ActGaze: Learning Action-Grounded Gaze through Counterfactual Visual Interventions for High-Precision Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28955)
*Jinxuan Zhu,Jiaheng Wang,Chao Tang,Mengfan Wang,Hao Wei,Shengbao Li,Hong Yin,Yiwen Gao,Chenrui Tie,Tingguang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ActGaze 训练方法，利用反事实视觉干预从 VLA 模型自身动作目标中导出空间监督，引导视觉注意力聚焦任务相关区域，从而提升高精度机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在高精度操作任务中表现不佳，作者将其归因于视觉注意力分散在任务无关区域；而先前依赖外部标注进行注视监督的方法受限于标注成本与可用性。

Method: ActGaze 不依赖外部 gaze 标签，而是通过反事实视觉干预（对图像区域进行扰动/遮蔽并观察动作预测的变化）识别对动作预测关键的区域，从而从动作目标中直接导出空间监督信号，指导 VLA 策略的视觉注意力分布。

Result: 在四个高精度真实机器人操作任务上的实验表明，ActGaze 使视觉注意力更集中于任务相关区域，并持续优于基础 VLA 策略及其他视觉定位（visual-grounding）方法。

Conclusion: 从动作目标自动导出的注视监督是一种无需额外标注、可有效提升 VLA 高精度操作能力的训练范式。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models often struggle with high-precision robotic manipulation. We attribute this limitation primarily to their visual attention being dispersed across task-irrelevant regions. To address this issue, we propose ActGaze, a training approach that guides VLA policies to gaze on task-relevant regions, much like humans gaze on critical visual cues while executing precise movements. Unlike prior methods that rely on external labels for gaze supervision, ActGaze derives spatial supervision directly from the VLA's own action objective by using counterfactual visual interventions to identify regions that are critical for action prediction. Extensive real-robot experiments on four high-precision robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that ActGaze induces more focused visual attention on task-relevant regions and consistently outperforms the base VLA policy and other visual-grounding approaches.

</details>


### [131] [TactileStep: Sole Tactile Learning for Regulating Foot-Terrain Interaction in Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28959)
*Zizhuo Wang,Ming-ju Lee,Shaoting Zhu,Haozhe Lou,Hang Zhao,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TactileStep：将足底压力触觉传感引入人形机器人运动控制的可部署学习框架，通过触觉仿真与真实压力鞋垫对齐、接触相位识别与相位感知奖励，实现更柔和的着地与更稳定的支撑，并在 Unitree G1 上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形跑酷策略虽能通过复杂地形，但任务成功掩盖了硬着地、边缘接触与支撑不稳等问题。人类依靠触觉反馈按地形刚度调节接触顺应性，而多数人形机器人缺乏丰富触觉感知，构成关键的能力差距。

Method: 提出 TactileStep 触觉学习框架：(1) 使触觉仿真与真实压力鞋垫对齐，保证策略学到与硬件一致的接触特征；(2) 训练中利用触觉与运动线索识别足部接触相位，并施加相位感知奖励，鼓励安全着地与稳定支撑。

Result: 在仿真与 Unitree G1 人形机器人多种地形上评估：相比强感知基线，着地峰值力最多降低 48.8%，A 计权冲击噪声峰值最多降低 30.1 dB，支撑接触面积最多提升 23.8%。

Conclusion: 足底触觉感知可有效弥合人机在接触调制上的差距，为人形机器人提供更柔和、更稳定的步态，且具备实机可部署性。

Abstract: Humanoid parkour policies can traverse various terrains, but task completion may mask challenges of harsh landings, edge contacts, and unstable stance contacts. Humans naturally regulate foot-terrain interaction through tactile feedback, modulating contact compliance according to terrain stiffness. This highlights a key domain gap between humans and humanoid robots: the absence of rich tactile sensing in most humanoid systems. We address this problem with TactileStep, a deployable tactile learning framework that brings sole pressure sensing into humanoid locomotion control for softer touchdowns and more stable support. TactileStep aligns tactile simulation with the real pressure insole, allowing the policy to learn from the same contact features available on hardware. During training, we use tactile and motion cues to recognize different foot-contact phases and apply phase-aware rewards that encourage safer landing and more stable stance. Evaluated in simulation and on a Unitree G1 humanoid across diverse terrains, TactileStep reduces peak touchdown force by up to 48.8% and peak A-weighted impact noise by up to 30.1 dB over a strong perceptive baseline, while increasing stance contact area by up to 23.8%.

</details>


### [132] [Echo in the Steps: Learning Perceptive Humanoid Parkour with Gated Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28960)
*Ming-Ju Lee,Zizhuo Wang,Shaoting Zhu,Haozhe Lou,Hang Zhao,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个仅用机载深度感知的人形机器人跑酷框架，通过显著性引导的时序感知模块（显著性先验+门控记忆）和交替损失对称正则，实现稀疏落脚点地形上的稳定穿越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化地形上的人形感知运动已有进展，但在高度不连续环境中的敏捷跑酷仍是开放难题：跨稀疏落脚点和狭窄支撑区需要精确落脚点选择、有效利用视觉观测，并在快速切换中保持一致的左右交替步态。

Method: 框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 显著性引导的时序感知模块，将显著性先验与门控记忆结合，跨帧保留有信息量的深度特征，从而在部分观测下可靠选点；(2) 引入交替损失（alternation loss）作为对称性正则，鼓励交替步态模式并提升通过鲁棒性。仅依赖机载深度观测。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界的挑战性地形上，方法在成功率与落脚点精度上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 仅凭机载深度即可实现人形机器人稀疏落脚点跑酷，显著性引导时序记忆与步态交替正则共同提升鲁棒性，具备真机验证。

Abstract: While recent advances in perceptive locomotion have enabled humanoid robots to traverse structured terrains, agile parkour in highly discontinuous environments remains an open challenge. In particular, crossing sparse footholds and narrow support regions requires precise foothold selection, effective use of visual observations, and consistent alternating foot placement during fast transitions. In this paper, we present a perceptive humanoid parkour framework that enables stable traversal across terrains with limited foothold availability using only onboard depth observations. The framework features a saliency-guided temporal perception module that combines a saliency prior with gated memory. It retains informative depth features across frames, enabling reliable foot placement from partial observations. By introducing an alternation loss, our symmetry regularization encourages alternating gait patterns and improves traversal robustness. Extensive experiments show that our method significantly improves success rate and foothold accuracy on challenging terrains in both simulation and the real world.

</details>


### [133] [Sim-to-Real Aware End-to-End Learning Environment for Micromobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28969)
*Shouma Amano,Takuya Azumi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文为WHILL Model CR微型移动车辆提出了一种面向仿真到现实迁移（sim-to-real）、车辆特定的端到端学习环境，基于AWSIM与ROS 2构建，并通过贝叶斯优化利用真实数据校正物理参数以缩小现实差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型移动车辆（如差速驱动、全向轮）的特定运动学难以被通用仿真器准确刻画，导致端到端自动驾驶系统难以迁移至此类平台，亟需车辆专属的仿真环境与sim-to-real方案。

Method: 在AWSIM与ROS 2上搭建车辆专属端到端学习环境；用真实世界数据通过贝叶斯优化物理参数以缩小sim-to-real差距；设计面向世界模型类智能体稳定训练的同步架构；采用DreamerV3训练端到端策略。

Result: 物理参数优化在多类驾驶场景下显著降低轨迹误差；DreamerV3训练的策略在仿真避障任务中完成学习并达成任务完成；策略无需微调即可直接迁移到实体车辆，在真实走廊复刻环境中绕过纸箱。

Conclusion: 该工作为微型移动车辆的sim-to-real策略研究提供了实用基础，验证了世界模型智能体在车辆特定仿真环境中的可训练性与直接迁移可行性。

Abstract: While end-to-end autonomous driving systems show promise, their application to micromobility vehicles is hindered by simulators failing to capture specific kinematics, such as differential drives and omni-wheels. This paper pro- poses a sim-to-real-aware, vehicle-specific end-to-end learning environment for the WHILL Model CR on AWSIM and ROS 2. To minimize the sim-to-real gap, physical parameters are optimized via Bayesian optimization using real-world data, reducing trajectory errors across various driving scenarios. Additionally, this study introduces a synchronized architecture tailored for the stable training of world model-based agents. An end-to-end policy trained with DreamerV3 exhibited learning progress and achieved task completion in a simulated obstacle avoidance setting. Furthermore, this policy demonstrated direct sim-to-real transfer to the physical vehicle, enabling the vehicle to navigate around a cardboard box in a real-world corridor replica without fine-tuning. This paper provides a practical foundation for sim-to-real micromobility policy studies.

</details>


### [134] [AquaMend: Minimal Re-probing and Conditional Rollback for Latent-Belief Failures in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28973)
*Yufan Liu,Shang Luo,Yang Liu,Haoxuan Jia,Feiyu Han,Qian Li,Chen Li,Yingguang Yang,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Kefu Xu,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AquaMend 在"探针-信念-动作图"上以期望损失为目标比较重新探测、回滚与支持性延续三种修复策略，用联合后验驱动带条件检测力筛选的一步策略；在自建仿真基准的 32 个配对场景中恢复 28/32，将平均完全损失较重启降低 21.6%，但与 DTT 的配对差异经 Holm 校正后不显著，且在线决策时间整体降低 12.3%、未覆盖晚期阶段反而增加 3.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体的任务相关信念会因物理变化或感知错误而失效，需要决定是重新探测、回滚到先前状态，还是直接继续执行。论文旨在为这三类修复动作提供一个可比较的决策论框架，并量化各策略在感知成本、物理恢复成本和未纠正失败之间的权衡。

Method: 构建探针-信念-动作图（probe-belief-action graph），以覆盖感知成本、物理恢复与未纠正失败的期望损失为目标函数；用联合后验（joint posterior）驱动一步策略，并加入条件检测力筛选（conditional detection-power screening）。理论分析给出单信念三选一最优所需的独立性、可分性与完全消解探针条件，并指出一般策略无全局最优保证。

Result: 32 个配对仿真场景中 28/32 恢复成功，平均完全损失较 restart 降低 21.6%；与决策论排障（DTT）的配对损失差异经 Holm 校正后不显著；相对全候选消融，在线决策时间整体下降 12.3%，但在未覆盖的晚期阶段上升 3.4%。

Conclusion: 信念修复可被形式化为期望损失下的三路选择问题，启发式一步策略在仿真中有效但缺乏全局最优保证，且优势相对已有 DTT 基线并不显著；证据限于自建仿真基准，缺少真机验证与真实任务泛化结果。

Abstract: Physical changes or sensing errors can invalidate embodied agents' task-relevant beliefs. AquaMend compares re-probing, rollback, and supported continuation on a probe-belief-action graph under an expected-loss objective covering sensing, physical recovery, and uncorrected failures. A joint posterior guides a one-step policy with conditional detection-power screening. The per-belief three-way optimum requires independence, separability, and fully resolving probes; the general policy has no global optimality guarantee. Across 32 paired scenarios in a self-constructed simulation benchmark, AquaMend recovers in 28/32 cases and reduces mean complete loss by 21.6% versus restart. Its paired loss difference from decision-theoretic troubleshooting (DTT) is not statistically significant after Holm correction. Against the all-candidate ablation, online decision time decreases by 12.3% overall but increases by 3.4% in the uncovered late stage.

</details>


### [135] [ReVNM: Learning-Based Visual Navigation from a Remote Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28976)
*Michikuni Eguchi,Kohei Honda,Masafumi Endo,Yasuhiro Yoshimura,Ryo Yonetani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ReVNM：用单个远程监控相机同时作为观测源与隐式环境地图，通过 exo2ego 模块从远程视角预测机器人自视角深度观测，仅在随机生成的多样化障碍与视角仿真世界中训练即可零微调泛化到真实机器人导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉导航模型（VNM）虽可摆脱几何定位与建图，但长距离导航仍依赖预建地图；使用远程相机可省去预建地图与机载视觉处理，但其有限的固定外视角视野取代了自视角观测，导致避障困难。同时，缺乏多样化远程视角的训练数据进一步加剧该问题。

Method: 提出 learning-by-synthesis 的学习框架。ReVNM 在 SOTA VNM 架构上扩展 exo2ego 模块，将远程相机的第三人称观测映射为机器人自视角深度预测，使 VNM 能在规划路径时考虑机器人前方障碍。训练数据由随机生成的 worlds 合成，包含多样化的障碍布局与相机视角，从而覆盖远程视角的分布多样性。

Result: 仅在随机生成世界中训练，ReVNM 即可泛化到真实机器人导航，无需额外微调。仿真与真实环境实验均验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 远程单目监控相机可作为隐式地图支撑视觉导航，exo2ego 的跨视角观测合成与数据合成训练策略可有效弥合远程视角与自视角之间的鸿沟，实现无预建地图的免碰撞导航。

Abstract: Visual Navigation Models (VNMs) enable robots to navigate from egocentric visual observations without geometric localization and planning, but long-range navigation still requires pre-built maps. This paper presents the Remote Visual Navigation Model (ReVNM), which uses a single remote surveillance camera to serve as both an observation source and an implicit environmental map for visual navigation. While the use of remote cameras could eliminate the need for pre-built maps as well as onboard vision processing, their limited field of view instead of egocentric observations makes it hard to achieve collision-free navigation. The lack of existing data with diverse remote viewpoints, which are crucial for training robust VNMs, further complicates the challenge. In this work, we propose a learning-by-synthesis approach to address this two-fold challenge. Our ReVNM extends a state-of-the-art VNM architecture with an exocentric-to-egocentric (exo2ego) module that predicts an egocentric depth observation from remote-camera observations. This helps the VNM to plan a path while considering obstacles in front of the robot. Trained only on randomly generated worlds with diverse obstacle layouts and camera viewpoints, ReVNM can generalize well to real robot navigation without additional fine-tuning. Experiments in both simulation and real-world environments confirmed the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [136] [CrossSafe: Towards Cross-Embodiment Latent Safety Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28984)
*Ihab Tabbara,Yuxuan Yang,Hussein Sibai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"具身条件安全滤波"：在形态感知潜空间中做Hamilton-Jacobi可达性分析,跨机器人共享安全价值函数与安全策略,单一策略可零样本泛化到未见具身并降低碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨具身学习已证明VLA模型可共享状态表示与操作技能,作者假设安全约束的推理(障碍检测、规避判断、抽象安全动作选择)同样跨具身共享;但安全抽象动作的物理实现依赖形态、运动学与动力学,同一动作对某机器人安全、对另一机器人不安全。通用操作策略通常工作于共享末端执行器动作空间,未显式建模安全性对形态/运动学的依赖。

Method: 提出具身条件安全滤波(embodiment-conditioned safety filtering)。使用机器人及其环境的形态感知潜表示,在潜空间直接执行Hamilton-Jacobi可达性分析,学习HJ值函数与对应的安全最大化策略;该值函数与策略跨机器人共享,同时显式以每个机器人的形态与运动学为条件。在5个双臂机器人具身、5个含全身避碰约束的操作任务上评估,并联合训练单策略。

Result: 在5个任务、4个具身上联合训练的单一策略可零样本泛化到留出的第5个具身,降低标称策略的碰撞率;使用更多具身进行训练能改善泛化性能。

Conclusion: 安全性推理可跨具身共享,只要其抽象动作的实现以具身形态/运动学为条件;潜空间HJ可达性分析提供了一种可扩展的跨具身安全滤波框架。

Abstract: Cross-embodiment learning has shown that a single model, such as a vision-language-action (VLA) model, can learn state representations and manipulation skills that can be applied across heterogeneous robots to accomplish various tasks. We hypothesize that the same holds for safety enforcement. The reasoning required to satisfy a safety constraint, such as detecting an obstacle, recognizing that it should be avoided, and selecting a safe abstract action, is largely shared across robots. What differs across embodiments is how the abstract safe action is realized: morphology, kinematics, and dynamics determine which actions are safe and feasible. Consequently, the same action can be safe for one robot and unsafe for another. This is especially important for generalist manipulation policies that operate in a common end-effector action space without explicitly capturing how safety depends on the robot's morphology and kinematics. We propose embodiment-conditioned safety filtering, in which a Hamilton-Jacobi reachability-based value function and its corresponding safety-maximizing policy are shared across robots. Using a morphology-aware latent representation of the robot and its environment, we perform Hamilton-Jacobi reachability analysis directly in latent space so that the learned safety concepts can generalize across embodiments while remaining explicitly conditioned on each robot's morphology and kinematics. We evaluate our approach across five bimanual robot embodiments and five manipulation tasks with whole-body collision-avoidance constraints. Our results show that a single policy, jointly trained across five manipulation tasks and four embodiments, exhibits zero-shot generalization to a held-out embodiment, reducing the nominal policy's collision rate. They also show that training using more embodiments improves generalization.

</details>


### [137] [CALM: Current Aligned Link Manipulation for Single Arm Oversized Object Lifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29017)
*Jun Hu,Sihan Chen,Kosta Jovanovic,David Navarro-Alarcon,Xueqian Wang,Jia Pan,Peng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Current-Aligned Link Manipulation 框架，利用电机电流作为关节负载反馈，分三阶段策略学习并用阶段路由器生成演示，通过因果电流映射器完成 sim-to-real 对齐，再以统一学生策略+DAgger 部署，实现超大物体整体手臂操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人通常仅用末端执行器操作物体，而人类会灵活使用前臂、肘部等部位处理超大物体。学习这种整体手臂操作面临长时程稀疏奖励、接触感知有限、接触与驱动器动力学 sim-to-real 差距等难题。
