Method: 1) 三阶段专用策略分别用仿真特权信息学习重定位、抓取、抬升，经阶段路由器串联生成完整任务演示；2) 因果电流映射器从仿真关节历史预测物理电机电流，对齐仿真与硬件的驱动器电流观测；3) 统一学生策略仅用可部署传感器观测学习演示，并用 DAgger 进一步精炼；任务策略全在仿真训练，学生在硬件部署。

Result: 仿真中完整任务成功率 76.2%（762/1000 次试验），物理机器人上顺序超大物体抬升任务成功率 73.3%（22/30 次试验）。

Conclusion: 以电机电流作为负载相关反馈并结合因果电流映射，可有效缓解长时程接触富集操作的稀疏奖励与 sim-to-real 差距，实现真实机器人上的整体手臂超大物体操作。

Abstract: Most robots manipulate objects solely with their end effectors, whereas humans flexibly leverage different body parts, such as the forearm and elbow, especially when handling oversized objects. Learning such whole-arm manipulation is chal-lenging due to long-horizon sparse rewards, limited contact sens-ing, and the sim-to-real gap in contact and actuator dynamics. To address these challenges, we propose Current-Aligned Link Manipulation, a framework for learning long-horizon contact-rich manipulation using motor current as joint load related feedback. Three stage-specific policies first learn repositioning, grasping, and lifting using privileged simulation information, and a stage router sequences them to generate complete task demonstrations. For sim-to-real transfer, a causal current mapper predicts physical motor current from simulated joint histories, aligning the actuator current observation between simulation and hardware. A unified student policy then learns from these demonstrations using only deployable sensor observations and is further refined with DAgger. The task policies are trained entirely in simulation, and the final student is deployed on hardware. Experiments demonstrate 76.2% (762/1000 trials) complete-task success in simulation and 73.3% success (22/30 trials) on the physical robot for sequential oversized-object lifting.

</details>


### [138] [Outcome-Sensitive Motion Search for Impact-Aware Dexterous Catching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29020)
*Guorui Pei,Jinsong Wu,Songyuan Su,Jiaming Qi,Sichao Liu,David Navarro-Alarcon,Bin Liu,Peng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"结果敏感窗口(OSW)"刻画接触前动作微扰对撞击/抓取结果的高敏感性，并用Outcome-Sensitive Motion Search学习任务条件下的成功OSW动作流形做局部测地搜索，修复RL教师失败的任务条件、精化教师成功轨迹，再经校准的动作误差模型全轨迹验证后产出IL示范，使IL策略在高速接物任务上的成功率与冲击抑制均超过特权RL教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速动态抓取需要同时完成拦截、速度匹配与跟随缓冲，RL需在保证可靠拦截抓取的同时调节敏感的接触过渡，学习困难。即使存在特权状态RL教师，其示范对可部署IL学生也不理想：教师失败导致任务覆盖不足，而接触前运动的微小变化会造成撞击与抓取结果的巨大差异，因此需要一种面向结果敏感性的示范构建方法。

Method: 以干预式结果敏感性分析定义OSW（接触前时段中对结果影响放大的状态区域）；提出Outcome-Sensitive Motion Search，学习任务条件下的成功OSW动作流形，并在流形上执行局部测地搜索，对教师成功rollout进行精修、并对教师失败的任务条件进行修复；随后在校准的IL学生动作误差模型下做完整rollout验证，仅保留成功执行作为示范，再训练IL策略。

Result: 仿真实验中，方法能有效修复教师失败的任务条件，所得IL策略在接住成功率与冲击缓解两方面均超过特权RL教师。

Conclusion: 将示范构建从"复制教师行为"转为"面向结果敏感性的目标化示范生成与验证"，可突破教师性能上限，为冲击感知的动态操作提供可部署的IL策略。证据目前限于仿真，尚缺真机验证。

Abstract: Skilled humans can catch fast-moving objects softly by coordinating interception, velocity matching, and follow-through to mitigate impact. Learning such impact-aware catching with reinforcement learning (RL), however, is challenging, as the policy must achieve reliable interception and grasping while regulating the sensitive transition into contact. Moreover, even a capable privileged-state RL teacher may not provide ideal demonstrations for a deployable imitation-learning (IL) student: teacher failures limit task coverage, while small variations in pre-contact motion can produce substantially different impact and grasping outcomes. We characterize this phenomenon through interventional outcome sensitivity and introduce the outcome-sensitive window (OSW) to guide targeted demonstration construction. Building on this formulation, we propose Outcome-Sensitive Motion Search, which learns a task-conditioned manifold of successful OSW motions and performs local geodesic search to refine successful teacher rollouts and repair task conditions where the teacher fails. We then validate candidate motions through complete rollouts under a calibrated IL-student action-error model and retain only successful executions as demonstrations. Extensive simulation experiments demonstrate that our method effectively repairs task conditions where the teacher fails and enables the resulting IL policy to outperform the privileged RL teacher in both catching success and impact mitigation.

</details>


### [139] [CAMP: Cooperative Arm-Hand Motion Planning in Constrained Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29021)
*Ziyuan Wang,Yunlong Shan,Fei Mo,Sichao Liu,David Navarro-Alarcon,Jia Pan,Kosta Jovanovic,Xin Jiang,Peng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 CAMP，一种协同臂-手运动规划器：以"可行手部纤维"刻画每个臂构型下的无碰撞手部构型，通过分层手部搜索 + 局部臂松弛构建候选轨迹，并用保端点的 via-point 运动基元（VMP）进行粗到细联合优化，在六个受限仿真任务中取得 84.2–98.5% 成功率，并有真机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限环境中灵巧操作需要臂与手的协同适配。分解式方法（先规划臂路径、再生成手部动作）会漏掉必须沿路径协同调整的可行解；直接在臂-手高维关节空间联合规划虽能捕捉耦合，却面临搜索空间剧增与非凸碰撞约束。核心动机是在保留耦合优势的同时控制搜索复杂度。

Method: 1) 提出"可行手部纤维"（feasible hand fibers）形式化：对每个臂构型刻画其无碰撞手部构型集合，显式建模臂-手耦合；2) 以此为基础设计 CAMP：先用分层手部搜索配合局部臂松弛（local arm relaxation）构造候选轨迹；3) 用保端点的 via-point 运动基元（VMP）紧凑表示候选轨迹；4) 在 VMP 参数空间做粗到细（coarse-to-fine）联合优化。

Result: 在六个受限仿真任务上，规划成功率达 84.2–98.5%，在效率相当的前提下优于对比规划器。消融实验验证了臂松弛、VMP 表示与粗到细优化三者的贡献。真机实验在受限操作任务上验证了 CAMP 的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过可行手部纤维的形式化与 VMP 参数化联合优化，CAMP 在受限环境下实现了高成功率且高效的臂-手协同规划，兼顾了分解方法的效率与联合规划对耦合的捕捉能力，并具备真机迁移能力。

Abstract: Coordinated arm-hand motion planning is fundamental to dexterous robotic manipulation in complex and constrained environments. A straightforward solution is to decompose the problem into separate arm path planning and hand motion generation; however, this poses a dilemma: decomposition can miss feasible solutions that require coordinated arm-hand adaptation along the path. Alternatively, directly planning in the high-dimensional joint arm-hand configuration space captures such coupling but faces a substantially enlarged search space and nonconvex collision constraints. To characterize this coupling, we formulate feasible hand fibers that capture collision-free hand configurations for each arm configuration. Based on this formulation, we propose CAMP, a high-success and efficient cooperative arm-hand motion planner for constrained environments. CAMP constructs candidate trajectories through layered hand search with local arm relaxation, then compactly represents them using endpoint-preserving via-point movement primitives (VMPs) for coarse-to-fine joint optimization. Across six constrained simulation tasks, CAMP achieves 84.2-98.5% planning success, outperforming alternative planners with competitive efficiency. Ablation studies verify the contributions of arm relaxation, VMP representation, and coarse-to-fine optimization, while real-robot experiments demonstrate CAMP on constrained manipulation tasks. The project website is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [140] [Simple Torque-Observation Alignment for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Grasping with a Direct-Drive Gripper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29031)
*Doyoung Kim,Edgar Lee,Hyeonsun Park,Chunghyeon Lee,Chihyun Han,Uisu Hwang,Seokhwan Jeong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向直驱电机的力矩观测对齐方法：用测力计标定力矩常数K_tau消除尺度失配，用delta_tau(t)=tau(t)-tau(t-1)消除常量偏置，并注入实测高斯噪声；仿真训练教师-学生抓取策略后零样本部署到多指直驱夹爪，9个ID物体上达100%抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中力矩观测的仿真与实测存在尺度、偏置和噪声三重域差，直接迁移策略会因观测失配而失效；现有工作多依赖昂贵的外部力矩传感器或复杂域随机化，缺少针对直驱电机电流-力矩线性特性的简洁对齐方案。

Method: 1) 测力计标定得到电机型号特定的力矩常数K_tau*，校正仿真与真实力矩的尺度失配；2) 在两个域均以差分力矩delta_tau(t)=tau(t)-tau(t-1)作为观测，替代带域相关偏置的绝对力矩tau(t)，从而消除常量偏置；3) 从测力计实测数据中提取高斯噪声，在学习过程中注入。整体在仿真中训练教师-学生抓取策略，学生仅用关节位置与力矩差分做本体感觉抓取，蒸馏后零样本部署于多指直驱夹爪。

Result: 在九个分布内（ID）物体上与多种对齐变体做消融对比，所提方法取得100%抓取成功率，验证了尺度校正、差分观测与噪声注入三项措施对真实力矩观测失配的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 针对直驱执行器的力矩观测对齐可显著提升零样本sim-to-real策略迁移的鲁棒性，方法简单、仅需一次测力计标定，可推广至其他直驱机械臂/夹爪平台。

Abstract: Torque observations in reinforcement learning remain challenging because simulated and measured torque differ in scale, offset, and noise. In this paper, we propose a simple torque observation alignment method for robots with direct-drive (DD) actuators, in which motor current maps linearly to joint torque through a motor-type-specific torque constant K_tau. First, dynamometer calibration identifies K_tau* and corrects the scale mismatch between simulated and real torque. Second, the method uses delta_tau(t) = tau(t) - tau(t-1) as the observation in both domains to eliminate the constant offset instead of using the direct torque tau(t), which carries a domain-dependent bias. Third, Gaussian noise obtained from the dynamometer measurement data is injected during the learning process. To validate the proposed method, we train a teacher-student grasping policy entirely in simulation and deploy the distilled student on a multifingered DD gripper. The deployed policy performs proprioceptive grasping using only joint positions and torque differences. We conduct an ablation study comparing the proposed method with alternative alignment variants on nine in-distribution (ID) objects. The proposed method achieves 100% grasp success. These results demonstrate that the proposed alignment method improves the robustness of zero-shot policy transfer on the DD gripper against real-world torque-observation mismatches.

</details>


### [141] [Design and Evaluation of LLM Chaining-Based Task Planning for General Purpose Service Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29043)
*Lucas Da Mota Bruno,Jiahao Sim,Yoshinobu Hagiwara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LLM链式（两阶段）架构，将GPSR指令分类与动作生成分离，缩短约45%提示长度，在多模型和HSR真机上验证规划提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPSR任务需机器人理解多样自然语言指令并生成多步动作序列；传统Single Prompt方法存在上下文膨胀与"Lost in the Middle"问题，导致规划不可靠。

Method: LLM chaining架构：将指令分类与动作生成拆分为两个专用阶段，降低单次推理提示长度约45%；在3个语言模型（本地开源+云端前沿）上用100条随机生成的GPSR指令评测，并在Toyota HSR真机上做执行实验。

Result: 所有模型上规划一致优于SP，本地模型提升最高达+37个百分点；真机10个任务中6个成功完成，执行层失败是主要剩余瓶颈。

Conclusion: 分阶段提示链可稳定提升LLM任务规划可靠性，但规划成功不等于任务完成，执行层才是当前主要瓶颈。

Abstract: General Purpose Service Robot (GPSR) tasks, as defined in the RoboCup@Home benchmark, require robots to interpret diverse natural language commands and generate multi-step action sequences in real home environments. Conventional Single Prompt (SP) approaches suffer from context bloat and the "Lost in the Middle" phenomenon, leading to unreliable task planning. We propose an LLM chaining architecture that separates instruction classification and action generation into two specialized stages, reducing per-inference prompt length by approximately 45% while improving planning consistency. We evaluate our method using 100 randomly generated GPSR commands across three language models spanning local open-source and frontier cloud deployment contexts. Results show consistent planning improvements over SP across all models, with gains of up to +37 percentage points on local models. Further, real-robot execution experiments on the Toyota Human Support Robot (HSR) reveal that planning success alone does not guarantee task completion, with 6 of 10 tasks completing successfully and execution-layer failures identified as the primary remaining bottleneck.

</details>


### [142] [DA-GRD: Decision-Aware Grasp-Relevant Disambiguation for tactile recovery under perception-to-execution mismatches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29065)
*Haoran Wang,Yuteng Sun,Yuanjie Li,Ruofei Bai,Meng Yee,Chuah,Wenyu Liang,Jun Li,Wei-Yun Yau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DA-GRD：在视觉-执行失配下，仅用稀疏触觉交互，通过加权平面位姿信念与决策感知的探针选择，恢复任务相关的可执行抓取，无需完整物体重定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械臂抓取中，从视觉感知生成的抓取位姿在物体移动后会失效（空间陈旧），而执行阶段往往缺乏进一步视觉观测。核心问题在于：是否必须完整重定位物体才能恢复可执行抓取，还是只需消除足以支持共同可行抓取的假设。

Method: 维护对物体构型（平面位姿）的加权假设信念；依据触觉探针消除假设、提升候选任务抓取一致性的能力来选择探针位置；当剩余假设支持共同可执行抓取时即停止，不追求完整位姿确定。在 MuJoCo 十类刚性物体上评测（平移至多 5 cm、偏航至 ±45°），并与 stale AnyGrasp、fix-scan 基线、SE(2) 信念改版 fix-scan 对比，另做六物体真机实验。

Result: 仿真：物理抬升成功率 84.7%（对比 stale AnyGrasp 9.1%、fix-scan 21.2%、SE(2) 信念版 63.7%），任务条件成功率 57.3%；平均 4.13 次触觉探针，较固定 15 探针基线减少 72.5%。真机：六物体抬升成功率 71.7%、任务条件成功率 38.3%，平均 4.20 探针。

Conclusion: 在视觉关闭条件下，稀疏触觉交互即可恢复任务相关抓取，且无需完整物体定位；以"任务可执行性"为停止准则的决策感知消歧显著优于固定扫描与纯位姿信念基线。

Abstract: Grasping is a fundamental robotic capability that bridges perception and physical task execution. This paper studies grasp pose recovery under a perception-to-execution mismatch, where a grasp generated from visual perception may become spatially stale if the object moves before execution, using only sparse tactile interactions and no further visual observations. We propose DA-GRD, Decision-Aware Grasp-Relevant Disambiguation, which maintains a weighted planar belief over possible object configurations and selects tactile probes according to their ability to eliminate hypotheses and improve agreement among candidate task grasps. Rather than fully relocalizing the object, DA-GRD stops when the remaining hypotheses support a common executable grasp. In MuJoCo experiments on ten rigid objects with translations up to 5~cm and yaw perturbations up to $\pm45^\circ$, DA-GRD achieves an 84.7% physical lift success rate, compared with 9.1% for stale AnyGrasp, 21.2% for the original fix-scan baseline, and 63.7% for fix-scan method adapted with an SE(2) belief. DA-GRD also achieves a 57.3% Task conditioned Success rate. Across objects, it uses a success-average of 4.13 tactile probes over the ten per-object means, corresponding to a 72.5% reduction relative to the fixed 15-probe baselines. Real-world experiments on six objects achieve 71.7% physical lift success and 38.3% task-conditioned success with 4.20 probes on average. These results show that tactile sensing can recover task-relevant grasps under vision-off conditions with limited physical interaction, without requiring complete object localization.

</details>


### [143] [From Passive Execution to Active Exploration: Agentic Embodied Manipulation in Realistic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29091)
*Shilin Ma,Chubin Zhang,Xulong Bai,Zifeng Gao,Shiyi Zhang,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于智能体的主动探索框架，通过规划、感知、执行三模块协同，结合细粒度感知-执行交错策略，使机器人在部分可观测条件下主动获取任务信息并完成操作任务，在Find-and-Place任务上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身操作框架多采用被动执行范式，难以应对含文本语义线索、干扰物及初始不可见目标的真实场景，需要机器人主动与环境交互而非仅执行预定义指令。

Method: 构建智能体式主动探索框架，含三个协作模块：规划模块负责高层任务推理，感知模块负责视觉场景理解，执行模块负责底层操作；并引入细粒度感知-执行交错策略，将视觉反馈与技能执行紧耦合以提升探索鲁棒性。

Result: 在真实感Find-and-Place任务上评估，表明该方法在需主动发现目标物体后再操作的挑战性环境中有效。

Conclusion: 主动探索范式可突破被动执行局限，支持部分可观测下的语义引导操作，但摘要未提供定量指标、真实机器人平台细节与代码链接。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic systems have substantially enhanced the long-horizon capability of embodied manipulation. However, many existing frameworks still follow a passive execution paradigm, which limits their applicability to real-world scenarios involving textual semantic cues, distractors, and initially invisible targets. To bridge this gap, we propose an agent-based active exploration framework that enables robots to dynamically interact with the environment rather than merely execute predefined instructions. Specifically, our framework consists of three collaborative modules: a planning module for high-level task reasoning, a perception module for visual scene understanding, and an execution module for low-level manipulation. This design allows the robot to actively acquire task-relevant information, adapt its behavior based on environmental feedback, and complete manipulation tasks under partial observability. Furthermore, we introduce a fine-grained perception-execution interleaving strategy, which tightly couples visual feedback with skill execution to improve exploration robustness. We evaluate our method on a realistic Find-and-Place task, demonstrating its effectiveness in challenging environments where target objects must be actively discovered before manipulation.

</details>


### [144] [DAWN: Noise-Robust Quadruped Parkour via Depth-Denoising World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29092)
*Yohan Choi,Min-Jun Kim,Jin-Sung Kim,Yong-Jae Kim,Youn-Hee Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DAWN 将去噪与对比对齐直接嵌入世界模型，使四足机器人无需手工滤波即可从含噪深度实现零样本跑酷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉腿足运动方法训练时假设深度干净，部署时依赖人工调参的后处理滤波；滤波参数少公开、可复现性差，且噪声未处理时性能显著退化。本体感知输入的噪声鲁棒性已有研究，但深度感知的同类思路在腿足运动中基本缺失。

Method: 提出 DAWN 感知框架，通过两项改动把噪声鲁棒性嵌入世界模型：(1) 编码器输入含噪深度、重建目标保持干净深度，迫使模型隐式去噪；(2) 对含噪与干净深度的隐状态施加对比学习进行对齐。方法不绑定特定噪声模型，部署时无需按噪声分布手工调参，且相比现有世界模型方法不增加推理开销。

Result: 在 Unitree Go1 上不经任何滤波标定，仅依赖学习到的鲁棒表征实现零样本四足跑酷：上 18 cm 台阶、跨 70 cm 沟壑、攀 45 cm 高台，均直接使用原始深度观测。消融显示去噪与对比对齐分别在重建层面与表征层面互补，组合后增益可加。

Conclusion: 将鲁棒性内建于学习流程可消除对人工滤波的依赖，且不牺牲推理效率；该思路不依赖特定噪声模型，具备跨噪声分布泛化潜力。

Abstract: Vision-based legged locomotion methods assume clean depth at training time and rely on hand-tuned post-processing filters at deployment. However, filter parameters are rarely disclosed, hindering reproducibility, and performance degrades substantially when depth noise is left unaddressed. Building noise robustness directly into the learning pipeline would eliminate this dependency. While such robustness has been explored for proprioceptive inputs, analogous approaches for depth perception remain largely absent in legged locomotion. We propose DAWN (Denoising and Alignment in World models for Noise-robustness), a noise-robust perception framework for legged locomotion, which builds noise robustness directly into a world model via two modifications: (1) feeding noisy depth to the encoder while keeping clean depth as the reconstruction target, forcing the model to implicitly denoise its input; and (2) applying contrastive learning to align the latent states of noisy and clean depth. Importantly, DAWN is not tied to a specific noise model, requiring no manual tuning to the noise distribution at deployment. Furthermore, it incurs no additional inference cost over existing world model-based methods. Without any manual filter calibration -- relying solely on the learned noise-robust representation -- DAWN achieves zero-shot quadruped parkour on a Unitree Go1: traversing stairs up to 18 cm, clearing gaps up to 70 cm, and mounting steps up to 45 cm from raw depth observations. Ablation studies show that denoising and contrastive alignment contribute at complementary levels -- reconstruction and representation, respectively -- and yield additive gains when combined. Videos and code are available at: this https URL

</details>


### [145] [A Support-Enhanced Granular-Jamming Gripper for RL-based Grasping with Continuum Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29093)
*Danyu Liu,Tianlin Zhang,Wei Chen,Wei Tang,Kecheng Qin,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向连续体机械臂的轻量支撑增强颗粒阻塞夹爪，通过膜材、颗粒、填充率与内部支撑结构设计建立抓取边界，并结合视觉反馈、腱驱动与气动控制，在随机化仿真中训练强化学习位姿控制器并部署到实物，完成模块化抓取-释放任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机械臂在狭窄空间运动灵巧，但其结构柔顺、迟滞与载荷相关变形会残留位置和姿态误差，影响与刚性夹爪的可靠接触。作者希望以自适应结构降低对高精度建模与定位控制的依赖，融合物理智能与具身智能。

Method: 设计轻量支撑增强颗粒阻塞夹爪：阻塞前保持柔顺，阻塞后形成指向连续体臂尖的直接载荷路径。系统设计膜材料、颗粒、填充比与内部支撑结构，并识别关于接触偏移和物体形状的几何依赖抓取边界。搭建包含连续体臂、颗粒阻塞夹爪、视觉反馈、腱驱动和气动控制的实物操作系统；在随机化仿真中训练强化学习到达控制器并迁移到实物。

Result: 给出了抓取边界随接触偏移与物体形状的变化规律，并成功将仿真训练的RL到达控制器部署于实物系统，展示了定位控制与接触级机械自适应在模块化抓取-释放任务中的互补效果。

Conclusion: 通过自适应结构降低建模与定位精度需求，本文探索了物理智能与具身智能集成的设计范式，为连续体机械臂末端抓取提供可部署方案。

Abstract: Continuum manipulators provide dexterous motion in confined spaces, but structural compliance, hysteresis, and load-dependent deformation leave residual position and orientation errors that can undermine reliable contact with rigid grippers. To address this limitation, this paper presents a lightweight support-enhanced granular-jamming gripper tailored to a continuum manipulator. The gripper maintains compliance before jamming while establishing a direct load path to the continuum manipulator tip after jamming. To improve its grasping performance, we systematically designed membrane materials, particles, filling ratios, and the internal support structure, and further identify geometry-dependent grasp boundaries with respect to contact offset and object shape. Building on these results, we construct a physical manipulation system integrating the continuum manipulator, granular-jamming gripper, visual feedback, tendon actuation, and pneumatic control. We then train a reinforcement-learning-based reaching controller in a randomized simulation and deploy it on the physical system, demonstrating how positioning control and contact level mechanical adaptation can complement each other in a modular grasp-and-release task. By introducing an adaptive structure that relaxes the need for highly accurate modeling and positioning control, this work explores a design paradigm that integrates physical and embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [146] [TRACE: Interactive Bi-Directional Tracing of Monochrome Cables Amid Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29103)
*Nidhya Shivakumar,Ethan Ransing,Josh Zhang,Shamak Gowda,Kevin Yang,Miles Hua,Anika Agrawal,Justin Yu,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRACE 通过双向线缆追踪与交互式感知原语（Divergence Push、Cluster Dilation）解决多根黑色线缆的遮挡、重叠与歧义交叉问题，将复杂场景下正确追踪的线缆长度比例从 ~60% (HANDLOOM 2.0) 提升至 ~90%，单根线缆平均计算耗时约 0.4 秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心、制造、建筑、家庭与手术等场景中，可变形线性物体（DLO，如线缆）的精确状态估计对操作安全与效率至关重要，但多根单色线缆在前景/背景杂乱环境下的遮挡、重叠与歧义交叉导致状态求解困难。

Method: 提出 Two-way Routing And Cable Estimation (TRACE)：将双向线缆追踪与交互式感知原语相结合，包含 Divergence Push（发散推挤）与 Cluster Dilation（簇膨胀），主动消解歧义交叉与重叠。

Result: 110 次物理实验表明，在最多 4 根线缆、40 个交叉点的复杂场景中，TRACE 将正确追踪线缆长度比例从最强基线 HANDLOOM 2.0 的约 60% 提升到约 90%，并优于 RT-DLO、Nano Banana Pro 与 ChatGPT 5.2；在 NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090 工作站上单次试验平均每根线缆计算时间 0.4 秒。

Conclusion: 双向追踪与交互式感知原语的结合可有效提升杂乱环境下多线缆状态估计的鲁棒性与准确性，并在真实物理实验中验证了性能与实时性。

Abstract: Accurate state estimation (tracing) of Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs) such as cables is a critical challenge for data centers, manufacturing, construction, homes, and surgery, where precise cable management directly impacts operational safety and efficiency. However, resolving the state of multiple monochrome cables amid foreground and background clutter poses challenges due to occlusions, overlap, and ambiguous crossings. We present Two-way Routing And Cable Estimation (TRACE), which combines bi-directional cable tracing with interactive perception primitives-Divergence Push and Cluster Dilation-to actively resolve ambiguities. Evaluation with 110 physical experiments suggests that TRACE can increase the percentage of cable length correctly traced in complex scenarios (with up to 4 cables and 40 crossings) from ~60% with the strongest prior method, HANDLOOM 2.0, to ~90%, outperforming RT-DLO, Nano Banana Pro, and ChatGPT 5.2 as well. For a trial run on a workstation with an NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090 GPU, the average computation time is 0.4 seconds per cable. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [147] [Modeling Load-, Velocity-, and Temperature-Dependent Transmission Errors of Cycloidal Drives for Industrial Robots Using Fourier Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29112)
*Christian J. E. Bauer,Valentin Kamm,Marcel Dzubba,Lukas Steinle,Armin Lechler,Alexander Verl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对工业机器人摆线针轮减速器传动误差建模，提出以傅里叶级数建模周期误差、幅值与相位随负载、速度和温度的多项式函数，并用单关节实验台测量识别谐波参数，最终以0.026 mrad的平均RMSE验证，可用于前馈补偿提升路径精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人因路径精度受限而很少用于加工任务，主要误差源为关节齿轮箱中的柔顺与传动误差；谐波减速器传动误差已有大量研究，而中重载机器人常用的摆线针轮减速器传动误差研究不足，因此需要建模并补偿以提升精度。

Method: 采用傅里叶级数建模传动误差的周期成分，将各次谐波的幅值与相位表示为负载扭矩、速度、温度的多项式函数；用代表单个机器人关节的实验台测量传动误差，结合测量数据将谐波频率与摆线针轮的机械特性对应，据此辨识多项式傅里叶级数模型参数。

Result: 与验证测量相比，所推导模型的平均均方根误差为0.026 mrad，精度较高。

Conclusion: 提出将模型输出用于前馈控制以补偿传动误差，从而提高工业机器人路径精度，为摆线针轮减速器误差建模与补偿提供可行方案。

Abstract: Industrial robots are rarely used for machining tasks due to their limited path accuracy. This accuracy is mainly limited by inaccuracies in the drive trains. Compliance and transmission errors occur in the joint gearboxes. While transmission errors have been extensively studied for strain wave gears, there is little research on these errors in cycloidal drives. This gearbox type is commonly used in industrial robots for medium to heavy payloads. It is proposed to model the mainly periodic transmission errors using a Fourier series where amplitude and phase are defined as a polynomial function of the main influence factors load-torque, velocity, and temperature. Measurements of the transmission errors were conducted using an experimental setup representing a single robot joint. In the evaluation of the measurement data, harmonic frequencies were related to mechanical properties of the cycloidal drive. These frequencies were used to identify the parameters of the polynomial Fourier series model. Compared to validation measurements, the derived model shows an average root mean square error of 0.026 mrad. It is proposed to use the output of the resulting model in a feedforward control approach to compensate the transmission errors and to increase the path accuracy of industrial robots.

</details>


### [148] [A Tendon-Driven Robotic Jellyfish with Constrained Soft Actuation and Depth Control via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29132)
*Jiarui Peng,Yutong Wu,Zelong Wang,Ping Deng,Xiaotian Zhang,Xian Chen,Kecheng Qin,Zhongyi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种腱驱动的仿水母软体机器人：柔性基底+离散约束的致动器实现150°弯曲且位移-弯曲近似线性，8个致动器由4个舵机驱动，配合强化学习控制器实现仿真与实机闭环深度调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体水下机器人虽具备顺应性与高效推进优势，但难以同时实现大变形、可重复致动与闭环控制；纯软致动器存在非线性、迟滞等问题，导致建模与控制困难。

Method: 设计"约束软致动"单元：柔性基底叠加离散机械约束，将弯曲限制为可控模式，使肌腱位移与弯曲角呈近似线性关系（最大150°）。机器人由8个致动器、4个舵机驱动，实现游动、姿态调整与自扶正；基于线性致动特性搭建强化学习控制器，用于深度闭环调节，并在仿真与实物实验中验证。

Result: 单致动器弯曲可达150°且位移-弯曲近似线性；样机实现稳定游动、姿态调节与自扶正；强化学习控制器在仿真与物理实验中均完成闭环定深控制。

Conclusion: 机械约束可在保持水母式顺应运动的同时提升软致动的可控性，为可机动、自主的水母型水下机器人提供了一条可行路径；但控制任务较单一（仅定深），缺乏复杂水下任务与长期自主性验证。

Abstract: Jellyfish-inspired robots offer a compliant and efficient approach to underwater locomotion, but achieving large deformation together with repeatable actuation and closed-loop control remains challenging. In this work, we present a tendon-driven robotic jellyfish with constrained soft actuation. Each actuator combines a flexible substrate with discrete constraints, enabling bending up to \(150^\circ\) with an approximately linear tendon displacement-bending relationship. Eight actuators driven by four servos allow the robot to perform stable swimming, attitude adjustment, and self-righting. Based on the linear actuation, a reinforcement-learning controller is further developed, enabling closed-loop depth regulation in both simulation and physical experiments. These results show that mechanical constraints can improve the controllability of soft actuation while preserving compliant jellyfish-like motion, providing a route toward manoeuvrable and autonomous jellyfish robots.

</details>


### [149] [OREN-X: Octree Residual Network for Real-Time Multi-Modal Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29157)
*Zhirui Dai,Qihao Qian,Dinh Minh Nguyen,Quan-Dung Pham,Kiana Bronder,Carlos Nieto-Granda,Yiyu Chen,Quan Nguyen,Nikolay Atanasov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OREN-X 提出以八叉树为统一数据结构的在线多模态建图方法，联合存储几何(SDF)、辐射与视觉语言特征，实现实时渲染与高压缩查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用长时程自主机器人需要同时维护几何、辐射和视觉语言三类环境信息以支持规划、重定位与开放词汇定位，但现有方法对每个模态分别表征与估计，导致内存与算力成倍增长，且丧失了跨模态互补的可能。

Method: 以三维八叉树作为共享索引与存储结构，构建统一多模态场，支持显式/隐式、完整/压缩形式的高效统一存储与检索；利用占用与辐射信息锐化SDF估计，采用GPU射线-八叉树遍历与八叉树查询实现实时渲染；用在线字典学习压缩视觉语言特征。

Result: 在Replica上实时建图（SDF 80+ fps，四模态全部30+ fps）；近表面SDF精度较单模态基线提升33%；平均开放词汇3D mIoU提升71%、平均精度提升61%；视觉语言特征压缩至逐顶点存储的1/3.7（约3.7倍压缩）且查询精度提高。

Conclusion: 统一多模态八叉树表示可带来跨模态协同，在保持实时性的同时显著提升几何与开放词汇语义建图性能，为长时程通用自主提供高效环境表征基础。

Abstract: To achieve general-purpose autonomy over long horizons, a robot needs to maintain spatial environment information that supports a variety of tasks: geometry for planning and control, radiance for rendering and relocalization, and vision-language features for open-vocabulary grounding. Existing methods represent and estimate each modality separately, multiplying memory and compute cost while forgoing potential synergy among the representations. We develop OREN-X, an online mapping method that uses an octree in 3D space as a shared data structure for indexing and storing a multi-modal field, capturing geometric, radiance, and vision-language information. OREN-X provides efficient unified storage and retrieval of these data in explicit/implicit and full/compressed form. Our unified representation yields cross-modality synergy: SDF estimates are sharpened by occupancy and radiance, while GPU-based ray-octree traversal and octree query enable real-time rendering. We also use online dictionary learning to compress the vision-language features, shrinking them 3.7x below full per-vertex storage while raising the query accuracy. On Replica, OREN-X maps in real time (80+ fps for SDF and 30+ fps for all four modalities), improves near-surface SDF accuracy by 33% over single-modality baselines, and improves mean open-vocabulary 3D mIoU by 71% and mean accuracy by 61% over the best prior method.

</details>


### [150] [HarnessPAI: An Evolving Harness for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29166)
*Xin Wang,Wenhao Wu,Menghao Zhang,Zhi Wang,Kun Shao,Jian Luan,Yang Li,Qing Li,Shangding Gu,Huichi Zhou,Shuqing Shi,Fei Ni,Shuo Lu,Weicheng Meng,Kang Li,Jin Wu,Kang Zhao,Shangmin Guo,Gen Li,Yongqiang Tang,Zhizhong Zhang,Yuan Xie,Heng Qu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HarnessPAI 提出一种模型与本体无关的"Harness"框架，把代码作为可执行、可演化的接口来组织底层动作原语：单次 rollout 内以固定程序开环引导与检查执行，跨 rollout 用执行反馈闭环修订程序、把失败蒸馏为可复用技能，在无需重训底层模型的情况下显著超越纯动作模型与 code-as-policy 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Physical AI 当前过度聚焦于动作模型（观测到低层控制的映射），而主流训练范式会侵蚀感知与推理能力，使强动作模型在场景扰动和长时程任务中依然脆弱。作者希望把感知、任务理解与推理、动作执行整合进一个统一、可验证、反馈驱动的系统，而非仅依赖更强的动作模型。

Method: HarnessPAI 是模型无关、本体无关的 Harness 框架，以代码作为可执行且可演化的接口，组织底层动作原语。它分离两个时间尺度：(1) 单次 rollout 内，在程序层面开环执行，由固定程序引导并检查执行；(2) 跨 rollout，闭环演化，用执行反馈修订程序，并将失败蒸馏为可复用技能。收敛后的程序还可作为廉价的专家数据采集器，用于微调底层模型。

Result: 在桌面机械臂、家用机器人、扫地机器人和足式行走智能体上，无需重训底层模型即超过纯动作模型与 code-as-policy 基线：LIBERO-PRO 上比 π0.5 提升 61.6 分，RoboCasa 原子任务上比 WorldDreamer 提升 27.2 分。程序选定后，rollout 执行无需在线高层 LLM 推理。用收敛程序采集的专家数据微调 π0.5，可在 LIBERO-PRO 上再提升 38.8 分成功率。

Conclusion: Physical AI 的前沿不仅取决于更强的动作模型，也取决于能把感知、任务理解与推理、动作执行整合为统一、可验证、反馈驱动系统的可执行 Harness。代码作为可演化接口为长时程鲁棒性提供了新的系统级路径。

Abstract: Physical AI aims to build embodied agents that perceive the world, understand and reason about it, and decide how to act. Yet the field has focused primarily on the last component: the action model that maps observations to low-level controls. The prevailing training recipe can erode the perceptual and reasoning capabilities needed for robust behavior, leaving even strong action models vulnerable to scene perturbations and long-horizon tasks. We introduce HarnessPAI, a model- and embodiment-agnostic Harness framework for Physical AI that treats code as the executable and evolvable interface that organizes the underlying action primitive. The framework separates two timescales: within a rollout, it executes open-loop at the program level, with a fixed program guiding and checking execution; across rollouts, it evolves closed-loop, using execution feedback to revise the program and distill failures into reusable skills. Across desktop robot arms, household robots, a robot vacuum, and a legged walking agent, HarnessPAI improves on both pure action models and code-as-policy baselines without retraining the underlying model: a 61.6-point gain over $\pi_{0.5}$ on LIBERO-PRO and a 27.2-point gain over WorldDreamer on RoboCasa atomic tasks. Once a program is selected, rollout execution requires no online high-level LLM deliberation. Beyond execution, the converged program is also a cheap and reliable expert-data collector, and fine-tuning $\pi_{0.5}$ on collected expert data lifts success rate on LIBERO-PRO by 38.8 points. Our results suggest that the frontier of Physical AI depends not only on stronger action models, but also on executable harnesses that integrate perception, task understanding and reasoning, and action execution into a unified, verifiable, and feedback-driven system. Website: this https URL

</details>


### [151] [Representation World Model: Learning States, Transition and Executable Plans in Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29171)
*Yijun Yuan,Weicheng Zheng,Weibang Wang,Minghui Qin,Chang Sun,Junhao Huang,Kenan Li,Anmin Liu,Yicheng Yao,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RWM 将状态、转移与可执行规划直接学习在表征空间中：通过逆动力学监督沿端点表征构建的隐路径，把规划嵌入表征几何，推理时直接构造当前到目标的隐路径并用逆动力学还原动作，无需递归 rollout 或动作空间搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型通常将隐表征与显式动力学模型联合学习，并依赖搜索、优化或策略预测完成规划，推理开销大且规划与表征割裂。作者希望让规划本身成为表征空间几何的一部分，从而绕开递归 rollout 与动作空间搜索。

Method: 学习状态、转移与可执行规划均直接在表征空间进行。核心是用逆动力学监督，沿着由端点表征（当前与目标）构造的隐路径局部施加约束，迫使该路径保留任务相关的状态与转移信息，从而塑造表征几何。推理阶段直接在当前与目标表征之间构建隐路径，再由逆动力学恢复对应动作，不进行递归展开或动作空间搜索。

Result: 在连续控制基准上验证了 RWM 用于直接规划的有效性；在机器人操作任务上的结果进一步显示其可扩展到更复杂的具身控制任务。

Conclusion: 直接在表征空间中进行规划，是传统世界模型规划范式（搜索/优化/策略预测+rollout）的有前景替代方案，且具备向复杂具身控制扩展的潜力。

Abstract: We propose the Representation World Model (RWM), which learns states, transitions, and executable plans directly in representation space. Unlike existing world models that typically learn latent representations together with explicit dynamics models and perform planning through search, optimization, or policy-based prediction, RWM directly incorporates planning into the learned representation geometry. RWM learns the representation geometry by applying inverse-dynamics supervision locally along latent paths constructed from endpoint representations, requiring these paths to preserve task-relevant state and transition information. At inference, planning is performed by directly constructing a latent path between the current and goal representations, with inverse dynamics used to recover the corresponding actions, without recursive rollouts or action-space search. Experiments on continuous-control benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of RWM for direct planning, while results on robotic manipulation further show its potential to extend to more complex embodied control tasks. These results suggest that planning directly in representation space provides a promising alternative to conventional world-model planning.

</details>


### [152] [Anthropomimetic Soft Robotic Forearm with Independently Articulated Carpal Bones Enabling Human-Like Adaptive Stiffness Modulability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29176)
*Yoshinobu Obata,Yinlai Jiang,Hiroshi Yokoi,Shunta Togo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究构建了解剖精确的拟人化软体机器人前臂（8块可动腕骨＋韧带＋22块驱动肌肉＋柔性指尖），在三种骨骼构型与四种肌肉激活模式下测量腕关节刚度椭圆，发现腕骨形态是人体腕部刚度可调性的主导因素，并给出仿人机器人腕部设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类腕关节刚度各向异性可通过肌肉共收缩主动调节，对稳定操作至关重要，但其形态学（骨骼构型）决定因素尚不明确。作者希望厘清腕骨形态对刚度调节的贡献，并为仿人机器人腕部提供设计依据。

Method: 搭建解剖学精确的拟人化软体机器人前臂：8块独立可动腕骨由韧带互连、22条驱动肌肉、柔性指尖。在三种骨骼构型（解剖正常腕骨、近排腕骨融合、几何椭球骨架）与四种肌肉激活模式（含手指肌、腕肌单独与协同激活）下测量腕关节刚度椭圆，并分析腕骨运动学耦合模式。

Result: 指肌激活时刚度椭圆在掷镖运动（DTM）方向呈低刚度，腕肌＋指肌同时激活则转为高刚度，与已有人体测量一致，说明精确解剖复现可重现类人刚度可调性。融合近排腕骨消除了指肌激活下的DTM低刚度；椭球骨架在所有条件下刚度椭圆重定向能力差。腕骨运动分析显示腕肌与指肌在近排腕骨处存在显著反向耦合，中腕关节呈一致但非显著趋势。

Conclusion: 腕骨形态在人体腕部刚度调制中起主导作用，研究为仿人机器人腕部设计提供了可操作的设计原则。

Abstract: The human wrist exhibits adaptive stiffness modulability: joint stiffness anisotropy can be actively regulated through muscle co-contraction. This functionality is essential for stable manipulation, yet the underlying morphological factors remain unclear. To identify these factors, we developed an anatomically accurate anthropomimetic soft robotic forearm comprising eight independently movable carpal bones interconnected by ligaments, 22 actuated muscles, and compliant fingertips. We measured wrist joint stiffness under four muscle activation patterns across three skeletal configurations: anatomically normal carpal bones, a fused proximal carpal row, and a geometric ellipsoidal skeleton. The stiffness ellipse exhibited low stiffness along the dart-throwing motion (DTM) direction when finger muscles were activated, but high stiffness along the same direction when wrist and finger muscles were activated simultaneously. These results agree with previously reported human measurements, demonstrating that precise anatomical replication reproduces human-like stiffness modulability. Fusing the proximal carpal row eliminated the low DTM-direction stiffness under finger muscle activation, while the geometric ellipsoidal skeleton showed poor stiffness ellipse reorientation across all conditions. Carpal bone motion analysis revealed significantly opposing coupling patterns between wrist and finger muscles at the proximal carpal row, accompanied by a consistent but non-significant trend at the midcarpal joint, providing a mechanical explanation for this modulation. These findings demonstrate that carpal bone morphology plays a dominant role in human wrist stiffness modulation and provide design principles for humanoid robot wrists.

</details>


### [153] [Continuous Online Fault Detection for Mobile Robots via Adaptive Edge Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29194)
*Jordan Levy,Nicolas Verstaevel,Vincent Talon,Benoit Gaudou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Teacher-Student 蒸馏框架：离线基础模型 TSPulse 生成伪标签，轻量 MiniRocket Student 结合递归最小二乘实现边缘端实时故障检测，并用不确定性引导的主动学习减少人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人需要在受限边缘硬件上实现鲁棒、可持续适应的实时故障检测，但深度时序模型计算开销大，难以高频机载执行；同时无监督异常检测缺乏标签且面临域偏移。

Method: 离线阶段用基础模型 TSPulse 在无标签时序（叠加故障注入增强）上生成伪标签；用带递归最小二乘估计器的轻量 MiniRocket 学生模型逼近教师决策边界，实现在线实时推理与在线自适应；并引入不确定性引导的主动学习，仅在遇到新故障分布时请求稀疏人工介入。

Result: 在 TSB-AD 基准与一台实体移动机器人上验证：学生模型 CPU 推理延迟 4.30 ms；真实域偏移下在线自适应可从未见机械退化中恢复，VUS-PR 从 0.26 提升至 0.75，且无灾难性遗忘；主动学习策略有效降低操作者认知负荷。

Conclusion: 离线到在线蒸馏可将 SOTA 异常检测部署到资源受限机器人平台，兼顾实时性、在线适应性与低人工干预。

Abstract: Mobile robots require robust, real-time fault detection capable of continuous adaptation on constrained edge hardware. While deep time-series models excel at unsupervised anomaly detection, their computational cost prohibits high-frequency onboard execution. This paper bridges this gap via a Teacher-Student distillation framework. An offline foundation model (TSPulse) generates pseudo-labels from unlabeled time series augmented with fault injections. A lightweight MiniRocket Student, adapted with a Recursive Least Squares estimator, approximates this complex decision boundary to execute real-time inference onboard. Evaluations on the TSB-AD benchmark and a physical mobile robot demonstrate the Student achieves a 4.30 ms CPU inference latency. During real-world domain shifts, online adaptation enables the Student to recover from unseen mechanical degradation, improving VUS-PR scores from 0.26 to 0.75 without catastrophic forgetting. Crucially, an uncertainty-guided active learning strategy minimizes operator cognitive load, requesting sparse interventions only when encountering novel fault distributions. These results validate the deployment of state-of-the-art anomaly detection on resource-constrained robotics through offline-to-online distillation.

</details>


### [154] [Assessing the Impact of Fleet Size on Crowdsourced Mapping Using a Dissimilarity Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29198)
*Marie-Ngoïe Badibanga Kalenda,Philippe Bonnifait,Marie-Anne Mittet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于仿真的众包交通标志维护评估框架，用GOSPAM差异性度量综合定位误差与检测性能（FP/FN），分析车队规模（5–50辆）对众包建图质量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精地图是ADAS/自动驾驶（如ISA）的关键，传统测绘维护成本高、难扩展；众包车队是潜在替代方案，但"参与车辆数—地图质量"的关系尚不清楚。

Method: 构建多车观测仿真：加入代表性传感器噪声、检测误差与语义识别不确定性；通过空间聚类与语义过滤聚合多车观测以估计标志位置。仿真轨迹由实车在含真值标志的区域采集得到。采用GOSPAM（Generalized Optimal SubPattern Assignment for Maps）度量，将定位误差与FP/FN统一纳入评价，并与标准指标对比。车队规模设为5–50辆。

Result: GOSPAM能有效评估众包建图质量，可量化首批车辆带来的贡献以及车辆规模增大后的边际改进。

Conclusion: 该仿真框架与GOSPAM为众包地图维护提供了可扩展的质量评估手段，有助于理解车队规模与地图精度/召回之间的关系。

Abstract: Accurate digital maps are essential for Advanced Driver Assistance Systems (ADAS) or Autonomous Driving (AD), providing critical information such as road geometry, traffic signs and speed limits required by safety functions including Intelligent Speed Assistance (ISA). Maintaining these map layers using traditional surveying methods is costly and difficult to scale. Crowdsourced approaches based on fleets provide a promising alternative for continuously validating and updating map information. However, the relationship between the number of contributing vehicles and the quality of the resulting map remains poorly understood. To address this gap, this paper presents a simulation-based framework for evaluating crowdsourced traffic sign maintenance using a dissimilarity measure called GOSPAM (Generalized Optimal SubPattern Assignment for Maps), which combines localization errors with detection performance by accounting for False Positives (FP) and False Negatives (FN). The proposed system models multivehicle observations with representative sensor noise, detection errors, and semantic recognition uncertainties. Observations from multiple vehicles are aggregated using spatial clustering and semantic filtering to estimate traffic sign locations. Using simulated trajectories generated from data carried out by an experimental vehicle in an area containing ground-truth traffic signs, we assess the influence of fleet size on the performance of crowdsourced mapping. The number of vehicles ranges from 5 to 50, and performance is analyzed using standard evaluation metrics which are compared to the GOSPAM . The results show that GOSPAM can be used to effectively assess the quality of crowdsourced mapping, such as the contributions made by the first vehicles or the improvements made by numerous vehicles.

</details>


### [155] [AdaHVLA: Adaptive Harnesses for Long-Horizon Vision-Language-Action Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29204)
*Junyi Tang,Jie Peng,Zezhen Ding,Yuan Shen,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AdaHVLA：一种自适应任务框架（harness），通过机器人经验迭代精炼代码化的协调策略，桥接 VLA 的局部控制能力与长时程任务所需的持久记忆与规划，并借助有状态修正图跨任务/环境持续适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在局部控制与指令跟随上表现强，但在需要持久记忆与规划的长时程任务中能力不足；而任务框架（harness）能保留任务历史与进度、提供持久上下文，两者能力互补但未有效结合，且现有框架缺乏基于执行证据的自适应精炼机制。

Method: AdaHVLA 采用解耦的多智能体适配流程，将证据分析、框架修订与行为评估分离到不同工作上下文；以可检验的协调假设驱动修订，并通过后续 rollout 验证假设的预期效果；构建有状态修正图，将执行证据、假设、修订与观测效果关联，保留备选框架与适配记忆，支持跨重复尝试与跨任务/环境的持续适配，从而精炼代码化的协调策略。

Result: 仿真中，NaVILA-LH 平均测试成功率从 22.5% 提升至最高 57.5%；在三个 VLA 骨干上，操作任务测试成功率相比初始框架提升最多 30.8 个百分点。真实世界部署进一步表明，适配后的策略能支持跨任务阶段的稳定执行。

Conclusion: 将自适应任务框架与 VLA 执行相结合，可有效弥补 VLA 在长时程记忆与规划上的短板，且修正图机制带来的经验积累可在多任务与多环境中持续生效；方法在仿真与真实部署中均有一致收益，但真实世界部分仅作示意性验证，定量证据仍以仿真为主。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models offer strong local control and instruction following but often struggle with long-horizon tasks requiring persistent memory and planning. Task harnesses provide persistent context for agent reasoning by retaining task history and tracking progress across execution stages. To bring these complementary capabilities together, we introduce AdaHVLA, an adaptive harness that refines code-based coordination policies through robot experience to better align agent reasoning and memory with VLA execution. Its decoupled multiagent adaptation process separates evidence analysis, harness revision, and behavioral assessment into distinct working contexts, using testable coordination hypotheses to guide revisions and subsequent rollouts to assess their predicted effects. A stateful revision graph links execution evidence, hypotheses, revisions, and observed effects, preserving alternative harnesses and adaptation memory to guide refinement across repeated attempts and continued adaptation across tasks and environments. In simulation, AdaHVLA raises mean test success on NaVILA-LH from 22.5\% to as high as 57.5\% and improves manipulation test success across three VLA backbones by up to 30.8 percentage points over the initial harness. Real-world deployment further illustrates how the adapted policies support stable execution across task stages.

</details>


### [156] [ADM-Planner: LLM-Guided Long-Horizon Planning for Mobile Manipulators with Attention-Enhanced Dynamic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29212)
*Jiaping Xiao,Pingyuan Ji,Mir Feroskhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ADM-Planner 用注意力增强的动态记忆分离持久工作区知识与物体状态，并通过有界检索器只暴露影响下一步决策的条目，实现长时程移动操作规划中状态的选择性维护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 可将移动操作目标分解为长动作序列，但固定场景描述会因物体被发现、移动或完成而失效；而保留全部观测又会产生冗余、冲突的膨胀历史。需要解决"上下文陈旧"与"历史膨胀"之间的张力。

Method: 提出 LLM 引导规划框架 ADM-Planner 与注意力增强动态记忆（ADM）：将持久工作区知识与物体为中心的状��相分离；异步观测与动作结果更新状态；有界检索器仅暴露可影响下一决策的条目；当更新使剩余计划失效时触发 LLM 重规划。

Result: 1,500 个任务-模拟器回合中，14 容器噪声动态设置下 ADM 达 100% 全任务成功率，静态记忆 62%、未过滤动态记忆 97%，且上下文规模代理较后者降低 95.8%。GPT-5 Mini 实机规划六回合中两种动态记忆均完成任务，ADM 输入 token 减少 14.4%，平均规划调用从 7.0 降至 6.0。60 次 PyBullet 试验中 ADM 保持 100% 成功，静态记忆 50%。移动操作平台在室内外物理实验中完成多类任务，并能纳入执行开始后出现的目��。

Conclusion: 在变化环境中进行长时程规划，应采用 ADM 的选择性状态维护，而非仅依赖提示历史。

Abstract: Large language models can decompose mobile-manipulation goals into long action sequences, but the resulting plans remain reliable only while their world context is current. A fixed scene description becomes stale when objects are discovered, moved, or completed while retaining every observation instead produces a growing history with redundant and conflicting state. To resolve this tension, we present an LLM-guided planning framework ADM-Planner with attention-enhanced dynamic memory (ADM). Persistent workspace knowledge is separated from object-centric state, asynchronous observations and action outcomes update that state, and a bounded retriever exposes only the entries that can affect the next decision. The LLM replans when an update invalidates the remaining plan. Across 1,500 task-simulator episodes, the proposed ADM achieved 100% full-task success in the 14-container noisy dynamic setting, compared with 62% for static memory and 97% for unfiltered dynamic memory, while reducing the context-size proxy by 95.8% relative to the latter. In a six-episode live GPT-5 Mini planner, both dynamic memory variants completed every mission, while ADM reduced provider-reported input tokens by 14.4% and mean planner calls from 7.0 to 6.0. A separate 60-trial PyBullet study retained 100% success for ADM, compared with 50% for static memory. Finally, the mobile manipulator with ADM-Planner completed various missions in indoor and outdoor physical experiments while incorporating targets revealed after execution began. The results show that selective state maintenance with ADM, rather than prompt history alone, is a practical basis for long-horizon planning in changing environments. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [157] [Dense-Joint-Based Obstacle-Aided Locomotion with a Joint-Repositionable Snake Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29261)
*Kyosuke Minomo,Ryo Takahashi,Kotaro Yasui,Yasutaka Nakashima,Motoji Yamamoto,Ayato Kanada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过解耦驱动器与关节的可重构机构搭建高密度与低密度两种蛇形机器人，在变障碍直径环境下对比推进性能，证明高关节密度可抑制接触力突变、降低功耗并实现连续稳定推进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统刚性连杆蛇形机器人关节密度低，与障碍物仅在离散点接触，易出现停滞或卡死，而生物蛇可连续自适应环境。作者希望验证关节密度是否是影响障碍辅助运动性能的关键因素。

Method: 采用关节位置可重构的蛇形机器人机构，将驱动器与关节解耦以实现高密度结构；设计总长度相同但关节密度不同（高、低两档）的两个实验模型，在障碍直径变化的障碍环境中开展对比推进实验，并测量接触反力与功耗。

Result: 高密度模型显著抑制了低密度模型中引起停滞的反力突变；通过维持平滑的接触点序列，高密度构型降低了功耗，并实现稳定连续的推进，环境适应性提升。

Conclusion: 高关节密度是提升蛇形机器人在复杂地形中环境适应性的关键设计因素，为障碍辅助运动机器人机构设计提供了实验依据。

Abstract: Obstacle-aided locomotion is a fundamental capability for snake robots to traverse complex environments. However, conventional rigid-link snake robots often suffer from stagnation or jamming caused by their low joint density (i.e., the number of joints per unit length). This results in discontinuous contact with obstacles, unlike the continuous adaptation of biological snakes. To investigate the effect of joint density on obstacle-aided locomotion performance, we utilized a joint-repositionable snake robot mechanism that decouples actuators from joints, enabling a high-density architecture. We developed two experimental models with identical total lengths but different joint densities (high-density and low-density) and conducted comparative propulsion experiments in obstacle environments with varying obstacle diameters. The experimental results demonstrate that the high-density model substantially suppresses the abrupt shifts in reaction forces that cause stagnation in the low-density model. By maintaining smooth contact points, the high-density configuration reduces power consumption and achieves stable, continuous propulsion. These results highlight high joint density as a key factor in improving the environmental adaptability of snake robots in complex terrains.

</details>


### [158] [EgoSpeedUp: Transferring Human Manipulation Tempo to Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29310)
*Hanbit Oh,Yukiyasu Domae,Takuma Yagi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoSpeedUp：以人类演示作为机器人模仿学习的时间监督，对齐操作阶段并估计相对执行节奏，通过重定时（retiming）机器人演示实现阶段化加速，再以标准行为克隆训练，使机器人在保留可执行行为的同时获得人类节奏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略会继承机器人演示的保守执行节奏（tempo），导致任务完成时间过长。现有加速方法主要依赖机器人侧信息或预设的加速因子集合，缺乏"每个操作阶段应当多快"的任务适宜参考量。

Method: 以同一任务的人类演示作为时间监督源：给定慢速机器人演示和人类演示，先将二者对应的操作阶段对齐，再从多个人类演示中估计各阶段的相对执行节奏，并将该阶段化节奏迁移到机器人演示上（时间重定时），最后用重定时后的演示做标准行为克隆。

Result: 在两个真实世界操作任务上，任务成功率平均提升 25 个百分点，同时成功执行时间减少 36.5%。

Conclusion: 人类操作节奏可作为有效的时间参考，用于学习更快且更可靠的机器人策略，无需依赖机器人侧启发式或预设加速因子。

Abstract: Robot manipulation policies trained through imitation learning inherit not only the demonstrated behavior but also the conservative execution tempo of robot demonstrations. Existing acceleration approaches can execute faster than the original demonstrations, but determine the appropriate acceleration primarily from robot-side information or a predefined set of tempo factors, leaving open how to obtain a task-appropriate reference for how fast each manipulation phase should progress. We introduce EgoSpeedUp, a framework that uses human manipulation as temporal supervision for robot imitation learning. Our key insight is that human demonstrations naturally reveal task-appropriate, phase-wise manipulation tempo. Given slow robot demonstrations and human demonstrations of the same task, EgoSpeedUp aligns corresponding manipulation phases, estimates their relative execution tempos from multiple human demonstrations, and transfers the resulting phase-wise tempo by retiming the robot demonstrations. The retimed demonstrations are then used for standard behavior cloning, allowing the robot to retain its executable manipulation behavior while learning to perform it at a human-informed tempo. Across two real-world manipulation tasks, EgoSpeedUp improves the task success rate by an average of 25 percentage points (pp) while reducing successful execution time by 36.5%. These results demonstrate that human manipulation tempo provides an effective temporal reference for learning faster and more reliable robot policies.

</details>


### [159] [A Simple Gripper Interface for Simulator-Agnostic Cloth Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29340)
*Abhilash Nayak,Franco Coltraro,Maria Alberich-Carramiñana,Carme Torras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向布料操作的简化抓取模型：仅用位置约束替代接触与摩擦建模，用轴对齐盒（捏取）与方锥体（点抓取）表示抓取体积，配合"挤压"步骤渐进收敛布料粒子，并在约束式不可拉伸布料模拟器中实现，同一末端轨迹迁移到真实机械臂完成布料折叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布料抓取涉及复杂的接触与摩擦建模，导致机器人控制方法难以部署。作者希望构建一个鲁棒、快速、易实现且与仿真/真机通用的抓取模型，从而简化布料操作的控制与仿真实现。

Method: 用位姿、夹爪状态和附加抓取体积描述夹爪；采用轴对齐包围盒模拟捏取、方锥体模拟点抓取。夹爪闭合时选取体积内的离散布料粒子并记录于局部坐标系，随后随夹爪运动被搬运；加入挤压步骤使粒子向抓取中心渐进移动，避免闭合瞬间的瞬时位移。模型仅依赖离散布料位置与位置约束机制，可嵌入任意模拟器；在基于约束的不可拉伸布料模拟器中，以移动位置等式约束耦合拉伸、剪切、碰撞与桌面接触投影步骤实现抓取。

Result: 该模型在仿真中与约束式不可拉伸布料模拟器结合可行，实现抓取与折叠操作；将相同抓取轨迹应用于真实机械臂折叠真实布料，验证了仿真到真机的可迁移性，并展示了简化抓取模型的真实感与实用性。

Conclusion: 以位置约束替代接触/摩擦建模的简化抓取模型可实现快速、鲁棒、易部署的布料操作，并可作为仿真与真实布料操作之间的桥梁；其局限在于未显式建模接触与摩擦，对强依赖摩擦的精细操作可能不足。

Abstract: This paper presents a grasping model for cloth manipulation specifically tailored to ease the deployment of robotic control methods. The model is robust, fast and easy to implement avoiding at the same time contact and friction considerations between the gripper and the cloth in favor of simple positional constraints. The gripper is described by its pose, jaw state, and an attached grasping volume. Two kinds of grasping volumes are considered: an axis-aligned box to simulate a pinch grasping and a square pyramidal volume to simulate point grasping. When the gripper closes, the discrete cloth positions lying inside this volume are selected, stored in the local gripper frame, and then transported with the gripper motion. A simple squeezing step is also included to progressively move the selected cloth positions toward the center of the grasping region, avoiding an instantaneous displacement at closure. The model can be used in any simulator as it only requires access to discrete cloth positions and a mechanism for imposing target positions as constraints. We implement our grasping model in conjunction with a constraint-based inextensible cloth simulator, where grasping is implemented as moving positional equality constraints coupled with stretch, shear, collision, and table contact projection steps. The same gripper trajectory is applied on a robot arm to fold a real piece of cloth, serving as a simple bridge between simulation and physical cloth manipulation and showcasing the realism and practicality of our idealized grasping model.

</details>


### [160] [C-space Analysis using Tropical Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29355)
*Abhilash Nayak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用热带几何与Puiseux级数对1自由度机构位形空间奇异点邻域进行近似有理参数化，成功检测横向分支与尖点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构位形空间（C-space）的奇异点决定运动分支切换，但切向、尖点、跨维及混合型奇异点的检测仍是难题，现有方法主要适用于横截相交情形。

Method: 借助热带几何方法，对1自由度机构C-space上任一点，用Puiseux级数求取过该点曲线的近似有理参数化，从而刻画该点邻域结构与分支行为。

Result: 在两种可折叠四杆机构中成功检测到横向分支，并在双瓦特（double Watt）机构的位形曲线上检测到尖点。

Conclusion: 该热带几何+Puiseux级数框架可有效分析1自由度机构C-space奇异点邻域，为机构运动学奇异分析提供了新工具；目前局限于1-dof与曲线情形，多维与更高自由度推广待研究。

Abstract: Configuration space~(C-space) of a mechanism is a real variety describing the set of feasible configurations that it can attain. To understand the behavior of a mechanism, it is crucial to identify and scrutinize especially the singular points of its C-space. They usually appear when the variety intersects itself, leading to different branches of motion. There exist many approaches to detect those intersections if they are transversal. However, the problem remains challenging if there are tangential, cuspidal, inter-dimensional or a combination of these intersections. This paper exploits an approach acquired from tropical geometry to analyze the neighborhood of any point on C-spaces of 1-degree-of-freedom~(\emph{dof}) mechanisms. This is done by finding the approximate rational parametrization of the curve(s) passing through the given point using Puiseux series. The proposed approach is shown to succesfully detect the transversal branchings in two foldable four bar mechanisms and a cusp in the configuration curve of the double Watt mechanism.

</details>


### [161] [FMCW-LIO: A Doppler LiDAR-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29374)
*Mingle Zhao,Jiahao Wang,Tianxiao Gao,Chengzhong Xu,Hui Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FMCW-LIO，首次利用 FMCW 多普勒 LiDAR 的点级瞬时多普勒速度构建运动补偿与多普勒辅助的流形状态估计模型，并通过多普勒判据剔除动态点，在结构退化场景下实现鲁棒的 LiDAR-惯性里程计与静态建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 LIO/SLAM 严重依赖环境几何特征，因 LiDAR 主要提供距离测量而非运动测量，在结构退化（长走廊、隧道、空旷/平面场景）中易失效；FMCW 多普勒 LiDAR 新增了瞬时多普勒速度这一运动观测，为突破几何依赖提供了新信息源，但缺乏相应的方法论。

Method: 提出 FMCW-LIO：1）设计运动补偿方法以正确利用多普勒速度测量；2）构建多普勒辅助观测模型，用于流形（on-manifold）状态估计；3）基于多普勒判据剔除动态点，获得更一致的几何观测；将多普勒与几何残差联合优化完成状态估计与静态建图。

Result: 在多种场景下进行大量实验，FMCW-LIO 在精度与鲁棒性上均优于其他算法，并在结构退化环境中仍能实现准确的状态估计与静态建图。

Conclusion: 多普勒速度作为独立于几何的运动观测，可有效补偿几何退化并抑制动态物体干扰，为新一代 FMCW 多普勒 LiDAR 的 LIO 框架提供了可复用的范式；后续可扩展到多传感器融合与更复杂动态场景。

Abstract: Conventional LiDAR-inertial odometry (LIO) or simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) methods heavily rely on geometric features of environments, as LiDARs primarily provide range measurements instead of motion measurements. From now on, however, the situation changes thanks to the novel Frequency Modulated Continuous Wave (FMCW) Doppler LiDARs. FMCW Doppler LiDARs not only offer the point range with high resolution but also capture the instant point Doppler velocity through the Doppler effect. In the letter, we propose FMCW-LIO, a novel and robust LIO, leveraging intrinsic Doppler measurements from FMCW Doppler LiDARs. To correctly exploit Doppler velocities, a motion compensation method is designed, and a Doppler-aided observation model is applied for on-manifold state estimation. Then, dynamic points can be effectively removed by the Doppler criteria, deriving more consistent geometric observations. FMCW-LIO eventually achieves accurate state estimation and static mapping, even in structure-degenerated environments. Extensive experiments in diverse scenes are performed and FMCW-LIO outperforms other algorithms on both accuracy and robustness.

</details>


### [162] [Free-Init: Scan-Free, Motion-Free, and Correspondence-Free Initialization for Doppler LiDAR-Inertial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29375)
*Mingle Zhao,Jiahao Wang,Tianxiao Gao,Chengzhong Xu,Hui Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Free-Init：一种面向 LiDAR-惯性系统的高频、鲁棒初始化框架，融合 FMCW 多普勒 LiDAR 的逐点多普勒速度与惯性测量，在非惯性运动学下摆脱对运动去畸变、激励运动与地图对应的依赖，输出频率超 10 kHz，兼容多种 LIO 系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线系统对初始化鲁棒性要求高，但传统 LiDAR-惯性初始化依赖运动去畸变、充分激励运动与地图对应，导致系统启动时在静止、动态或剧烈运动场景下易失败或收敛慢。

Method: 利用 FMCW 多普勒 LiDAR 提供的逐点多普勒速度，与非惯性运动学下的惯性测量融合，构建内嵌的多普勒-惯性测速器；无需 LiDAR 扫描去畸变、无需激励运动、无需地图对应，且即插即用于典型 LiDAR-惯性系统。

Result: 在多平台、多运动场景的实验中验证了有效性，实现快速收敛与超过 10 kHz 的高频输出，支持静止、动态乃至剧烈运动下的初始化。

Conclusion: Free-Init 展示了快速、鲁棒、动态初始化对在线系统的必要性，为多普勒 LiDAR-惯性融合初始化提供了即插即用的可行方案。

Abstract: Robust initialization is crucial for online systems. In the letter, a high-frequency and resilient initialization framework is designed for LiDAR-inertial systems, leveraging both inertial sensors and Doppler LiDAR. The innovative FMCW Doppler LiDAR opens up a novel avenue for robotic sensing by capturing not only point range but also Doppler velocity via the intrinsic Doppler effect. By fusing point-wise Doppler velocity with inertial measurements under non-inertial kinematics, the proposed framework, Free-Init, eliminates reliance on motion undistortion of LiDAR scans, excitation motions, and map correspondences during the initialization phase. Free-Init is also plug-and-play compatible with typical LiDAR-inertial systems and is versatile to handle a wide range of initial motions when the system starts, including stationary, dynamic, and even violent motions. The embedded Doppler-inertial velocimeter ensures fast convergence and high-frequency performance, delivering outputs exceeding 10 kHz. Comprehensive experiments on diverse platforms and across myriad motion scenes validate the framework's effectiveness. The results demonstrate the superior performance of Free-Init, highlighting the necessity of fast, resilient, and dynamic initialization for online systems.

</details>


### [163] [Decoupled Early Exits for Task-Dependent Compute Allocation in Flow-Matching VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29382)
*Riccardo Andrea Izzo,Rimvydas Rubavicius,Gianluca Bardaro,Subramanian Ramamoorthy,Matteo Matteucci,Alessandro Suglia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出将VLA的骨干深度V、动作专家深度A与去噪步数D作为三个协同可配置的计算轴，通过在中间层插入轻量Exit Transformer并合成KV Cache实现免从头训练的弹性推理，在LIBERO与Meta-World上降低79.2%延迟与31.8% FLOPs并提升5.6%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow-matching VLA（VLM骨干+动作专家）参数量巨大，机器人端推理开销过高。已有加速方法主要依靠骨干层提前退出或减少去噪步数，而动作专家深度未被利用，计算预算的配置维度不完整。

Method: 以预训练VLA为起点，在骨干与动作专家的中间深度附加轻量Exit Transformer（ET），训练目标为将策略最后一层蒸馏到各出口；提出KV Cache合成机制，为被跳过的骨干层补全缺失的key/value，使动作专家可以比骨干退出得更深；将V、A、D作为联合可配置计算轴进行搜索。无需从零重训原策略，每个出口仅增加2.1%（SmolVLA）/4.1%（π0.5）参数量。

Result: 在SmolVLA与π0.5两个flow-matching VLA、LIBERO与Meta-World两个基准上验证：V与A分别降低FLOPs与延迟，D同时改善两者；最优计算预算具任务依赖性；联合配置(V,A,D)降低79.2%延迟、31.8% FLOPs，平均成功率提升5.6%。

Conclusion: VLA推理效率的关键维度应扩展到动作专家深度，三轴联合配置可在不牺牲甚至提升成功率的前提下大幅压缩计算与延迟；但证据仅来自仿真基准，缺真实机器人部署与代码/开源细节。

Abstract: Flow-matching Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a potential solution for generalist robot control, designed by combining a pretrained Vision-Language Model (VLM) backbone with an action expert that generates continuous robot actions. While these models exhibit impressive capabilities, due to their very high number of parameters, their computational requirements are often prohibitive for robotics control. To mitigate these inefficiencies, existing methods predominantly skip VLM backbone layers with early exits or reduce denoising steps, while leaving action expert depth untouched. We propose a framework that exposes backbone depth $V$, action expert depth $A$, and denoising steps $D$ as three jointly configurable compute axes in a VLA. Starting from a pretrained VLA, we attach lightweight Exit Transformers (ET) at intermediate depths in both the backbone and the action expert, trained to distil the last layer of the policy into each exit. Furthermore, we introduce a KV Cache synthesis mechanism that manages the missing keys and values of the skipped backbone layers, allowing the action expert to exit deeper than the backbone. Finally, we show that the optimal compute budget is task-dependent, with different tasks benefiting from different axes and depths. Notably, our method does not require training the original policy from scratch, and for each exit, it increases the number of parameters by only $2.1\%$ for SmolVLA and $4.1\%$ for $\pi_{0.5}$. We validate our approach across two flow-matching VLAs (SmolVLA, $\pi_{0.5}$) and two benchmarks (LIBERO, Meta-World), revealing complementary effects: $V$ and $A$ respectively reduce FLOPs and latency, while $D$ improves both. Our joint configurations $(V,A,D)$ reduce latency by $79.2\%$ and computation (FLOPs) by $31.8\%$, while improving mean success rate by $5.6\%$.

</details>


### [164] [Robo-Harness K1: Harnessing Robot-Use Agents via Perception Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29389)
*Zexi Li,Yehang Zhang,Wenqian Li,Haojian Huang,Chenxu Wang,Shiyuan Deng,Yangkai Wei,Tianyi Zhang,Binghui Xie,Bohan Zhou,Yifan Chang,Kaiwen Zhou,Ying-Cong Chen,James Cheng,Yinchuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Robo-Harness K1，一种"机器人使用型 agent"(RUA) 框架，将感知（标定深度、持久视觉锚点、空间测量、抓取假设）以工具形式暴露给 VLM，再由 agent 从证据中选择通用动作，无需改动 VLM 架构或训练深度编码器，实现免微调的跨本体操作迁移与少样本策略蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础 VLM 已能理解物体、指令与空间关系，但迁移到机器人操作仍困难：VLA 模型需要大量演示且可能损害预训练理解能力；纯 RGB 的 VLM 直接控制成本高、强依赖模型自身能力。作者希望找到一种既保留 VLM 通用能力、又低成本引入 3D 几何信息的接口方案。

Method: 提出 Robo-Harness K1 的 robot-use agent 框架，把感知能力封装为可调用工具：agent 按需查询标定深度、持久视觉锚点、空间测量与抓取假设，并基于返回的证据选择通用运动原语。该接口在不修改 VLM 架构、不额外训练深度编码器的前提下让模型获得 3D 几何可及性。工具调用轨迹与 next-token 训练格式对齐，可蒸馏到小模型（Qwen3.5-9B）。

Result: LIBERO-PRO 匹配任务上，Gemini 3.7 Flash + K1 达 77.8%，超过 GPT-6 Astra + RGB-only harness 的 61.1%；K1 将 Astra 提升至 88.9%。无目标微调下迁移到三个 RoboSuite 机械臂与双臂 RoboTwin；RoboTwin 上 Easy 32.0%、Hard 28.0%，对视觉与环境扰动有韧性。仅用 107 条教师 episode 训练的 Qwen3.5-9B 学生在新初始状态达 44.2%（OpenVLA 30.2%），在未见任务条件上 13.9%（OpenVLA 0.0%）。

Conclusion: 感知增强的 robot-use agent 提供了一条可复用 VLM 能力、样本高效且具泛化性的机器人策略路线；关键在于工具化感知接口而非改造模型或大规模动作演示。

Abstract: Foundation vision-language models (VLMs) understand objects, instructions, and spatial relations, yet translating this capability into robotic manipulation remains difficult. Vision-language-action (VLA) models require extensive demonstrations and may compromise pretrained understanding, while direct RGB-only VLM control is costly and strongly dependent on model capability. We introduce Robo-Harness K1, a robot-use agent (RUA) framework that exposes perception as tools. The agent queries calibrated depth, persistent visual anchors, spatial measurements, and grasp hypotheses, then selects generic motions from the returned evidence. This interface makes 3D geometry accessible without changing the VLM architecture or training a depth encoder. On matched LIBERO-PRO tasks, Gemini 3.7 Flash with K1 reaches 77.8% accuracy, surpassing GPT-6 Astra's 61.1% with an RGB-only harness; K1 further improves Astra to 88.9%. Without target fine-tuning, Gemini with K1 transfers to three RoboSuite arms and dual-arm RoboTwin tasks. On RoboTwin, it achieves 32.0% on Easy and 28.0% on Hard, showing resilience to visual and environmental perturbations. K1 also produces tool-call traces aligned with next-token training. A Qwen3.5-9B student trained on only 107 teacher episodes reaches 44.2% accuracy on new initial states versus 30.2% for OpenVLA, and 13.9% on held-out task conditions versus 0.0% for OpenVLA. These results suggest that perception-augmented RUAs offer a promising route to sample-efficient, generalizable robotic policies that leverage VLM capabilities through an accessible tool interface.

</details>


### [165] [RACaP: Agentic Reasoning, Acting, and Coding as Policies for Evolvable Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29394)
*Zexi Li,Yehang Zhang,Haojian Huang,Bohan Zhou,Wenqian Li,Chenxu Wang,Yifan Chang,Yangkai Wei,Tianyi Zhang,Ying-Cong Chen,Kaiwen Zhou,Yinchuan Li,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RACaP将代码生成从运行时迁移到进化阶段，部署时用ReAct循环调用冻结的类型化Policy API，通过能力课程学习与自主自演化改进API、ReAct框架和经验记忆，在LIBERO长程任务上大幅超越Code-as-Policies基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Code as Policies（CaP）方法在运行时生成与修复程序，导致推理延迟高，且把可复用机制与任务特定决策纠缠在一起，难以持续演化与迁移。作者希望将可复用代码与运行时决策解耦。

Method: 提出RACaP智能体框架：将编码过程移至进化阶段，部署时以ReAct循环调用冻结的类型化Policy API；API编码可复用物理机制并暴露参数供运行时自适应；两阶段策略结合能力课程学习与自主自演化，同时优化API、ReAct harness与经验记忆；ReAct融合任务工作记忆、长期经验记忆与视觉反馈进行动作选择、结果校验与失败恢复，无需改源码；用拒绝采样微调将GPT-5.6的ReAct决策蒸馏到Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct。

Result: LIBERO-90成功率54.4%，零样本LIBERO-PRO 45.0%，LIBERO-Long 46.0%，而CaP基线在长程任务上最高仅4.0%；LIBERO-PRO上成功率约为CaP基线的2.5倍，中位策略耗时提速1.9倍；蒸馏后单次决策推理提速13.2倍，重复物理调用从16次降至4次。

Conclusion: 将可复用代码与运行时决策分离，能够支持持续演化、有效迁移与高效的长程控制，为通用机器人智能体提供了一条可演化的部署路径。

Abstract: General-purpose robot agents must learn from experience, transfer to new tasks, and act efficiently. Code as Policies (CaP) methods generate and repair programs at runtime, incurring latency and entangling reusable mechanisms with task-specific decisions. We introduce RACaP, an agentic framework that moves coding to evolution and uses a Reasoning-and-Acting (ReAct) loop to call frozen, typed Policy APIs at deployment. A two-phase strategy combines capability curriculum learning with autonomous self-evolution to improve the APIs, the ReAct harness, and experience memory. The APIs encode reusable physical mechanisms while exposing arguments for runtime adaptation. ReAct combines task-specific working memory, long-term experience memory, and visual feedback to select actions, verify outcomes, and recover from failures without modifying source code. RACaP achieves 54.4% success on LIBERO-90, 45.0% on zero-shot LIBERO-PRO, and 46.0% on LIBERO-Long, compared with at most 4.0% for CaP baselines on long-horizon tasks. On LIBERO-PRO, it achieves 2.5 times the success rate of CaP baselines and a 1.9-fold speedup in median policy time. For efficient on-robot deployment, rejection-sampled fine-tuning distills GPT-5.6 ReAct decisions into Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, yielding a 13.2-fold per-decision inference speedup and reducing repeated physical calls from 16 to 4. These results show that separating reusable code from runtime decisions supports continued evolution, effective transfer, and efficient long-horizon control.

</details>


### [166] [WRAP: Fixtureless Wrench-aware Multi-Robot Assembly Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29407)
*Valentin N. Hartmann,Huang Su,Yijiang Huang,Stelian Coros*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Wrap：面向多零件装配的多机器人装配规划器，联合推理装配顺序、支撑力与抓取，生成多机器人多目标运动规划并可执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装配常需专用夹具，或只能采用自上而下的装配策略。多机器人可免去夹具、提升灵活性，但多机器人装配序列规划面临任务分配与顺序组合爆炸，并且必须推理装配过程中的受力，以决定是否需要多机器人协同支撑或借助外部支撑（如桌面）。

Method: 输入为零件间装配顺序依赖、零件网格与初始状态。形式化为线性规划以推理能支撑装配过程受力与重量的有效抓取；以装配序列为骨架进行搜索，通过廉价的反向搜索计算启发式，再将该启发式用于代价更高的前向搜索，逐步贪婪寻找每一步的可行解。随后求解多机器人多目标运动规划问题；执行阶段将整体规划拆分为接触丰富的装配技能与自由空间运动。

Result: 在一系列多零件装配任务上进行基准测试，并应用于尺寸与运动学各异的机器人组；同时通过物理仿真与真实机器人实验进行了验证。

Conclusion: 该方法使无夹具、非纯自上而下的多机器人装配成为可能，在仿真与真实系统中验证了规划与执行流程的整体有效性。

Abstract: Assembly using robots often requires specially designed fixtures, or relies on top-down only assembly strategies. Using multiple robots, we can avoid using fixtures and make robotic assembly more flexible. Planning assembly sequences for multiple robots is challenging due to the high number of possible task assignments and orders. In addition, we need to reason over forces that occur during the assembly process, e.g., to decide if multiple robots are required for support, or if external support such as a table should be used. We present Wrap, a multi-robot assembly planner for multi-part assemblies, given the inter-part ordering-dependencies, the part meshes, and their initial state. We formulate a linear program to reason about valid grasps for supporting the forces that occur during assembly. The search leverages the assembly sequence, and greedily finds a feasible solution per assembly step by computing a heuristic via a cheap backwards search, and using the heuristic in the more expensive forward search. We then solve the multi-robot, multi-goal motion planning problem, and for execution, we split the plan into contact-rich assembly skills, and free space motion. We benchmark the planner on a variety of multi-part assemblies, and apply the planner to groups of robots differing in size and kinematics. We validate the work both in a physics simulation, and in real. Videos and code are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [167] [Singularity Analysis for the Perspective-Four and Five-Line Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29417)
*Jorge García Fontán,Abhilash Nayak,Sébastien Briot,Mohab Safey El Din*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文利用计算代数几何方法系统刻画了基于四、五条直线的图像视觉伺服与位姿估计中雅可比矩阵的奇异配置，证明一维奇异发生在相机位于观测直线横截线上，并给出四线（存在至多10个不可避免奇异位形）与五线（通常情形无奇异）的结论及约束情形下的特征分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像视觉伺服与位姿估计在特定相机-直线相对位形下会出现控制不稳定与精度下降，作者希望从根本上确定导致这些问题的相对配置，即雅可比矩阵的奇异点，以指导可避开奇异的设计。

Method: 选择合适基重访三条直线情形，借助计算代数几何工具求解雅可比矩阵全部子式同时为零的配置；由单叶双曲面结论推广至n条直线，证明一维奇异对应相机位于观测直线的横截线上；对四条线强制横截线为复数以规避该类型奇异，分析另一类奇异在四线时的不可避免位置数量；对五条线讨论一般情形及正交、平行约束下的奇异特征；最后用视觉伺服库进行仿真实验验证。

Result: 三条线时一类奇异发生在相机位于直线唯一确定的单叶双曲面上；n线情形的一维奇异发生在相机位于观测直线横截线上；四线可借复数横截线规避该类型奇异，但另一类奇异最多存在10个不可避免的相机奇异位置；五线在一般情形下不存在奇异；并给出四、五线在正交与平行约束下的奇异刻画；仿真验证了奇异附近控制问题与位姿估计误差增大的预期行为。

Conclusion: 该工作用代数几何工具给出了多直线视觉伺服中奇异位形的完整刻画，明确了可规避与不可避免奇异的区别，并通过仿真验证，为设计无奇异或近奇异鲁棒的视觉伺服与位姿估计方案提供理论依据。

Abstract: This paper deals with image-based visual servoing and pose estimation by observing four and five lines. Our main interest is to determine the relative configurations of the camera and the observed lines that lead to problems in control and stability. Since it is equivalent to finding the singularities of the corresponding Jacobian matrix, we use tools from computational algebraic geometry to seek configurations such that all of its minors vanish simultaneously. By choosing a suitable basis for this matrix, we revisit the problem in the case of three lines to show that one type of the singularities is when the camera lies on the hyperboloid of one sheet uniquely defined by the lines. This result is further exploited to prove that the one-dimensional singularities, if any, in the case of $n$ lines appear when the camera lies on the transversals to the observed lines. Thus, by forcing the transversals to be complex, we can avoid the aforementioned type of singularities in the case of four lines although the algebra shows that there can always be up to 10 inevitable singular locations of the camera for the other type of singularity. For five lines, we find out that there are no singularities in the generic case. The singularities are also characterized for four and five lines with orthogonality and parallelism constraints. Furthermore, a visual servoing library is used to conduct some simulated experiments to substantiate the theoretical results. As expected, we observe problems in control in the vicinity of a singularity as well as increased errors in pose estimation.

</details>


### [168] [UCON: Uncertainty-aware Navigation with Historical Re-association in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29419)
*Bing Sun,Yue Lin,Yongsheng Yuan,Yang Liu,Dong Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UCON：面向动态环境的不确定性感知导航算法，通过点级历史重关联恢复丢失目标并保持身份连续性，用卡尔曼滤波做各向异性运动估计与协方差传播，再将预测状态与协方差转为不确定性扇区作为可微代价项嵌入轨迹优化，实现一致的动态避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境下自主导航受两大问题制约：感知不稳定导致身份切换与运动估计不可靠；不确定性-优化不匹配使得运动不确定性无法被有原则地纳入轨迹优化。

Method: (1) 点级历史重关联机制，利用历史点云片段恢复丢失目标并维持ID连续性；(2) 卡尔曼滤波提供各向异性运动状态估计与协方差传播；(3) 将预测状态及协方差转化为不确定性扇区，作为可微代价项嵌入轨迹优化，兼顾避障一致性、平滑性与可行性。

Result: 大量仿真与真实世界实验表明，在保持高计算效率的同时，UCON在感知稳定性与动态环境导航鲁棒性上优于现有最先进方法，代码将开源。

Conclusion: UCON通过历史重关联与不确定性扇区的可微优化耦合，缓解了感知不稳定与不确定性-优化不匹配问题，实现了高效鲁棒的动态环境不确定性感知导航。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in dynamic environments is hindered by two fundamental challenges: perception instability and uncertainty-optimization mismatch. The former leads to identity switches and unreliable motion estimation, while the latter prevents principled incorporation of motion uncertainty into trajectory optimization. To address these challenges, we propose UCON, an uncertainty-aware navigation algorithm in dynamic environments. For perception instability, we present a point-level historical re-association mechanism that leverages historical point cloud fragments to recover lost targets while maintaining identity continuity. Subsequently, a Kalman filter is employed to provide anisotropic motion state estimation and covariance propagation. To resolve the uncertainty-optimization mismatch, we transform predicted states and their covariances into uncertainty sectors, which are embedded as differentiable cost terms within a trajectory optimization framework. This achieves consistent uncertainty-aware dynamic obstacle avoidance while maintaining smoothness and feasibility. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate that, while maintaining high computational efficiency, UCON achieves superior perception stability and robust navigation performance in dynamic environments compared to state-of-the-art methods. The code will be open-sourced to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [169] [Temperament Engineering: Designing Strategic Behavioural Diversity in Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29423)
*Edmund R. Hunt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"性情工程"框架：以动物行为学的五条性情轴（害羞-大胆、探索-回避、活跃性、攻击性、社交性）作为设计词汇，将每个轴参数化为连续控制参数τ∈[0,1]，把群体中τ的分布而非个体控制器作为设计对象，并给出三阶段工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人个体差异（标定误差、电池状态、传感器漂移、磨损）通常被视为需最小化的缺陷，而动物集体中的个体行为差异（性情）由自然选择塑造并对群体性能有决定性作用。作者希望将这种异质性从"误差"重新定位为可设计的资源。

Method: 观点/立场论文（perspective），无实验。核心方法路径：1）借用5条经演化验证的动物性情轴作为设计词汇，映射为控制器之上的连续参数τ∈[0,1]，可实现为模块阈值、MARL中的策略条件向量、或基础模型规划器的约束；2）三阶段工作流：将任务成功准则映射到相关轴→规划τ分布形状→调节反应规范（reaction norms）；3）区分中心化（τ分布可作为规划器在线输出）与去中心化（必须作为离线、预见性设计输入）两种情形。

Result: 无定量结果。文中引用既有结论：工程化异质性在聚集、探索等任务上已显示优于同质群体。作者提出从机器人特有属性（自模型可塑性、力度、主动性、表达性）衍生机器人原生的性情轴，并给出未来研究议程：确立异质性何时、以多大代价换取性能回报。

Conclusion: 将行为异质性与平台异质性提升为协同设计变量，把群体分布而非单体控制器作为设计对象，为群体机器人提供一套生物启发的设计框架。

Abstract: No two robots are truly identical: calibration, battery state, sensor drift and wear give every swarm a distribution of behaviour rather than a single point, usually treated as an imperfection to be minimised. In animal collectives the reverse holds: consistent individual differences in behaviour ('temperament') are shaped by natural selection and often decisive for group performance. This perspective proposes 'temperament engineering', a bio-inspired framework that treats the swarm's distribution of temperaments, rather than the individual controller, as the design object. It borrows five evolutionarily validated axes of animal temperament (shyness-boldness, exploration-avoidance, activity, aggressiveness and sociability) as a design vocabulary, rendering each as a continuous control parameter $\tau \in [0,1]$ above the controller, realisable as a module threshold, a policy-conditioning vector in multi-agent reinforcement learning, or a constraint on a foundation-model planner. A three-phase workflow maps mission success criteria onto relevant axes, plans the shape of the $\tau$ distribution, and tunes reaction norms governing how temperament responds to environmental cues. The payoff is greatest under decentralisation: where a central planner can reassign behaviour online, a temperament distribution is a planner output, but in a swarm without global knowledge it must be an offline, anticipatory design input. Behavioural and platform heterogeneity are thereby co-design variables, and I sketch tentative robot-native axes (self-model plasticity, forcefulness, initiative and expressiveness) arising from features robots have and animals do not. Engineered heterogeneity has been shown to outperform homogeneous swarms in tasks such as aggregation and exploration; establishing when, and how much, heterogeneity repays its cost is the work the field can now take forward.

</details>


### [170] [Coupled State-Space Modelling, Control, and Policy Distillation for Hybrid Rigid-Pneumatic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29424)
*Alan Royce Gabriel Samuel,Pulkit Verma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对"刚性关节+折纸气动段"混合机械臂，首次建立含气室动力学与折痕迟滞的全耦合模型，量化解耦控制的代价，并将MPC蒸馏为小型神经策略实现实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已发表的混合（电机刚性关节+压力驱动折纸段）机械臂均采用按自由度解耦的控制回路，但该近似带来的耦合代价从未被量化，因为可用于测量的耦合模型尚未构建。论文旨在补上该模型并衡量解耦控制的损失。

Method: 为N个交替的旋转关节与Kresling折纸段构成的链式结构推导耦合动力学模型，纳入气动腔室动力学与折痕迟滞；基于该模型直接测量关节间耦合强度；将解耦PID与耦合模型控制器对比；采用行为克隆与DAgger将MPC蒸馏为小型神经策略；并针对教师失败案例定位极限环，通过选择低压可保持的目标位姿进行消除。

Result: 耦合强度逐关节不同，解耦控制在强耦合关节上损失明显、在近似解耦关节上仍有竞争力；耦合模型控制器相比解耦PID基线跟踪精度提升2.5倍且力矩更低；MPC实时性不足，无模型强化学习在严格整定指标下成功率远不达标；蒸馏策略目标整定成功率93–94%、零碰撞，接近教师水平，且能在5 ms控制步内运行；教师失败可归因于与波纹管轻阻尼模态相关的极限环，通过选取低压可保持目标位姿予以消除。

Conclusion: 耦合建模揭示了混合折纸机械臂解耦控制的真实代价，为混合驱动结构提供了高精度且可实时部署的控制路径；MPC蒸馏是兼顾精度与实时性的有效方案，且失效模式可通过物理约束（低压可保持位姿）主动规避。

Abstract: Hybrid manipulators combine motorized rigid joints with pressure-actuated origami segments. Published arms of this kind are controlled with decoupled per-DOF loops, and the cost of this approximation has not been quantified, because the coupled model needed to measure it has not been built. This paper derives such a model for a chain of $N$ alternating revolute joints and Kresling origami segments, including pneumatic chamber dynamics and crease hysteresis. Using the model, we measure the coupling directly and show that its strength varies joint by joint, and that decoupled control loses precisely on the strongly coupled joints while remaining competitive on the one nearly decoupled joint. Coupled model-based controllers track $2.5\times$ tighter than a decoupled PID baseline at lower torque. However, the model predictive controller (MPC) is too slow for real time, and model-free reinforcement learning stalls far below acceptable success rates on a strict settling metric. We therefore distill the MPC into a small neural policy with behavior cloning and DAgger. The distilled policy settles 93-94$\%$ of goals with zero collisions, within a few points of its teacher, and runs inside the 5 ms control step where the MPC does not. Where the teacher itself fails, we trace the failure to a limit cycle with the bellows' lightly damped mode, and we remove it by selecting goal postures holdable at low pressure.

</details>


### [171] [RoboLDA: A Probabilistic Generative Model for Uncovering Embodied Hierarchical Structures in Voxel-based Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29490)
*Junru Song,Yang Yang,Jingdan Shi,Guozhen Li,Weien Zhou,Ying Wen,Feifei Wang,Wen Yao,Tingsong Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboLDA 是一个分层贝叶斯生成模型，将体素软体机器人形态分解为"任务-机器人-器官-体素"四级结构，通过变分推断训练，可从已有高性能设计中提取分层先验，实现零样本机器人设计，无需优化即可达进化算法性能的106.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体素软体机器人（VSR）的分层形态结构能降低设计与控制复杂度，但现有方法严重依赖领域专家知识，缺乏自动获取分层设计原则的手段。作者追问：能否仅从已有成功设计中推导出这类分层设计原则？

Method: 提出 RoboLDA，将 VSR 形态生成建模为"任务-机器人-器官-体素"四级层次的贝叶斯概率模型，并采用变分推断进行训练，从而从高性能 VSR 设计数据中学习分层先验分布。

Result: 仿真 VSR 实验验证了高性能设计中存在一致且直觉可解释的分层模式；RoboLDA 能提取并利用这些分层先验完成未见任务的零样本设计，生成设计平均达到进化算法优化性能的 106.4%；推断出的"器官"结构是有效的功能子结构，与模块化控制策略结合可显著增强协同运动控制。

Conclusion: 该工作开创了机器人形态的分层生成式建模，为具身智能体更可解释、更可泛化的开发提供了新路径。
