Abstract: Recent advances in robotics highlight hierarchical configurations of robot morphology, where multiple levels of functional substructures synergize to facilitate intelligent behaviors. This hierarchical perspective, while particularly advantageous for voxel-based soft robots (VSRs) to ease design and control complexities, is hindered by its heavy reliance on domain expertise. In this work, we address the following question: can we derive such hierarchical design principles solely from existing successful designs? We answer affirmatively by presenting RoboLDA, a Bayesian probabilistic model that decomposes VSR morphology generation into a four-level hierarchy: "task-robot-organ-voxel", and is trained via variational inference. Through extensive experiments on simulated VSRs, we verify the presence of consistent, intuitive hierarchical patterns underlying high-performing VSR designs and showcase RoboLDA's proficiency to extract and leverage these hierarchical priors for zero-shot robot design in unseen tasks. The generated designs, even without further optimization, achieve on average 106.4% of the optimized performance produced by evolutionary algorithms. Additionally, the organ structures inferred by RoboLDA serve as valid functional substructures, significantly enhancing synergistic motion control when integrated with modular control policies. Our work pioneers hierarchical generative modeling of robot morphology, offering a promising pathway towards more interpretable and generalizable development of embodied agents.

</details>


### [172] [Generative Evolutionary Design of Voxel-Based Soft Robots with Provable Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29491)
*Junru Song,Huan Xiao,Yang Yang,Guozhen Li,Wei Peng,Xiaoya Zhang,Tingsong Jiang,Weien Zhou,Ying Wen,Feifei Wang,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MISCO：以深度生成模型（多任务VAE+EDA）驱动的体素软体机器人设计进化优化框架，并提供全局最优渐近收敛的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体素软体机器人（VSR）设计空间巨大、单次评估代价高，传统进化优化在样本效率与设计空间探索上受限，亟需兼具表达力与理论可靠性的设计优化方法。

Method: 将分布估计算法（EDA）与精心设计的变分自编码器结合：VAE具备多任务学习、位置感知（position awareness）和体素间信号传递（inter-voxel signaling），以增强形态表征能力，并支持形态分布的高效采样与迭代优化。

Result: 理论上证明了MISCO渐近收敛到全局最优设计并给出有利的收敛速率；仿真实验显示其能在庞大设计空间中高效搜索，为多种任务进化出高性能VSR，并可灵活权衡优化效率与形态多样性。

Conclusion: MISCO在经验与理论两方面均得到验证，是迈向更可扩展、更可靠的软体机器人设计自动化的一步。

Abstract: Voxel-based soft robots (VSRs) present a promising avenue for developing artificial organisms with lifelike intelligence. However, the vast design spaces and expensive evaluations substantially challenge their design optimization. Here we develop MISCO, a novel evolutionary framework empowered by deep generative models to optimize VSR designs with theoretical guarantees. MISCO integrates an estimation-of-distribution algorithm with a meticulously designed variational autoencoder featuring multi-task learning, position awareness, and inter-voxel signaling. These key components enhance the representational capacity of VSR morphologies and facilitate highly efficient sampling and optimization of morphological distributions. We provide theoretical guarantees for MISCO's asymptotic convergence to globally optimal designs, alongside a favorable convergence rate. Extensive simulated experiments further demonstrate MISCO's exceptional effectiveness in navigating vast design spaces, evolving high-performing VSRs for diverse tasks while flexibly balancing optimization efficiency and morphological diversity. Being validated both empirically and theoretically, MISCO represents a step change towards more scalable and reliable soft robot development.

</details>


### [173] [Free the Language Model From the Vision Encoder: Semantic Serialization as a Perception Interface for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29601)
*Cong Xu,Ravi Sankar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种“视觉不进入语言模型”的具身场景问答接口：冻结的感知栈检测与测距，确定性语义序列化器把感知状态（含错误）编译为面向决策的文本，再由纯文本LLM作答。在遮挡审计的校园机器人基准上，序列化接口优于同规模（7B/3B）零样本VLM，增益经释义实验证实来自决策对齐计算而非答案泄漏；但在同等任务监督下两种路线收敛，且均为感知瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端VLM把视觉能力绑定在语言模型规模上，语言模型缩小则感知与推理解耦退化。作者希望检验：若视觉完全不进入语言模型，仅通过确定性文本序列化传递感知状态，能否在具身问答中解耦“读取能力”与“视觉感知”，并量化该接口的优势边界与失效条件。

Method: 冻结感知栈做目标检测与测距；确定性语义序列化器将感知状态（包含检测错误）编译为面向决策的文本；使用未经修改的纯文本LLM作答。在可见范围匹配、遮挡审计的校园机器人基准上，按前瞻性冻结准则评测，并在每折内用域内微调检测器。另设预注册解耦实验（释义改写、新判断词表）区分决策对齐计算与答案串泄漏，并用真值 oracle 定位读者能力下限，最后与LoRA微调的VLM及同监督文本读者做对照。

Result: 序列化接口在7B规模上优于同规模零样本VLM（0.7892 vs 0.7462），在3B上优势更大（0.7673 vs 0.6913）；增益经释义仍存在，归因于决策对齐计算而非答案串泄漏；新判断词表限制其适用范围。优势随读者缩小到1.5B而扩大，在0.5B处反转，真值 oracle 给出读者能力下限。同等任务监督下两者收敛：LoRA微调VLM超过零样本系统，但与同等监督的文本读者持平（0.8441 vs 0.8396，无统计显著差异），两条路线均为感知瓶颈。感知参数量与VLM视觉塔相当，总计算量并未更小。

Conclusion: 解耦式“感知→确定性文本序列化→纯文本LLM”接口在特定具身QA设定下可超越同规模零样本VLM，且在读者能力足够时优势稳健、可归因于决策对齐计算；但该优势受新判断词表限制、在极小读者上反转，且在同监督下与VLM收敛并同受感知瓶颈约束。其价值在于提供解耦视角与边界刻画，而非通用性能优势；总计算与感知参数并无节省。

Abstract: End-to-end vision-language models (VLMs) bind visual competence to the scale of their language model: as the language model shrinks, perception and reasoning degrade together. We study an embodied scene question-answering (QA) interface in which vision never enters the language model. A frozen perception stack detects and ranges objects; a deterministic semantic serializer compiles the perceived state, errors included, into decision-aligned text; an unmodified text-only large language model (LLM) answers. On a visible-scope-matched, occlusion-audited campus-robot benchmark, under a prospectively frozen criterion, the serialized interface, using detectors fine-tuned in-domain within each fold, outperforms a zero-shot VLM whose language model has the same 7B scale (0.7892 vs 0.7462), with a larger margin at 3B (0.7673 vs 0.6913). Preregistered decoupling experiments show the gain survives paraphrase, attributing it to decision-aligned computation rather than answer-string leakage, while novel judgment vocabularies bound its scope. The advantage grows as the reader shrinks to 1.5B and reverses at 0.5B, and a ground-truth oracle locates the reader-capability floor. Under matched task supervision the interfaces converge: a VLM fine-tuned with low-rank adaptation (LoRA) overtakes the zero-shot system but only ties an equally supervised text reader (0.8441 vs 0.8396, no statistically resolved difference), and both routes remain perception-bound. Reported perception parameters are comparable to those of the VLM's vision tower, and total compute is not smaller.

</details>


### [174] [Markerless Multi-Modal Autonomous Robotic Inspection of Large Space Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29644)
*Juan De Dios Alfaro,Arturo Ríos,David Rodríguez-Martínez,Carlos Pérez-del-Pulgar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无标记自主机器人巡检流水线，以3D重建作为巡检支撑表示，集成Kinova Gen2机械臂与RGB-D/热成像/2D LiDAR多模态传感头，通过体素采样生成视点、MoveIt避障规划，并用Nerfacto与Thermal-Nerfacto完成几何与热感知重建，Gazebo仿真与初步实验室验证了空间一致数据采集能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来可展开天线、太阳能电站与大型轨道平台等空间设施需要能在先验知识有限、无合作标志条件下自主作业的巡检系统；现有在轨服务方案依赖预定义轨迹、标准接口、基准标志或精确目标模型，难以扩展到大型、异构或部分未知结构，因此需要无标记、模型无关的自主巡检表示与流程。

Method: 系统硬件为Kinova Gen2机械臂与末端多模态传感头（RGB-D相机、热相机、2D LiDAR）；流水线先估计近似巡检体积，在其内生成候选视点，用MoveIt做无碰撞运动规划，并在ROS2中同步记录RGB-D图像、热数据与机器人位姿。对比评估多种候选重建方法后，选用Nerfacto做几何重建、Thermal-Nerfacto演示热感知渲染。

Result: Gazebo仿真与初步实验室测试表明，系统可自主获取空间一致的巡检数据，并生成适用于视觉与几何评估的重建结果，验证了无标记流程在非合作结构巡检中的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作为大型非合作空间结构的自主巡检提供了初步但完整的系统框架，将神经辐射场重建引入在轨巡检支撑表示；目前证据以仿真与初步实验室测试为主，尚缺真实在轨或大规模实物部署验证，未来需扩展至更复杂尺度与真实环境。

Abstract: Future orbital infrastructures, such as deployable antennas, solar farms, and large orbital platforms will require autonomous inspection systems able to operate with limited prior knowledge and without cooperative markers. Current on-orbit servicing approaches often rely on predefined trajectories, standard interfaces, fiducial markers or accurate target models, which limits scalability for large, heterogeneous or partially unknown structures. This paper presents a markerless autonomous robotic inspection pipeline in which 3D reconstruction is used as an inspection-support representation. The system integrates a Kinova Gen2 manipulator with an end-effector-mounted multimodal sensor head composed of an RGB-D camera, a thermal camera and a 2D LiDAR. The pipeline estimates an approximate inspection volume, generates viewpoints, plans collision-free motions with MoveIt, and synchronously records RGB-D images, thermal data, and robot poses in ROS2. Candidate reconstruction methods were evaluated to select a practical method for this pipeline, with Nerfacto used for geometric reconstruction and Thermal-Nerfacto used to demonstrate thermal-aware rendering for inspection. Validation in a Gazebo-based simulator and preliminary laboratory tests reveal that the proposed system can autonomously acquire spatially coherent inspection data and produce reconstructions suitable for visual and geometric assessment, representing a step towards inspection of large non-cooperative space structures.

</details>


### [175] [Do World Models Make Better Robots? A Survey of Evaluation Benchmarks for Predictive Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29669)
*Gaytri Jena,Kapil Wanaskar,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Vasu Sharma,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文是一篇评测视角的综述，指出世界模型与直接VLA策略之间缺乏可比的闭环评测，系统梳理2017–2026年160个基准并给出分类学、评测回路与四类"优势感知"指标协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习分两条路线：VLA策略以闭环任务成功率衡量，世界模型以开环预测/生成质量衡量，两者评测标准割裂，导致"世界建模是否带来可测量的闭环优势、体现在哪些能力上"这一核心问题无法回答。作者认为瓶颈在于评测方式而非模型本身。

Method: 综述性工作。整理160个经网络核验的基准（2017–2026），按评测模式、机器人能力、模型族三个维度组织为四条赛道：策略套件、具身智能体、世界模型评测、预测到动作的桥接；构建可操作分类学；与8篇最接近的综述做覆盖对比；提出可隔离预测优势的评测回路；给出锚定具名测试床的四项优势感知指标协议。

Result: 160个基准中138个为模型无关，仅11个（7%）构建显式VLA-对-世界模型对比；反事实能力几乎完全未被度量；仅4个基准将预测转化为实际执行的动作。作者自述是唯一在"能力×模型族"两个维度上交叉覆盖的综述。

Conclusion: 结论不是断言世界模型有用或无用，而是：要回答该问题，必须构建专门用于提问的基准；当前评测缺口使该问题在现有体系下不可判定。

Abstract: Robot learning now advances along two tracks that rarely meet. On one side, direct Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies map observations to actions and are scored by closed-loop task success. On the other, predictive and generative world models forecast future observations and are scored by open-loop prediction or generation quality. A natural question sits between them: does world modelling earn a measurable, closed-loop advantage over a direct policy, and for which robotic capabilities? We argue that the field cannot yet answer this question, and that the reason is a gap in how it is measured, not in the models themselves. World-model benchmarks score prediction without ever executing it, while task-success suites host a single policy and never build a world-model versus VLA contrast. This survey maps the evaluation landscape around that gap. We catalogue 160 web-verified benchmarks spanning 2017 to 2026 and organise them by evaluation mode, robotic capability, and model family into four lanes: policy suites, embodied agents, world model evaluation, and prediction-to-action bridges. Across the corpus, 138 of 160 benchmarks are model-agnostic and only 11 (7%) build an explicit VLA-versus-world-model contrast; counterfactual capability is almost entirely unmeasured, and only four benchmarks turn prediction into executed action. We contribute an operational taxonomy, a coverage comparison against the eight closest surveys (ours is the only one to cross capability with model family), an evaluation loop that isolates the advantage of prediction, and an actionable protocol of four advantage-aware metrics anchored on named testbeds. The organising claim is not that world models help or do not help, but that answering the question requires benchmarks built to ask it.

</details>


### [176] [Combining Evasive and Braking Reactions for Safety Reference Models in Automated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29738)
*Riccardo Donà,Konstantinos Mattas,Biagio Ciuffo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合模糊安全模型 FSM-H，将纵向减速与横向避让统一在人因行为框架中，用于自动驾驶系统仿真安全基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶安全参考模型多只刻画纵向制动，忽略人类驾驶员通过转向避撞的作用，导致可预防性评估偏于保守、缺乏行为拟真性。

Method: 构建有限状态架构，串联名义行驶→制动→紧急转向的升级逻辑；纵向由 Proactive Fuzzy Safety（PFS）度量安全裕度侵蚀，横向由 Criticality Fuzzy Safety for lane-change（CFS-LC）刻画侧向冲突严重度与变道可行性，并引入感知-反应时间与变道检查延迟。

Result: 在重建的高危 cut-in 场景中与纯制动、纯转向参考策略对比，混合模型拓展了事故可预防边界，同时保持行为合理性与计算可行性。

Conclusion: FSM-H 提供了一个透明、可解释的人类驾驶参考模型，适用于基于仿真的 ADS 安全基准测试与监管评估。

Abstract: Computational models of careful and competent human drivers are essential for scenario-based evaluation of automated driving systems (ADS). However, most existing safety reference models primarily focus on longitudinal braking, neglecting the role of evasive steering in human collision avoidance. This paper proposes a hybrid Fuzzy-Safety Model (FSM-H) that integrates longitudinal mitigation and lateral avoidance within a unified behavioral framework. The braking component is governed by Proactive Fuzzy Safety (PFS) metrics, representing the erosion of longitudinal safety margins, while the steering component is driven by Criticality Fuzzy Safety for lane-change (CFS-LC), capturing lateral conflict severity and maneuver feasibility. A finite-state architecture models the sequential escalation from nominal driving to braking and, when necessary, to evasive steering, incorporating perception-reaction time and lane-check delays to reflect human decision processes. The model is evaluated in reconstructed high-criticality cut-in scenarios and compared with braking-only and steering-only reference strategies. Results show that the hybrid approach expands the preventability envelope while maintaining behavioral plausibility and computational tractability. The proposed framework provides a transparent and explainable human reference model suitable for simulation-based ADS safety benchmarking and regulatory assessment.

</details>


### [177] [From Target Selection to Digging: A Learning-Based Framework for Continuous Autonomous Excavation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29750)
*Shuai Zhao,Ji-an Pan,Quantao Yang,Zheng Wang,Chaoyi Chen,Qing Xu,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个学习驱动的连续自主挖掘框架：用LiDAR高程图选择挖掘目标并转为铲斗尖端路标点；任务条件RL策略负责路标引导的接近与载运，IL策略从专家示教学习视觉挖掘与提升，经统一运动接口协调，部署在缩比液压挖掘机上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反复挖掘会不断改变料堆几何，传统方法难以在连续多个挖掘循环中自适应调整挖掘目标并协调动作，因此需要把"地形感知目标选择"与"局部挖掘运动"解耦的自主学习系统。

Method: 框架分层：(1) 目标选择——从LiDAR高程图选取挖掘目标并转换为铲斗尖端路标点；(2) 运动控制——共享的任务条件RL策略执行路标引导的接近与载载运输；(3) 局部挖掘——IL策略从专家演示学习基于视觉的挖掘与提升；(4) 统一运动接口协调两个学习策略与确定性卸载；硬件为配备多模态传感与闭环执行器控制的缩比液压挖掘机。

Result: 离线回放与实机实验显示：相比各自基线，目标选择更一致、局部运动时间更短、有效载荷更高；学习挖掘策略每完成循环平均载荷6.52 kg，固定挖掘(Fixed Dig)基线为2.68 kg；三次五铲连续作业进一步验证了在不断变化的料堆几何下可连续自主挖掘。

Conclusion: 将目标条件运动与局部挖掘解耦、并以统一运动接口集成RL与IL，可有效支撑连续自主土方作业；实机结果表明该方法在效率与载荷上显著优于基线，具备在动态地形中持续作业的能力。

Abstract: Repeated excavation continuously reshapes pile geometry, requiring an autonomous excavator to adapt its digging targets and coordinate motion across successive excavation cycles. We present a learning-based framework for continuous autonomous excavation that integrates terrain-aware target selection with reinforcement- and imitation-learning controllers. The framework separates target-conditioned motion from local digging: a shared task-conditioned RL policy controls waypoint-guided approach and loaded transport, while an IL policy learns vision-based digging and lifting from expert demonstrations. Digging targets are selected from LiDAR elevation maps and converted into bucket-tip waypoints for motion control. The control architecture coordinates the learned policies and deterministic unloading through a shared motion interface. The complete system is deployed on a scaled hydraulic excavator with multimodal sensing and closed-loop actuator control. Offline replay and physical experiments demonstrate more consistent target selection, shorter local motion time, and increased payload compared with the respective baselines. The learned digging policy achieves a mean payload of 6.52 kg per completed cycle, compared with 2.68 kg for Fixed Dig. Three five-scoop runs further demonstrate consecutive autonomous excavation under continuously changing pile geometry.

</details>


### [178] [PolyUMI: Accessible Visual-Tactile-Audio Data Collection for Object Inference and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29760)
*Conor W. Hayes,Rickmer Krohn,Aravind Ramaswami,Anunth Ramaswami,Nils Dengler,Kevin M. Lynch,J. Edward Colgate,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Matthew L. Elwin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PolyUMI 是开源无线手持夹爪数据采集平台，同步记录腕部视觉、光学触觉、接触音频与本体感知，传感指可直接迁移到机器人末端以保持一致几何；配套 VisTA 词元级多模态策略融合跨传感、跨时间信息预测接触感知动作，在物体推断、滑移控制与接触丰富操作上验证触觉与音频提供视觉之外的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类依靠视觉、触觉、听觉和本体感知完成操作，而现有模仿学习系统多仅使用视觉与本体感知，难以获取视觉上难以推断的接触信息，且采集端与执行端传感几何不一致会削弱策略迁移效果。

Method: 提出 PolyUMI 开源平台：轻量无线手持夹爪，无需有线工作站即可同步采集腕部相机、光学触觉、接触音频与本体感知演示；同一传感指可转移至机器人末端执行器以保持演示与执行的传感几何一致。提出 VisTA 词元级多模态策略，在传感器与时间维度上融合异构观测，预测接触感知的机器人动作。

Result: 在物体推断、滑移控制和接触丰富操作实验中，触觉与音频揭示了视觉和本体感知之外的任务相关信息；VisTA 与现有多模态策略相比具有竞争力或更优表现。

Conclusion: PolyUMI 与 VisTA 共同提供了可访问的多模态演示采集与策略学习流程，使机器人策略能够在视觉之外感知物理交互，硬件与软件开源并附项目页面。

Abstract: Humans typically rely on vision, touch, hearing, and proprioception to perceive contact and adapt their actions during manipulation. Providing robots with comparable responsiveness therefore requires hardware that can retain and use these complementary sensory signals. Most imitation-learning systems, however, observe demonstrations primarily through vision and proprioception, limiting access to contact information that is difficult to infer visually. We present PolyUMI, an open-source platform for scalable visual--tactile--audio demonstration collection and robot deployment. Its lightweight, wireless handheld gripper records synchronized wrist-camera, optical tactile, contact-audio, and proprioceptive observations without requiring a tethered workstation. The same sensing finger can be transferred to the robot end effector, preserving the sensing geometry between demonstration collection and policy execution. To effectively use these heterogeneous observations, we further introduce VisTA, a token-level multimodal policy that integrates information across sensors and time to predict contact-aware robot actions. Experiments spanning object inference, slip control, and contact-rich manipulation show that touch and audio reveal task-relevant information beyond vision and that VisTA is competitive with or outperforms existing multimodal policies. Together, PolyUMI and VisTA provide an accessible pipeline for collecting multimodal demonstrations and learning policies that perceive physical interaction beyond vision. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [179] [Self-Supervised Anchoring of Fingertip Sensing to Proprioception and Proactive Actions for Robot Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29822)
*Tomohiro Motoda,Masaki Murooka,Keisuke Shirai,Hanbit Oh,Ryoichi Nakajo,Shotaro Miwa,Roman Mykhailyshyn,Hugo Duarte,Yukiyasu Domae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PROPRA：以本体感知为锚，分别对齐指尖触觉与接近觉传感器历史，解决模仿学习中稀疏、阶段依赖触觉信号难以利用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯视觉模仿学习在指尖附近受遮挡、时间分辨率受限，难以获取物体接近、接触起始与抓取状态等局部交互线索；而简单地把触觉/接近觉信号接入策略并不能稳定提升，甚至不如纯视觉，原因是这类信号稀疏且依赖操作阶段，在少量示教下难以被有效利用。

Method: 使用压敏触觉与反射式接近觉传感器及预训练传感器编码器；提出本体感知与主动锚定预训练方法 PROPRA，将每根手指的传感器历史分别与本体感知片段和动作片段对齐，形成持续可得的传感器运动参考，使各模态在各自有效阶段独立对齐。

Result: 在真实操作任务上，预训练方法相比纯视觉策略与以图像为锚的预训练基线提升了平均成功率；表征分析显示其保留了更丰富的接触前状态信息，从而更有效地利用互补的指尖传感。

Conclusion: 以本体感知/动作作为持续锚点进行分模态独立对齐，是接入阶段依赖、稀疏指尖触觉信号的有效预训练范式。

Abstract: Robotic imitation learning often relies on external cameras, yet local interaction cues such as object proximity, contact onset, and grasp state are difficult to observe near the fingertips because of occlusion and limited temporal resolution. We study how to effectively incorporate complementary fingertip sensing into imitation learning using pressure-sensitive tactile and reflective proximity sensors, along with pretrained sensor encoders. The two modalities provide information at different manipulation phases: proximity sensing is informative before contact, whereas tactile sensing becomes informative after contact. However, naively adding these signals to a policy does not consistently improve performance and can even underperform vision-only policies, suggesting that sparse, phase-dependent sensor signals are difficult to exploit from limited demonstrations. We therefore propose a proprioception-anchored pretraining method, PROprioceptive-and-PRoactive Anchoring (PROPRA), which independently aligns each fingertip sensor history with proprioceptive and action segments. This provides a continuously available sensorimotor reference, allowing each sensor to be aligned independently during its informative phases. Experiments on real-world manipulation tasks show that our pretraining method improves average success rates over vision-only policies and image-anchored pretraining baselines. Representation analysis further shows that it preserves richer information about pre-contact states, enabling more effective use of complementary fingertip sensing. Please refer to our project page: this https URL

</details>


### [180] [BeyondRetarget: Learning Executable Humanoid Motions Directly from Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29850)
*Tianyu Xiong,Yi Lu,Jinrui Wang,Ziqi Liang,Dandan Lei,Xiaoyang Zhou,Xiao-xiao Long,Qiu Shen,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BeyondRetarget：一种端到端框架，直接将单目RGB视频映射为人形机器人运动，摒弃显式人体运动表示，学习面向机器人的隐式视觉表示，并引入接触感知运动优化，提升时序一致性与物理合理性，在仿真与真机上均取得更高执行成功率与更低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"人体视频→显式人体运动表示→运动重定向"流水线可复用大规模人体数据，但人与人形机器人在移动机制和关节自由度配置上差异巨大，导致生成动作难以执行；且人体运动估计误差会传播到重定向阶段，无法通过联合优化消除。因此需要绕过显式人体表示、直接从视觉观测学习面向机器人的运动生成方法。

Method: 提出BeyondRetarget端到端框架：直接由单目RGB视频映射到机器人运动，放弃显式人体运动表示，从视觉观测中学习面向机器人的隐式运动表示以捕获跨形态运动结构；并设计接触感知运动优化机制，提升生成运动的时序一致性与物理合理性。

Result: 实验表明，BeyondRetarget显著提升了生成机器人运动的精度与鲁棒性，在仿真环境和真实人形机器人上均实现更高的执行成功率和更低的延迟。

Conclusion: 以隐式、面向机器人的视觉运动表示为中枢，可规避人体表示与重定向的误差累积与形态失配问题，是人形机器人从人类视频学习可执行运动的一条有效路径。

Abstract: Learning executable motions from human videos offers a scalable solution for humanoid robots to acquire demonstration motions. However, existing pipelines typically first construct an explicit human motion representation and then convert it into robot motions via motion retargeting. Although such methods can effectively leverage large volumes of existing human data for training, the substantial differences between humans and humanoid robots in locomotion mechanisms and joint degree-of-freedom configurations make motions generated by this human-representation-centric approach difficult to execute on robots. Furthermore, errors introduced during human motion estimation inevitably propagate to the retargeting stage and cannot be eliminated via joint optimization. We propose BeyondRetarget, an end-to-end framework that directly maps monocular RGB videos to robot motions. Discarding the explicit human representation, this framework learns robot-oriented implicit representations directly from visual observations, enabling the model to capture cross-morphology motion structures. To generate motions more suitable for robot execution, we further design a contact-aware motion optimization mechanism to improve temporal consistency and physical plausibility. Experiments show that BeyondRetarget significantly improves the accuracy and robustness of generated robot motions, while achieving higher execution success rates and lower latency in both simulation environments and real humanoid robots.

</details>


### [181] [GPT-6-Astra Lights Up Embodied Navigation: Evaluation in Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29861)
*Guangzhao Dai,Qianru Sun,Qi Wu,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 评估通用基础模型 GPT-6-Astra 在连续环境零样本视觉语言导航（VLN-CE）中的表现，仅用单目 RGB，在 Codex harness 最小接口下取得 79.0% 成功率，超出最强零样本与监督基线 13.0 与 6.9 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索通用基础模型能否依靠自身感知、推理与决策能力在陌生环境中导航，从而重新思考具身学习中训练式导航模型的必要性，推动可泛化且可靠的具身智能研究。

Method: 在连续环境 VLN-CE（R2R-CE）上做零样本评测，通过 Codex harness 中的最小接口暴露单目 RGB 观测，由 GPT-6-Astra 自行决定何时观察、如何移动与何时停止；不使用导航专用微调、不训练 waypoint predictor、不预建场景地图，并以 ultra reasoning 模式进行推理。

Result: zero-shot R2R-CE 上 ultra reasoning 成功率达 79.0%，分别超过已报道最强零样本与监督方法 13.0 与 6.9 个百分点；模型能将多阶段语言指令转化为连贯自适应导航，通过空间关系 grounding、任务进度跟踪与动作修正实现；但可靠路径执行与目标验证仍具挑战，局部地标匹配看似合理却未必带来正确任务完成。

Conclusion: 基础模型已具备强零样本具身导航能力，但其路线执行与目标验证的可靠性仍是瓶颈，这促使重新审视具身学习的作用，未来 VLN 研究宜以基础模型为基座，发展可泛化且可靠的具身智能。

Abstract: We investigate whether GPT-6-Astra, a general-purpose foundation model, can navigate unfamiliar environments using its own perception, reasoning, and decision-making capabilities. Our evaluation focues on zero-shot vision-and-language navigation in continuous environments (VLN-CE) through a minimal interface in the Codex harness, aiming to unleash GPT-6-Astra's full potential for navigation. Using monocular RGB, GPT-6-Astra decides when to observe, how to move, and when to stop, without navigation-specific fine-tuning, a trained waypoint predictor, or a pre-built scene map. Our evaluation yields four main findings. First, \textbf{\textit{GPT-6-Astra achieves strong zero-shot navigation performance using only monocular RGB observations}}. On the common-adopted zero-shot R2R-CE benchmark, ultra reasoning achieves a success rate of \textbf{\textit{79.0\%}}, exceeding the strongest reported zero-shot and supervised success rates by \textbf{\textit{13.0}} and \textbf{\textit{6.9}} percentage points, respectively. Second, \textbf{\textit{GPT-6-Astra advances multi-stage language instructions into coherent, adaptive navigation}} by grounding spatial relations, tracking task progress, and revising its actions. Third, \textbf{\textit{reliable route execution and goal verification remain challenging, even with ultra reasoning}}. Plausible local landmark matches do not consistently lead to correct task completion. Fourth, \textbf{\textit{these capabilities motivate rethinking the role of embodied learning}}. Future VLN research should build on foundation models to advance generalizable and reliable embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [182] [MorphIK: Morphology-Conditioned Neural Inverse Kinematics for Unknown Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29908)
*Lennart Clasmeier,Jan Gerrit Habekost,Cornelius Weber,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MorphIK 是一个基于 flow-matching 的通用逆运动学模型，用 transformer 编码机器人构型与目标位姿，可在从未见过的串联机器人上达到约 5 cm 精度，并可作先验经阻尼最小二乘优化进一步降至亚毫米级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的逆运动学方法通常局限于单一机器人，无法跨形态泛化；作者希望仅用程序化生成的合成数据训练，就能对训练中未见过的真实机器人求解 IK，并同时具备高精度与多样解（零空间采样）能力。

Method: MorphIK 采用 transformer 架构编码机器人形态（转动关节运动链结构）与目标位姿，用该编码条件化一个 flow-matching 生成头，从噪声中生成关节位姿；训练数据完全来自程序化生成的合成机器人；生成结果可作为先验交由阻尼最小二乘（DLS）等优化算法精修。

Result: 在 6–9 自由度的未见真实机器人上，单次前向精度约 5 cm；作为优化先验时，一步 DLS 将误差降至 <1 cm，多数情况下三步后达亚毫米级；同时借助 flow matching 的生成多样性可高效采样零空间，对同一位姿给出多种构型。

Conclusion: MorphIK 证明神经逆运动学可以对大量已知与未知机器人实现学习与跨形态泛化，并可与经典优化互补，兼顾精度与解多样性。

Abstract: Neural models can learn to generate various solutions to the inverse kinematics problem from data, but are usually limited to a single robot. We present MorphIK, a flow-matching model that solves inverse kinematics for revolute-joint-based kinematic chains it has never seen during training. The model uses a transformer architecture to encode the robot's morphology along with the target pose. This encoding then conditions a flow-matching head that generates poses from noise. Trained on purely synthetic data from procedurally generated robots, the model reaches a precision of about 5 cm on unseen real-world robots with 6 to 9 Degrees of Freedom. For higher precision, the model serves as an excellent Prior for further optimization algorithms, reducing error to less than 1 cm after a single step of Damped Least Squares optimization and to sub-1 mm error after 3 steps in most cases. Building on flow matching's generative capabilities to produce highly diverse outputs, our model can efficiently sample the robot's null space, providing a wide variety of configurations for the same pose. Thus, overall, MorphIK allows learning and generalizing neural inverse kinematics for a multitude of known and unknown robots.

</details>


### [183] [High-Voltage Optocoupler Amplifier for Electrostatic Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29914)
*George C. Jurgiel,Alex S. Miller,Jeffrey H. Lang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用光耦合器作为线性反馈有源元件，实现 20 kV 峰峰值输出、500 Hz 带宽的高压放大器，简化静电执行器驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静电执行器通常需要数千伏、亚毫安级的驱动电压，传统开关器件难以胜任。作者希望提供一种元件少、失真低、带宽高的高压驱动方案，以提升静电执行器的实用性。

Method: 以光耦合器作为放大器的有源器件并置于反馈环路中作线性元件使用，构建高压放大器电路；与等效 PWM 放大器进行对比。

Result: 放大器可实现 20 kV 峰峰值输出，带宽最高 500 Hz，元件数量极少；相比等效 PWM 放大器，谐波失真更低、带宽更高。

Conclusion: 该设计为静电执行器提供了更简单的高压驱动波形生成方法，提升了其可行性。

Abstract: Many electrostatic actuators require multi-kilovolt drive voltages at sub-milliamp currents, a task poorly suited for conventional switching devices. As an alternative, we demonstrate a high-voltage amplifier using optocouplers as active elements. The amplifier produces a 20-kV peak-to-peak output with up to 500 Hz bandwidth while maintaining a minimal component count. By using optocouplers as linear devices in feedback, lower harmonic distortion and higher bandwidth are achieved than offered by equivalent PWM amplifiers. This design improves the viability of electrostatic actuators by providing a simpler method to achieve useful drive waveforms.

</details>


### [184] [Pairwise Approximation Can Select the Wrong Multi-Robot Plan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29929)
*William Teo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 多机器人协调中常用的成对（2阶）近似会显著改变计划选择，引入可观后悔值；且重构误差更小并不保证选择后悔更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协调方法通常只用单体和成对项对联合计划打分，忽略三元及以上项。作者希望量化这种成对近似在计划选择上的损失（regret），检验"更精确的成对近似是否更可靠"这一直觉。

Method: 在室内探索基准上取冻结的四机器人轨迹。对每条计划，通过重放16个机器人子集获得精确的覆盖效用集函数F。基于同一子集值构造两种成对打分：精确的2阶Möbius截断F2（仅依赖单体和成对值）与等权最小二乘两可加拟合G；另用仅保留单体项的加性得分F1作为对照。

Result: 在15 m候选生成范围内、两类候选族中：按F2排序相比F，在七张地图中有六张改变了所选计划，后悔最高达地图覆盖的0.337。改用G可降低后悔，但每族七图中仍有三张改变选择。仅用单体的F1在一族中六图选中正确计划、另一族四图，而F2仅一图。此外发现平均重构误差越低并不保证选择后悔越低。

Conclusion: 成对近似在与精确集函数比较时可能大幅改变计划选择，近似精度（重构误差）与决策质量（选择后悔）并不同向；多机器人协调需谨慎对待高阶交互项的截断。

Abstract: Multi-robot coordination methods often score a joint plan from singleton and pairwise terms, leaving out the terms that involve three or more robots. We measure the plan-selection regret of two pairwise approximations to delivered coverage using frozen multi-robot trajectories. For each four-robot plan on an indoor exploration benchmark, replaying all 16 robot subsets gives the exact delivered-coverage set function $F$. From the same subset values we compute two pairwise scores: the exact order-2 Möbius truncation $F_2$, which depends only on the singleton and pair values, and an equal-weight least-squares two-additive fit $G$. Ranking by $F_2$ instead of $F$ changes the selected plan on six of seven maps at the 15 m candidate-generation range in each of two candidate families, with regret up to 0.337 of map coverage. Switching to $G$ reduces the regret but still changes the selection on three of seven maps in each family. The additive score $F_1$, which keeps only the singleton terms, selects the exact winner on six of seven maps in one family and four of seven in the other, against one of seven for $F_2$. We also find that lower average reconstruction error does not guarantee lower selection regret.

</details>


### [185] [World Action Agent: Harnessing VLMs for Robot Manipulation via World Action Rehearsal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29964)
*Yehang Zhang,Haojian Huang,Yifan Chang,Jianchong Su,Bohan Zhou,Yingjie Xu,Wosong Chen,Tianhao Zhou,Chenxu Wang,Tianyi Zhang,Yangkai Wei,Wenqian Li,Shiyuan Deng,Yinchuan Li,Ying-Cong Chen,Zexi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WAA 是一个多智能体框架，让 VLM 在"视觉动作工作空间"中直接驾驶机器人：通过接触视图、动作预演与视图内纠偏三步闭环做决策，并从专家视频/人类示教中进化多模态技能、用交互轨迹微调小模型。LIBERO-Pro 上平均成功率 75.6%（SOTA），技能可零样本迁移到 robosuite；Qwen3.5-9B 经轨迹微调后域外成功率从 1.7% 提升到 43.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用 VLM 具备广博知识与空间推理能力，但现有机器人系统对它们的用法存在两方面局限：一是"间接使用"——只让 VLM 预测约束或编写程序，决策主体仍是外部规划器；二是"只看场景"——给 VLM 一个视角图像而非一个可供行动的世界。作者希望让 VLM 在具备行动性的视觉工作空间内直接充当机器人驾驶者，并同时解决 embodied 程序性知识的获取与复用问题。

Method: 提出 World Action Agent（WAA）多智能体 harness，VLM 借助基础工具驾驶机器人，全部决策都在视觉动作工作空间内完成。该工作空间有三条性质：(1) Contact views：依据场景几何自动选取，呈现当前交互附近的局部视图；(2) Action rehearsal：把每个动作变为可编辑提案，智能体自身或经 Imagination Agent 依据规划反馈进行预演与修订后再执行；(3) In-view correction：在观察到偏差的同一视图中闭环修正残余偏移，打通观测—预演—底层执行。知识获取有两条路径：在证据化评审下从专家视频与人类示教进化多模态技能并由 Skill Agent 调用；用交互轨迹训练更小的 VLM 来驾驶同一 harness。

Result: LIBERO-Pro 上，仅用 LIBERO-90 进化出的技能，WAA 达到 75.6% 平均成功率（SOTA），优于端到端 VLA、code-as-policy 智能体以及同骨干的视觉 harness 基线；同一套技能无需额外学习即可在 robosuite 上保持有效。将 Qwen3.5-9B 在 harness 轨迹上微调后，其域外成功率从 1.7% 提升至 43.3%。

Conclusion: 以视觉动作工作空间为核心的多智能体 harness，为 VLM 直接驱动机器人提供了一条可行路径，并同时打通"技能进化—技能调用—轨迹蒸馏"的 embodied 知识闭环；技能具备跨环境迁移性，小模型经轨迹微调可显著提升域外表现。不过实证主要停留在 LIBERO-Pro/robosuite 仿真基准，尚缺真机验证。

Abstract: General-purpose vision-language models (VLMs) bring broad knowledge and spatial reasoning to robot manipulation, yet existing systems either use them indirectly, to predict constraints or write programs, or give them a view of the scene rather than a world in which to act. We present World Action Agent (WAA), a multi-agent harness through which VLMs pilot robots with basic tools, making every decision within a visual action workspace. The workspace has three properties. Contact views, selected automatically from the scene geometry, present the scene around the current interaction. Action rehearsal turns each action into an editable proposal that the agent, alone or through an Imagination Agent, previews and revises against planning feedback before execution. In-view correction closes the loop between observation, rehearsal, and low-level execution, letting the agent remove residual offsets in the view where it observes them. Through the same workspace, WAA acquires embodied procedural knowledge in two ways: it evolves multimodal skills from expert videos and human teaching under evidence-based review and consults them through a Skill Agent, and its interaction traces train smaller VLMs to pilot the same harness. On LIBERO-Pro, WAA with skills evolved only from LIBERO-90 reaches a state-of-the-art 75.6% average success, outperforming end-to-end VLAs, code-as-policy agents, and a visual-harness baseline with the same backbone; the same skills remain effective on robosuite without further learning. Fine-tuning Qwen3.5-9B on harness traces raises its out-of-domain success from 1.7% to 43.3%.

</details>


### [186] [Res-HIL: Human-Guided Residual Reinforcement Learning for Sample-Efficient Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30023)
*Mariia Iavorskaia,Christian Dietz,Sebastian Albrecht,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Res-HIL 提出冻结模仿策略上的残差式人在回路强化学习框架：每次人工干预同时提供残差策略的直接监督和前置自主行为的奖励塑形，配合残差零初始化稳定并加速在线学习，20 条示范下十分钟在线训练即可超越全策略 HIL-RL 与无人工引导的残差微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习可从示范获取操作技能，但策略在训练分布外易失效，而补充示范成本高。现有 HIL-RL 通常直接学习完整任务策略，而非对预训练模仿策略做精细修正，导致样本与人力效率不足。本文希望以最少的在线人工干预，把已有模仿策略"补正"出鲁棒性。

Method: Res-HIL 冻结预训练模仿策略，学习其上的残差动作策略。每次人工干预产生两类互补信号：（1）对残差策略的直接监督（行为克隆式引导）；（2）对干预发生前自主执行轨迹段的干预感知奖励塑形。残差策略采用零初始化，使训练初期策略等价于原模仿策略，从而稳定并加速在线学习。

Result: 在五个接触丰富操作任务（涵盖高精度与长时程行为）上评估，仅用 20 条初始示范，十分钟在线训练后每个任务均优于 SOTA 全策略 HIL-RL 与无人工引导的残差微调；优于基础模仿策略，且超过使用 5 倍示范训练的模仿策略。消融显示：残差直接监督是性能关键，干预感知奖励塑形显著提升训练效率。

Conclusion: 将人工纠正定位为对冻结模仿策略的残差修正，并把"直接监督 + 干预感知奖励塑形 + 零初始化"三者结合，是低人力成本下提升操作策略分布外鲁棒性的有效且高效路径。

Abstract: Imitation learning enables robots to acquire manipulation skills from demonstrations, but the resulting policies can fail outside the training data, while collecting more demonstrations requires substantial human effort. Human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning uses corrective feedback during online training, but typically learns the complete task policy rather than refining a pretrained imitation policy. We introduce Res-HIL, a human-in-the-loop residual reinforcement learning framework that learns corrective actions on top of a frozen imitation policy. Each human intervention provides two complementary learning signals: direct supervision of the residual policy and reward shaping of preceding autonomous behavior. Res-HIL combines these signals with zero initialization of the residual policy to stabilize and accelerate online learning. We evaluate Res-HIL on five contact-rich manipulation tasks spanning high-precision and long-horizon behaviors. With only 20 initial demonstrations, Res-HIL outperforms state-of-the-art full-policy human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning and residual fine-tuning without human guidance on every task after ten minutes of online training. Res-HIL improves its pretrained base policies and outperforms imitation policies trained with five times more demonstrations. An ablation study shows that direct residual supervision is critical to performance, while intervention-aware reward shaping substantially improves training efficiency.

</details>


### [187] [Body-Grounded Replanning for Physically Adaptive Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30024)
*Namiko Saito,Hiroshi Kera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"身体接地的高层重规划"框架，用机器人内部物理状态（关节负载、机动性）触发策略切换，由LLM解读关节级状态与执行统计来选择上下文相关的替代策略，任务目标与底层控制器保持不变；在仿真与真机的到达任务中降低物理代价并保持高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高层任务与运动规划只考虑外部环境的几何可行性，策略在几何上仍然可行，却可能因关节负载增大或机动性受限而物理上不再适用；而内部物理状态通常仅用于底层控制，未参与高层策略决策。

Method: 提出body-grounded high-level replanning：以身体状态事件触发策略重规划，LLM作为高层决策器，输入为关节级状态、近期执行统计与执行历史，输出上下文相关的替代策略；任务目标与底层控制器不变。在仿真的受控负载与不对称机动性约束的到达任务上评估，并在真机上验证；另加接触丰富的操作实验验证接口的通用性。

Result: 在受控负载与不对称机动性约束的到达任务中，身体接地重规划在保持高任务成功率的同时降低了物理努力（关节负载/代价），并实现更高效的策略自适应；接触丰富的操作实验表明同一重规划接口可扩展到到达之外的任务。

Conclusion: 内部物理状态不仅能指导底层控制，也能为高层决策提供依据——即操作任务"应该如何执行"的策略选择可由身体状态驱动，从而提升执行的物理合理性与效率。

Abstract: Manipulation requires not only reasoning about the external environment, but also about the robot's physical condition. A strategy may remain geometrically feasible while becoming physically unsuitable due to increased joint load or limited mobility, yet internal physical state is typically used only for low-level control. We propose body-grounded high-level replanning, which uses internal physical state to adapt manipulation strategies during execution. Body-state events trigger strategy replanning, and an LLM interprets the underlying joint-level state, recent execution statistics, and execution history to select a context-dependent alternative, while leaving the task objective and low-level controller unchanged. We evaluate the framework on a reaching task under controlled load and asymmetric mobility constraints in simulation and on a real robot. Our experiments show that body-grounded replanning maintains high task success while reducing physical effort and enabling more efficient strategy adaptation. Additional contact-rich manipulation experiments demonstrate the applicability of the same replanning interface beyond reaching. These results show that internal physical state can inform not only low-level control, but also high-level decisions about how a manipulation task should be performed.

</details>


### [188] [M3GD: Multi-Modal Multi-View Geometric Diffusion for Camera--LiDAR Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30056)
*Yang Zhou,Jiuhong Xiao,Shizhao Ye,Long Quang,Carlos Nieto-Granda,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: M3GD将冻结的2D图像与3D点云基础模型的特征在相机投影后对齐，利用共享空间结构构造视角对齐的LiDAR条件包，通过轻量残差适配器注入多视角流匹配生成器，实现机器人场景下的相机-LiDAR多模态新视角合成，并在真实地面机器人上部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人新视角合成需同时恢复视觉外观与度量3D结构，但多数生成式NVS方法仅依赖图像，忽视了机器人平台上常见的互补传感器LiDAR。

Method: 组合独立预训练的2D图像与3D点云基础模型，无需单独预训练跨模态翻译器；论证相机投影后冻结的LiDAR特征与图像特征具有显著共享空间结构，作为天然的跨模态表示。将显式几何统计与学习到的点云描述子组合为图像隐网格上的视角对齐包，经轻量残差适配器注入多视角流匹配生成器，保持其隐空间、解码器与训练目标不变。

Result: 在GrandTour数据集上，M3GD相对于同骨干的纯图像版本提升了目标视角RGB与深度合成质量；消融表明增益来自像素对齐的LiDAR内容，目标视角LiDAR作为几何查询将请求视角与源观测关联；地面机器人部署验证了真实场景可用性，并可通过欧拉积分步数调节质量-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 相机-LiDAR多模态表示可在不重新预训练跨模态翻译器的前提下有效增强生成式新视角合成，兼具几何与外观一致性，并可用于实际机器人部署。

Abstract: Robotic novel view synthesis (NVS) must recover both visual appearance and metric 3D structure, yet most generative NVS methods rely only on images, overlooking LiDAR, a complementary sensor common on robotic platforms. We present M3GD, a Camera--LiDAR multimodal representation for generative NVS that composes independently pretrained 2D image and 3D point-cloud foundation models without separately pretraining a cross-modal translator. We show that, after camera projection, frozen LiDAR and image features exhibit substantial shared spatial structure, providing a natural cross-modal representation. M3GD conditions generation on LiDAR through this structure: it combines explicit geometry statistics with learned point-cloud descriptors into view-aligned packets on the image-latent grid, injected through a lightweight residual adapter into a multi-view flow-matching generator whose latent space, decoders, and training objective remain intact. On the GrandTour dataset, M3GD improves target-view RGB and depth synthesis over an image-only version of the same backbone. Ablations show that the gains come from pixel-aligned LiDAR content and that target-view LiDAR acts as a geometric query linking the requested view to source observations. Deployment on a ground robot demonstrates practical real-world operation, with a configurable quality--cost trade-off controlled by the number of Euler integration steps.

</details>


### [189] [Real-Time Force Regulation for Whole-Hand Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30082)
*Sang Min Kim,Alexander Alexiev,Tzu-Yuan Lin,Sangbae Kim,Young Min Kim,Yonghyeon Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于本体感知与物体跟踪几何估计全手接触，实时重算满足摩擦与驱动一致性约束的接触力分布，实现抗扰抓取维持与重抓，真机27自由度臂手验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧抓取的鲁棒性依赖全手接触力矩的实时调节，但预计算力分配在物体运动、建模误差或外力扰动下易失效，且全手接触难以靠触觉全覆盖感知。

Method: 利用被跟踪物体模型与本体感知几何估计全手各连杆接触，无需触觉传感；反复在线重算满足摩擦锥、执行器限幅与全肢力分析导出的"驱动一致性"约束的期望接触力分布，并与反应式伸手控制集成，实现抓取、抗扰与掉落后重抓。

Result: 无重力仿真中，受控扰动下相比固定分配与仅指尖执行策略抓取保持率更高；27自由度臂手系统真实实验展示了人手施加扰动下接触随全手演化时的抓取维持与恢复。

Conclusion: 提出一种无需触觉的全手接触力实时调节框架，配合反应式控制可实现抓取获取、抗扰维持与重抓，仿真与真机均验证有效性。

Abstract: Robust dexterous grasping requires maintaining physical stability despite contacts interactively evolving across the entire hand. A precomputed force distribution can easily fail under object motion, modeling errors, or external disturbances. In this paper, we present a framework for real-time force regulation over dynamically changing whole-hand contacts. Our method geometrically estimates contacts across all hand links using a tracked object model and proprioception, without requiring tactile sensing at those contacts. It repeatedly recomputes the desired contact-force distribution subject to friction constraints, actuator limits, and an actuation-consistency constraint motivated by classical whole-limb force analysis. We integrate this force-regulation controller with reactive reaching, enabling the hand to acquire a grasp, maintain it under disturbances, and regrasp after losing the object. Simulation experiments without gravity demonstrate improved grasp retention over fixed-allocation and fingertip-only execution under controlled perturbations, while real-world experiments on a 27-DoF arm-hand system demonstrate grasp maintenance and recovery under human-applied disturbances as contacts evolve across the whole hand. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [190] [Self-Adaptive VLA for Robust Robot Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30092)
*Hongxin Zhang,Chunru Lin,Tsun-Hsuan Wang,Zhenjia Xu,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Self-Adaptive VLA 后训练方法：利用策略自身 rollout 构建移变（shift）条件下的专家示范，并用轻量上下文编码器将视觉、本体感知与动作压缩为上下文 token，经 AdaLN 调制策略，使 VLA 能在部署时迭代自校正以适应硬件偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型无记忆，面对测试时环境变化（尤其硬件磨损/标定误差导致的移变）表现脆弱；若需持续现场重标定则难以规模化部署，因此需要部署期自适应的能力。

Method: 1) 在刻意注入硬件移变的条件下采集策略 rollout；2) 对专家动作预补偿已知移变，将原始训练数据转化为移变条件专家示范；3) 引入插件式轻量上下文编码器，将移变环境中的视觉观测、本体感知与动作压缩为隐上下文 token；4) 该 token 通过 AdaLN 调制策略；5) 上下文 token 可集成（ensemble），使策略逐步自校正、缓解失败。

Result: 在四个精度敏感的双臂与灵巧操作任务上，Self-Adaptive VLA 在硬件移变（如驱动偏置、关节编码器偏移）下恢复基策略 80% 以上性能，并比基策略在新工作站上部署更稳健。

Conclusion: 该工作为大规模真实机器人部署与更易维护提供了一条提升鲁棒性的路径，但方法依赖已知移变注入与预补偿，对未知/复合移变的泛化与真机长期部署验证仍待进一步检验。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models demonstrate impressive capabilities in robotic manipulation, their memoryless nature renders them brittle to test-time environment shifts, particularly hardware shifts caused by wear or imperfect calibration. Enabling these models to self-adapt during deployment without requiring continuous on-site recalibration remains a critical bottleneck for real-world scalability. In this work, we introduce Self-Adaptive VLA, a novel post-training recipe that enables the policy to iteratively adapt to deployment-time hardware shifts leveraging its own rollouts as context. To do so, we first collect policy rollouts under deliberately injected hardware shifts. We then transform the base policy's training data into shift-conditioned expert demonstrations by pre-compensating the expert actions for these known shifts. Next, we introduce a lightweight, plug-in context encoder that compresses the context, including visual observation, proprioception, and actions in the shifted environment, into a latent context token. This token modulates the policy through adaptive layer normalization (AdaLN). Furthermore, we find that context tokens can be ensembled, allowing the policy to iteratively self-correct and mitigate failures step by step. Extensive experiments across four precision-critical bi-manual and dexterous manipulation tasks show that Self-Adaptive VLA recovers over 80% of the base policy's performance under hardware shifts, such as actuation bias and joint encoder offsets. Moreover, Self-Adaptive VLA enables more robust deployment to new workstations compared to the base policy. Our approach provides a pathway for robust large-scale real-world robot deployments and easier maintenance. See videos at this https URL .

</details>


### [191] [Faster Visuomotor Policy Learning on Action Manifolds via Riemannian MeanFlow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30127)
*S. Talha Bukhari,Austin Garrett,Yi Wei,Ruiqi Ni,Zachary Kingston,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RMFP（黎曼MeanFlow策略）在机器人动作流形上学习条件流映射，以黎曼条件流匹配为锚点构建流映射一致性目标，实现仅需1次网络函数评估的on-manifold动作序列生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion/Flow Matching策略虽能刻画动作序列的多峰分布，但生成动作需多步数值积分，采样成本高、难以满足机器人高频控制需求；同时机器人动作序列位于光滑可微流形上，策略需尊重动作空间的内在几何结构。

Method: 提出Riemannian MeanFlow Policy：学习概率路径在机器人动作流形上的条件流映射（flow map），将flow map consistency目标以Riemannian Conditional Flow Matching作为数据锚定，一致性条件训练稳定，约束模型为有限时间传输（finite-time transport），从而支持少至1次函数评估的on-manifold动作生成。

Result: 在球面LASA与Push-T基准、Robomimic的Tool Hang与Transport任务、以及流形约束动作生成的Franka Kitchen任务上，RMFP以更低采样成本取得与先前工作相当的性能；并在真实机器人操作任务中验证了不完美传感器测量下的快速动作生成。

Conclusion: RMFP通过黎曼流形上的流映射一致性学习，兼顾多峰表达力与几何约束，将动作生成压缩至单步评估，在仿真基准与真实机器人任务上均展示出低采样成本下的竞争性表现，为高频机器人控制提供了高效的生成式策略框架。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies learn a direct map from raw sensory observations to robot action sequences. Policies based on Diffusion and Flow Matching capture the multimodal distribution over action sequences in an end-to-end manner. This expressivity comes at the cost of multi-step numerical integration of the learned vector field for action generation, which can be expensive and time-consuming, impeding fast control rates required in robotics applications. Furthermore, robot action sequences are usually defined on a smooth, differentiable manifold, requiring that the learned policy respects the intrinsic geometry of the robot's action space. Here, we present Riemannian MeanFlow Policy (RMFP), which learns the conditioned flow map of the probability path on the robot action manifold. Our formulation employs a flow map consistency objective grounded in the data by a Riemannian Conditional Flow Matching anchor. The flow map consistency condition is stable to train and constrains the learned model to finite-time transport, which yields on-manifold action sequence generation with as few as one network function evaluation. We present results on the spherical LASA and Push-T benchmarks, on the Tool Hang and Transport tasks of the Robomimic suite, and on the Franka Kitchen task with manifold-constrained action generation, and demonstrate that RMFP attains performance competitive with prior work at a lower sampling cost. We also employ RMFP on a real-world robotic manipulation task to demonstrate fast action generation under imperfect sensor measurements in the physical world.

</details>


### [192] [Training-free Behavior Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30134)
*Maximilian Adang,Timothy Chen,Lars Osterberg,Aiden Swann,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BPC：用行为系统理论（Hankel 动作延续先验 + 动作感知检索度量 + 闭式单步残差修正）在不做端到端策略训练的前提下合成策略，仅需存储的演示数据即可预测动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经行为克隆将演示压缩进大模型，导致单步动作不可追溯、策略更新代价高；检索式策略虽保留演示但难以处理记录行为与实际运行行为之间的分布错配。作者希望在保留演示可追溯性的同时获得接近学习策略的性能与极低的拟合成本。

Method: 将控制视为行为预测：给定近期运行时观测-动作历史，用动作感知的检索度量在演示库中寻找最能重构该历史的数据窗口，借助 Hankel 矩阵形式的行为系统理论构造动作延续先验，并对预测做闭式一步残差修正，从而无需端到端训练即可闭环输出动作。

Result: 在仿真基准与真实机器人部署中与 π0.5 等学习策略竞争力相当（部分场景超越）；策略拟合从数小时降至消费级 GPU 上数秒，并在 Jetson Orin Nano 上实现 75 Hz 以上闭环控制；检索窗口及其系数可给出任务进度的内在估计。

Conclusion: 保留演示于部署策略内部使预测可追溯到支撑轨迹，并可通过演示库进行行为修订，为可解释、低成本的机器人策略提供了一条非端到端训练路线。

Abstract: Neural behavior cloning compresses demonstrations into large models, making individual actions difficult to trace and policy updates costly. Retrieval policies retain access to demonstrations but struggle with mismatch between recorded and live behavior. We introduce Behavior Predictive Control (BPC), which synthesizes policies without end-to-end policy training by combining an action-aware retrieval metric, a Hankel-based action-continuation prior, and a closed-form one-step residual correction. Inspired by behavioral systems theory, BPC predicts future actions by blending stored observation-action data that best reconstructs the recent runtime observation--action history. Across simulated benchmarks and real-robot deployments, BPC is competitive with learned policies such as $\pi_{0.5}$ (surpassing it in some cases), while reducing policy fitting from hours to seconds on consumer GPUs and supporting closed-loop control upwards of 75 Hz on a Jetson Orin Nano. The retrieved demonstration windows and their coefficients also provide an intrinsic estimate of task progress. Retaining demonstrations within the deployed policy makes its predictions traceable to supporting trajectories and enables behavior revision through the demonstration bank.

</details>


### [193] [Contact as a Decision Variable: Capability-Tradeoff Contact Selection for Legged Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30140)
*Al Jaber Mahmud,Shuai Li,Xuan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CTCS方法用于loco-manipulation任务中环境支撑接触面与全身构型的联合选择，通过局部灵敏度分析预测候选面能力并选择性精确验证，实现约3倍加速且目标值接近全枚举。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: loco-manipulation任务中，环境接触可提供物理支撑但会限制任务所需运动，二者存在能力权衡；而评估每个候选接触面上的残余力矩、末端可达性与基座机动性需反复求解全身优化，计算代价高。

Method: 将问题形式化为残余wrench、末端执行器可达范围、基座机动性三项能力度量与接触获取成本的权衡；提出Capability-Tradeoff Contact Selection（CTCS）：先筛选接触与任务可行性，再在每个面内对相似候选分组，用精确锚点评估加局部灵敏度分析预测能力，通过选择性精确评估校验预测，按能力排序并对候选短名单精确求解。

Result: 在Unitree Go2四足+AgileX NERO机械臂上，覆盖392种任务条件、9个可用支撑面进行仿真与硬件实验。CTCS优于仅地面支撑与固定接触支撑方案，能选出能力权衡更优的支撑面；相比逐候选精确评估约3倍加速，平均目标值基本一致。

Conclusion: CTCS以低成本能力预测+选择性精确验证的框架，高效解决了支撑接触选择的能力权衡问题，并在真实四足-机械臂平台上验证有效，为loco-manipulation中的接触-构型联合优化提供可扩展方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the joint selection of an environmental support contact and a whole-body configuration for a prescribed loco-manipulation task. A contact may provide greater physical support while restricting the motion required for the task. We formulate this problem through three capability measures: residual wrench, end-effector reach, and base mobility available after satisfying the task requirements, and we balance them against contact acquisition cost. Evaluating these capabilities for every candidate requires repeated whole-body optimizations. To reduce this computational cost, we propose Capability-Tradeoff Contact Selection (CTCS). CTCS screens candidates for contact and task feasibility, groups similar candidates within each surface, and predicts their capabilities from exact anchor evaluations using local sensitivity analysis. It checks these predictions through selective exact evaluations, ranks candidates by capability, and evaluates a shortlist exactly for final selection. We evaluate CTCS in simulations and hardware experiments using a Unitree Go2 quadruped with an AgileX NERO arm across $392$ task conditions with nine available support surfaces. Results show that CTCS outperforms ground-only and fixed-contact support, as it can select support surfaces that provide favorable capability trade-offs for the task. Compared with evaluating every candidate exactly, CTCS achieves approximately $3\times$ speedup while closely matching the resulting mean objective value.

</details>


### [194] [ReVAMP: Vector-Accelerated Motion Planning for Kinematically-Constrained Systems via Reparameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30213)
*Shrutheesh R. Iyer,Thomas Cohn,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于解析IK重参数化规划空间的向量化运动规划方法，在高维系统与复杂约束下实现微秒至毫秒级规划，比现有最优方法快达10倍，并可用于重构顺序操作流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束型运动规划中，约束使有效构型空间降为测度零子集，采样类算法难以直接采样；基于IK的参数化方法虽可构造性满足约束，但因向量化程度不足而速度落后于投影类方法，其性能上限未知。作者希望提升此类方法的并行化与效率。

Method: 通过对规划空间进行解析IK重参数化，构建新的向量化轴；针对现有向量化投影类规划器的低效环节进行改进，并在规划器内部挖掘新的并行机会。

Result: 在高达20维的高维系统及复杂约束下，规划器可在微秒至毫秒量级生成规划，速度较当前最优方法提升最多10倍。

Conclusion: 解析IK重参数化的向量化规划显著突破参数化规划器的性能天花板，其高速规划能力可支撑顺序操作流水线的重构。

Abstract: Robots often must satisfy one or more constraints during motion planning for real-world tasks. When such constraints reduce the valid configuration space to a measure-zero subset, sampling based planning algorithms require modifications to draw feasible samples. For many common end-effector constraints, parameterizations built on inverse kinematics (IK) provide an alternate formulation where the constraints are satisfied by construction, allowing directly sampling the feasible set. Despite their elegant approach, parameterized planners have remained slower than vector-accelerated implementations of projection-based approaches, leaving their performance ceiling an open question. We explore a new axis of vectorization built upon reparameterizing the planning space through analytic IK. This approach addresses existing inefficiencies in vectorized projection-based planners and exposes new opportunities for parallelism within the planner. We show that the planner can synthesize plans in microseconds to milliseconds for high dimensional systems (up to 20 dimensions), with complex constraints, up to 10x faster than the current state-of-the-art. Furthermore, we demonstrate how such planning speeds open up avenues for restructuring sequential manipulation pipelines.

</details>


### [195] [Underwater C3-JEPA: An Object-Centric Cross-View World Model for ROV Salvage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30214)
*Yuncong Yang,Jinlong Li,Yulong Xue,Feng Wu,Chunwen Zhang,Lei Qiao,Xuyang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出水下ROV打捞的对象中心多视角预测世界模型C³-JEPA，在潜空间预测物体在接触交互与水动力滞后下的状态演化，支持MPC评估与想象式rollout训练，并在真实水下视频上验证跨视角状态恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近场重载水下ROV打捞缺乏接触传感器，视觉观测受浑浊水体与水动力滞后干扰，物体状态在接触交互中难以建模；现有方法多以重建为目标、缺乏控制条件化的预测接口，且难以在真实水下环境中泛化。

Method: C³-JEPA（cross-view, control-conditioned, context-extended）：将多相机RGB观测编码为任务物体token与上下文token，通过held-out-view注意力融合跨相机证据，直接以车辆控制信号为条件预测未来潜在状态；采用弱绑定（weak binding）以低标注成本锚定目标物与夹爪，并用SIGReg锐化几何表示；预测器保持轻量。

Result: 所学表示向下游探针传递的任务相关信息显著多于无重建的潜在基线；预测接口可用于MPC候选评估与行为智能体的想象rollout训练；真实水下视频验证中，同一架构能恢复被遮蔽相机的物体状态并优于persistence基线，表明方法可超越仿真迁移到真实场景。

Conclusion: 面向水下重载打捞的对象中心多视角预测世界模型，以控制条件化的潜空间预测替代重建，兼具轻量与跨域迁移能力，可作为MPC与策略学习的预测接口；未来可扩展至更复杂的接触动力学与实机闭环部署。

Abstract: We present Underwater C$^{3}$-JEPA (cross-view, control-conditioned, context-extended), an object-centric multi-view predictive world model for near-field heavy-load underwater ROV salvage. Without contact sensors, it predicts in latent space how the task-object state evolves through contact interaction and under the hydrodynamic lag of the vehicle, from synchronized multi-view RGB observations and vehicle control signals. C$^{3}$-JEPA encodes multi-camera observations into task-object and context tokens, fuses cross-camera evidence through held-out-view attention, and directly predicts future states conditioned on control. Weak binding anchors the target and gripper at low annotation cost, while SIGReg sharpens the geometric representation. Experiments show that the learned representation transfers substantially more task-relevant information to downstream probes than a reconstruction-free latent baseline, while keeping the predictor lightweight. The resulting predictive interface supports model-predictive-control (MPC) candidate evaluation and imagined-rollout behavior-agent training. Validation on real underwater video shows the same architecture recovering a withheld camera's object state and staying ahead of persistence, so the recipe transfers beyond simulation.

</details>


### [196] [Coding Agents for Generalized Task and Motion Planning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30233)
*Matteo Merler,Bowen Li,Josh Roy,Yichao Liang,Qianwei Wang,Yixuan Huang,Tom Silver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究用编程智能体（coding agents）自动合成跨实例泛化的任务与运动规划（TAMP）程序。给定任务描述与仿真器访问权限，智能体在固定预算内自行选择交互方式并编写程序，程序冻结后在未见实例上评估。在KinDER与PDDLStream的28个仿真环境中，对Claude Code、Codex等3种配置共980个生成程序、每个在100个留出实例上评测（共98,000次episode）。结果显示编程智能体平均成功率56%–95%，显著优于手工规划器（47%），且在物体数量增加时保持更高成功率，平均每实例计算量低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义TAMP虽能利用跨问题实例的规律降低新实例的规划代价，但现有方法需要大量TAMP专用工程；作者想验证编程智能体能否通过合成可泛化程序来自动化这一过程。

Method: 以任务描述和仿真器访问为输入，让每个智能体在固定合成预算内自主决定如何与环境交互并开发程序；程序随后冻结，在未见实例上评估。在KinDER和PDDLStream的28个仿真环境（物体数量超出原基准）中对Claude Code（Opus 5）与Codex（GPT-5.6 Sol、GPT-6 Astra）进行评测，覆盖980个生成程序、每个100个留出实例，共98,000次评估episode，并与手工规划器、一次性生成及基于LLM的广义规划基线对比。

Result: 三种智能体配置在平均成功率上均优于手工规划器、一次性生成和LLM广义规划基线（在有规划器的16个环境上为56%–95% vs 47%）。随物体数量增加，智能体程序成功率高于规划器，且平均每实例计算量约低一个数量级。日志显示智能体利用交互校准物理模型、测试边界情况并改进策略。

Conclusion: 编程智能体在广义TAMP上表现意外地有效，可作为该领域强有力的基线；作者开源全部代码及完整提示词，并指出交互式程序合成是自动化规划工程的有前景方向。

Abstract: Task and motion planning (TAMP) problems remain difficult even with full observability and object-centric states because discrete decisions are tightly coupled to geometric, kinematic, and dynamic constraints. Generalized TAMP addresses this difficulty by exploiting regularities across problem instances to reduce planning effort on new instances. However, existing methods require substantial TAMP-specific engineering. We investigate whether coding agents can automate this process by synthesizing programs that generalize across instances. Given a task description and simulator access, each agent chooses how to interact with the environment while developing a program within a fixed synthesis budget. The program is then frozen and evaluated on unseen instances. We evaluate Claude Code (Opus 5) and Codex (GPT-5.6 Sol and GPT-6 Astra) on 28 simulated environments from KinDER and PDDLStream, with object counts beyond those evaluated in the original benchmark. Across all program synthesis methods, we evaluate 980 generated programs on 100 held-out instances each, 98,000 evaluation episodes in total. Overall, we find that coding agents are surprisingly effective at generalized TAMP: all three agent configurations outperform hand-engineered planners, one-shot generation, and an LLM-based generalized planning baseline in mean success (56% to 95% versus 47% for the planners, on the 16 environments where a planner is available). As object counts grow, the agents' programs maintain higher success than the planner, using an order of magnitude less computation per instance on average. Logs show agents using interaction to calibrate physical models, test edge cases, and refine strategies. We release all code, including the full prompts given to the agents. These findings suggest that coding agents are a strong baseline for generalized TAMP.

</details>


### [197] [Rolling-WAM: World Action Models with Rolling Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30247)
*Yinghua Zhou,Junjie Ye,Yiqi Zhao,Hao Dong,Celina Shiyu Wang,Ruohai Ge,Tingyi Yang,Basile Van Hoorick,Gaurav Sukhatme,Vitor Guizilini,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Rolling-WAM，将视频-动作联合去噪分散到连续的重规划周期中：通过错开噪声水平的滑动窗口，每步仅完整去噪即将执行的动作块，同时部分精化远期块，实现 4.5 倍稳态重规划加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models 将动作生成与未来视觉预测耦合，但每个重规划周期都需完成整个视频-动作联合去噪，造成显著延迟，拖慢动作更新并限制闭环响应能力。

Method: 维护一个处于交错噪声水平的视频-动作块滑动窗口，采用滚动噪声调度：每步对即将执行的 imminent 动作块完全去噪，对更远期的块仅部分精化；窗口随新相机观测推进，保留的未来块继续其去噪过程，从而把算力分散到时间轴上并跨块边界传递演化的视觉-动作上下文。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 及真实 Unitree G1 人形机器人上评估，操作性能具有竞争力；无需从头去噪整个预测时域，相比标准联合 WAM 获得 4.5 倍稳态重规划加速。

Conclusion: 通过滚动式跨周期去噪调度，WAM 可在保持操作性能的同时大幅降低闭环重规划延迟，为实时具身操作提供了可行路径。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) couple action generation with future visual prediction for robotic manipulation. However, completing the joint video-action denoising process at each replanning cycle incurs substantial latency, delaying action updates and limiting closed-loop responsiveness. We present Rolling-WAM, a formulation that distributes joint denoising across successive replanning cycles. Our method maintains a sliding window of video-action chunks at staggered noise levels. At each step, a rolling noise schedule fully denoises the imminent action chunk for execution, while partially refining farther-future chunks. As the window advances with new camera observations, the retained future chunks continue their denoising process. This distributes the computational cost over time while carrying an evolving visual-action context across chunk boundaries. Evaluations on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and a real-world Unitree G1 humanoid show that Rolling-WAM achieves competitive manipulation performance. By removing the need to denoise the entire prediction horizon from scratch, it delivers a 4.5x steady-state replanning speedup over standard joint WAMs.

</details>


### [198] [RAPID: Robot Agentic Programming from Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30249)
*Yuyao Liu,Jiayuan Mao,David Hsu,Leslie Pack Kaelbling,Tomás Lozano-Pérez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAPID 提出用编码智能体从单次人类演示中自动生成、验证并迭代优化机器人程序，实现可跨物体位姿/形状/材质/环境的操作泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在复杂编程任务上表现优异，但将其潜力迁移到机器人系统面临挑战：缺乏可测试的任务规范、可执行的原子动作，以及用于程序执行与验证的交互环境。现有机器人编程/模仿学习方法多依赖运动轨迹的逐点复现，难以泛化到演示之外的新场景。作者希望以"程序"而非"轨迹"为表示，借助智能体迭代式代码精炼循环，自动从单次视觉演示中推断上述三大要素。

Method: RAPID 的核心是一个迭代式智能体代码精炼循环，依赖三项自动从演示中推断的要素：(i) 可测试的任务规范，(ii) 机器人执行的原子动作原语，(iii) 用于程序执行与验证的交互环境。为使程序可超出演示场景复用，采用以物体为中心的关系式程序表示：把动作原语表达为轨迹优化程序以实现物体级运动效果，并通过运行时捕获场景特定几何的关系约束进行组合，从而关注演示策略的底层结构而非具体运动轨迹。评估在仿真中进行，涵盖八个高难度接触丰富的非抓取操作任务及 LIBERO-Pro 基准中的常规抓取操作任务；并在真实 Franka 机械臂上对全部八个非抓取任务完成部署与验证。结果显示 RAPID 在所有实验中表现强劲，并具备对物体位姿、形状、材质与环境的泛化能力。

Result: 仿真中八个接触丰富的非抓取操作任务和 LIBERO-Pro 常规抓取任务上均表现强劲；在真实 Franka 机械臂上成功部署并完成全部八个非抓取任务评估；对物体位姿、形状、材质和环境具有泛化能力。

Conclusion: RAPID 将编码智能体的迭代式程序生成-验证-精炼范式引入机器人操作，通过物体中心关系式程序表示实现单次演示到可复用程序的转化，仿真与真机实验共同验证了其有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: Coding agents have demonstrated enormous success in solving complex programming problems. To leverage their potential for robot systems, this work introduces Robot Agentic Programming from Demonstrations (RAPID), which automatically generates, verifies, and refines robot programs, given a single visual human demonstration. The iterative agentic loop of code refinement requires several key ingredients: (i) a testable task specification, (ii) action primitives for robot execution, and (iii) an interactive environment for program execution and verification. RAPID infers all three from the demonstration automatically. To make the resulting program reusable beyond the demonstration setting, RAPID uses an object-centric relational program representation that focuses on the underlying structure of the demonstrated strategy rather than the specific motion per se: it expresses the action primitives as trajectory-optimization programs that realize object-level motion effects, while composing them through relational constraints that capture scene-specific geometry at run time. We evaluated RAPID in simulation on eight challenging contact-rich nonprehensile manipulation tasks as well as general prehensile manipulation tasks in the LIBERO-Pro benchmark. We also successfully deployed it on a real Franka arm and evaluated on all eight nonprehensile tasks. In all experiments, RAPID demonstrated strong performance, with generalization over object pose, shape, material, and environment. Website: this https URL .

</details>
